1b1(rgyud): @##/7/rgya gar skad du/he badzra tan tra ra dza na ma/bod skad du/kye'e rdo rje zhes bya ba rgyud kyi rgyal po ;;
1b2(rgyud): kye'e rdo rje la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin 
1b3(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang/thugs kyi snying po rdo rje'i btsun mo'i ba rnams la bzhugs so//
1b4(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi skudang gsung dang thugs kyi snying po//rje btsun gsang ba las shin tu 'ang ches
1b5(rgyud): shin tu gsang ba 'ang/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsal pa/e ma'o byang chub sems dpa' sems
2a1(rgyud): @##//dpa' chen po//rdo rje snying po snying rje chen po legs so//rdo rje sems dpa' dang/sems dpa' chen po
2a2(rgyud): dang dam tshig sems dpa' chen po snying po ro//kye rdo rje zhes bya ba nyon cig//rdo rje snying pos gsol pa//gang phyir rdo rje
2a3(rgyud): sems dpa' lags//ji ltar sems dpa' chen po lags//gang gis dam tshig sems dpa' lags//bcom
2a4(rgyud): ldan 'das kyis bdag la gsungs//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//rdo rje mi phyir zhes byar brjod//sems
2a5(rgyud): dpa' srid pa gsum gcig pa//'dis ni shes rab rigs pa yis//rdo rje sems dpa' zhes byar brjod//ye shes
2b1(rgyud): chen po'i ro gang ba//sems dpa' chen por brjod par bya//rtag tu dam tshig la spyod phyir//dam tshig sems dpa' brjod par bya//rdo rje
2b2(rgyud): snying pos gsol pa//ming bsdus pas ni ji lta bur//gang gis kye'i rdo rje lags//he'i rnam par ci zhig bshad//badzra yis kyang de bzhin ci
2b3(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/he ni snying rje chen po nyid//badzra 'ang shes rab brjod par bya//thabs dang shes rab bdag nyid rgyud//de ni nga
2b4(rgyud): yis bshad kyis nyon//lta stangs dgug dang bda' chen dang//don byed rnam mang brjod pa dang//rengs par byed dang bskrod par byed//snga rengs byed dang
2b5(rgyud): mngon spyod dang//ji ltar rigs pa'i rnal 'byor ma'i//bskyed dang gnas dang byed rgyu dang//don byed ye shes rnam byed dang//lha rnams ji ltar 'byung
2b6(rgyud): ba'o//he ru ka ni bskyed pa'i rgyu//dang po gcig ni sgom par bya//rdo rje snying po snying rje che//dngos po nyid kyis rnam grol zhing//dngos pos 'ching
3a1(rgyud): @#//bas 'ching bar 'gyur//de yong shes pas grol ba yin//dngos po med pa 'ang yong shes par//dngos po grol 'gyur shes rab can//dngos po med par
3a2(rgyud): yong shes pas//de ltar he ru ka bsgom pa//lus la ye shes chen po gnas//rtog pa thams cad yang dag spangs//dngos po kun la khyab pa po//lus can gnas lus la
3a3(rgyud): ma skyes pa'o//kye'i bcom ldan 'das/rdo rje'i lus la rtsa du lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rtsa rnams ni sum bcu rtsa gnyis te//byang chub kyi sems
3a4(rgyud): sum bcu rtsa gnyis dbab pa//bde ba chen po'i gnas su 'dzag pa'o//de rnams kyi nang nas rtsa rnam pa gsum ni rtso mo ste//rkyang ma dang/ro ma dang/kun
3a5(rgyud): dar ma'o//rkyang ma shes rab rang bzhin gyis//thabs kyi ro ma yang dag gnas//kun 'dar ma ni dbus gnas su//bzung dang 'dzin pa rnam par spangs//rkyang ma mi skyod
3a6(rgyud): dbab pa ste//ro ma de bzhin khrag 'bab cing//shes rab zla ba 'babs bshad pa//de ni kun 'dar ma rab grags//mi phyed ma dang/'phra gzugs ma dang//rtse ba ma dang/
3a7(rgyud): g.yon pa dang/thu ngu ma dang/rus sbal skyes ma dang/sgom pa ma dang/dbang ma dang/bskyed ma dang/'jug ma dang/ma mo dang/mtshan mo dang/gsil 'dzin ma dang//
3b1(rgyud): tsha ba mo dang;rkyang ma dang/kun 'dar ma dang//ro ma dang/gzhol ma dang/ras ma dang/shin tu gzugs can ma dang/spyi ma dang /rgyu sbyin dang/sbyor bral ma dang/sdu gu ma dang /grub ma dang/'tshed ma
3b2(rgyud): dang;de bzhin du yid bzangs ma dang/gsum bskor ma dang/'dod ma dang/khyim ma dang/rtum mo dang /bdud bral ma'o//rdo rje snying pos gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das rtsa sum bcu rtsa gnyis po 'di
3b3(rgyud): rnams ji lta bu lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/srid gsum yongs 'gyur thams cad ni//bzung dang 'dzin pa rnam par spangs//yang na thabs ni thams cad kyi//dngos po'i mtshan
3b4(rgyud): nyid du ni rtags//sdom pa'i dbye ba 'ang bshad par byaste//a li dang ka li dang/zla ba dang nyi ma dang/shes rab dang thabs so//chos dang longs spyod rdzogs pa dang//sprul pa dang ni
3b5(rgyud): sku dang thugs so//e bam ya ni e'i rnam pas lha mo sbyan//bgyi rnam pas bdag mor brjod//ma ni lha mo gos kar mo//ya'i rnam pas sgrol mar brjod//sprul pa'i 'khor lo la
3b6(rgyud): pad ma 'dab ma drug bcu rtsa bzhi dang/chos kyi 'khor lo la pad ma brgyad dang/longs spyod rdzogs pa'i 'khor lo la 'dab ma bcu drug dang/bde ba chen po'i 'khor la 'dab ma sum bcu rtsa gnyis so//
3b7(rgyud): 'khor lo'i drang IIrim pas rnam par bzhag pa skad cig pa'i gzhi ni rnam pa sna tshogs dang/rnam par smin pa dang/rnam par med pa dang/mtshan nyid dang bral ba'o//'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi ni;
3b8(rgyud): sdug bsngal Ikun 'byung dang/'gog pa dang/lam dang/de kho na nyid bzhi ni/bdag gi de kho na dang/sngags kyi de kho na dang/lha'i de kho na dang/ye shes kyi de kho na'o//dga' ba bzhi ni;dga' 
4a1(rgyud): @#//dang /mchog tu dga' ba dang /dga' bral gyi dga' ba dang /lhan cig skyes pa'i dga' ba dang /sde pa bzhi ni/gnas gtan pa dang /thams cad yod par smra ba dang /kun gyis
4a2(rgyud): bkur ba dang /de 'dun phal chen no//zla ba dang /nyi ma dang /a li dang /ka li dang /'pho ba bcu drug dang /dbyu gu drug bcu rtsa bzhi dang /chu tshod sum bcu rtsa gnyis dang /thun tshod bzhi ste/de ltar
4a3(rgyud): thams cad bzhi bzhi'o//lte bar Dum mo 'bar ba yis//de bzhin gshegs pa lnga srogs cing //spyan laswogs pa'ang yang sregs te//sregs pas ri 'ong can haM 'dzag////rdo rje rigs kyi le'u ste
4a4(rgyud): dang pa'o////sngags kyi le'u bshad par bya ste////oM a kha ro mu khaM sarba d+harma nang ar+ya nud pan ta tA oM a hUM phaT swahA//'byung po thams cad kyi gtor ma'i sngags so//
4a5(rgyud): bru~M oM dza~M kha~M hU~M/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sa bon no//oM d+he ba pe tsu badzra hU~M hU~M hU~M phaT swahA//kye'i rdo rje'i snying po'o//sngags thams cad kyi dang por ni/oM gyi rnam pa dang /mthar ni
4a6(rgyud): swahA dang hU~M phaT/kyi rnam pa ni nang du 'jug go//oM a ka tsa Ta ta ba ya sha swahA//grong khyer de krug pa'i sngags so//a A i i/u U/ri rI li li/e A+e/o A+o/aM//rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi sa bon/
4a7(rgyud): oM tre lo kyA k+she pa hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M phaDa swahA//phyag gnyis pa'i snying po'o//oM dza la dza la b+h+yo hU~M hU~M hU~M pha Ta swahA//phyag bzhi pa yi'o//oM ki ki Ti Ti badzra hU~M hU~M hUM phaT swahA//phyag drug pa'i'o//A+oM a hU~M//sku dang
4a8(rgyud): gsung dang thugs byin gyis rlabs pa'i sngags so//oM rak+sha rak+sha hU~M hU~M hU~M phag swahA//sa sbyang ba'i sngags so//A+oM hU~M swahA//rengs par byed pa'o//A+oM a swahA//dbang du byed pa'o//A+oM khaM swahA//
4b1(rgyud): bskrod par byed pa'o//oM badzri ro swAhA//sdang bar byed pa'o//A+oM buM swahA//mngon spyod do//A+oM hUM swAhA//'gugs par byed pa'o//A+oM gu swahA/A+oM ku ru ku ru le la hi swahA/ku ru ku lu'i sngags so//oM hUM
4b2(rgyud): swahA//a phu'i rnam pas mtha' yas kyi gzugs brnyan byas la/bdud rtsi lnga yis ni blugs/matog nag pos ni mchod//klu 'dul bar byed pa'i khu phyags su rdzing byas te/mtha' yas der gzhago//
4b3(rgyud): de'i 'gram du dkyil 'khor bzhengs par byaste//dur khrod kyi sol bas ni byug/glang po che'i chang gis ni mgo bo byug//kham phor kha sbyar du gzhug//ba nag mo'i 'o mas ni dgang /gzhon nu ma nag mos
4b4(rgyud): bkal ba'i bskud pas ni dkri/rlung gi phyogs su rdzing byas te/mtha'i yas der gzhag go//de'i 'bram du dkyil 'khor bzhengasapar byaste/dur khrod kyi sol bas ni rdul tshon nag po/skyes
4b5(rgyud): pa'i rus pa'i phye mas ni rdul tshon dkar po//ldong ros kyis ni rdul tshon ser po//dur khrod kyi sa phag gis ni rdul tshon dmar po//ro sregs pa'i sol ba dang skyes pa'i rus pas
4b6(rgyud): sngon po'o//rkun mo'i lo ma dang skyes pa'i rus pa'i phye ma dag gis ni rdul tshon ljang khu ste//rdul tshon 'di rnams kyis dkyil 'khor bzhengs so//dur khrod kyi srad bus thig gdab cing/dkyil
4b7(rgyud): 'khor khru gsu byas te/de'i dbus su mtha' yas kye rdo rje zhal brgyad pa/zhabs bzhi pa/phyag bcu drug gis brgyan pa/spyan nyi shu rtsa bzhis che ba 'jigs par mdzad pa bri'o//de nas bslob
4b8(rgyud): dpon bdag nyid shes shin tu ma rungs pa'i sems kyis dben pa'i gnas su sngags bzlas te//oM g+hu ru g+hu ru T+ya g+hu ga Ta ga Ta ma sha ma sha ga Ta g+ha Ta/g+ho Ta ya g+ho Ta ya/a rna rma k+sho ba ka rta ya na ga rti
5a1(rgyud): @#//ha ye he he he ru ru ka sa b+ha ba ta la ga ta nA gA na kA ka ya ga r+dz+dza ya ta r+dz+dza ya/pu phu phu phu phu phu phu phu/hUM hUM hUM phaT swahA//gal te char mi 'bab na/de'i tshe sngags 'di bzlog la zlos te/dbab po
5a2(rgyud): gal te mi dbab pa/de'i tshe mgo a rdza ga'i gdog pa ltar 'gis so//char dbab pa zhes bya ba'i cho ga'o//sprin 'bral ba bshad de/dur khrod kyi ras la nye bar 'dug nas/sngags zla zhing dral te//oM ar+ya 
5a3(rgyud): ar+ya sh+ma shA na sra ya/hUM hUM hUM paT swahA//sprin 'dral ba'i cho ga'o//pha rol gyi sde rnam par gzhig pa'i phyir//kha ti ga'i bsgrub pa bshad de/kha ti ka dang bdud rtsi lnga dang /ku tha ra tshi nan lhan cig
5a4(rgyud): btags la ri la byas te/sngags ni//oM badzra kar d+ha ri he badzra ya hUM hUM hUM phaDa swahA//nye ba'i snying po'o//dngos grub gyi don du bye ba zlas shing /sngon du bsnyen pa 'bum zlas te/de sgrubs
5a5(rgyud): la ril ba spyi blugs kyis mgrin pa bskor zhing bskor nas bcad na/dgra thams cad kyi mgo med par 'gyur ro//rdo rje dri khug gi cho ga'o//lha rnams 'dod pas thig le bsgrubs te/tshangs
5a6(rgyud): pa'i sa bon rgyal la bsgrubs nas/ku tha ra tshi nA dang sres la/nyi ma gzas zin pa na/mi skyod pa dang btags cing /btags nas dgra sta byaste/de rkang pas mnan nas sngags bzlas te//A+oM badzra ku ta ra
5a7(rgyud): pA ta ya hUM hUM hU~M phaT swahA//bye ba bzlas nas thig le byas te/gang la phyag byas pa de 'gas so//lha 'gas pa'i cho ga'o//tsha bas gdung bar bya ba 'dod pas/a rga'i lo ma dug dang skye tshe dang /tsi tra kI khu bas
5a8(rgyud): dgra'i ming bri zhing /'bras phub kyi me la gdugs te/sngags khri bzlas so//A+oM he badzra dz+wa ra dz+wa ra sha truM b+h+yo bruM hUM hUM hUM phaT swahA//tsha ba'i cho ga'o//khri bzlas pas 'grub bo//chang skyug par bya ba'i 'dod pas//
5b1(rgyud): bsgrub bya'i lte bar yaM gyi rnam pa bsgom zhing /maM gyi rnam par gyur pa las/chang lto bar rnam par bsgoms te/skyug par byed pa ltas na chang skyug go//rab tu myos ma dbang du bya bar 'dod pas/mya ngan med pa'i
5b2(rgyud): brgyud la mya ngan med pa'i 'og tu song ste/gos dmar po dgos te/myos byed kyi 'bras bu bza' zhing/kA mA tsi ke'i khu bas thig le byas nas/sngags bzlas te//oM a mu ki me hri b+ha si b+hi rba tu swahA//khris 'ong bar
5b3(rgyud): 'gyur ro//zla ba dang nyi ma dbang du bya bar 'dodapas/sa lu btags pa'i 'gyur ba la zla ba dang nyi ma byas nas rdo rje'i chu la bzhug cing sngags bzlas te//oM badzra r+k+ko ma tsa la ma tsa ma ti Sh+Ta ti Sh+Ta badzra ya hUM hUM hUM phaT
5b4(rgyud): swahA//bye ba khrag bdun bzlas te/nyi ma dang zla ba yang dag par gnas shing nyin mtshan bye brag med par 'gyur ro//nyi ma dang zla ba yongasu 'dzin pa zhes bya ba'i cho ga'o//oM na grA na grA zhes bya ba'i sngags 'dis
5b5(rgyud): dus ma yin pa'i ngas su don bsgrub pa'i phyir//gzhon nu ma'i mig dag la brgya rtsa brgyad mngon par sngags nas/me tog la sogs pa'i mchod pa lngas yang dag par mchod cing/snyan gsan dbab po//
5b6(rgyud): bcu bzhi pa 'am/brgyad pa'i snga gro'i dus su bum pa la sogs pa bzhag cing/'bru mar dang a k+ha'i khu ba sngags 'di nyid kyis lan brgya rtsa brgyad mngon par mngag go//de nas mngon par sngags pa
5b7(rgyud): a la ka ta'i khu ba sngags pa'i 'the bo la bskas nas/'bru mar gyis kyang blags la gzhon nu ma bstan te/nga'i rdzas sus khyer smras so//de la des smras pa/che ge mo zhes bya bas smras pa/khyer ro rdo rje dkar
5b8(rgyud): btsis mo//be Ta Tu ya zhes brjod na glang po lo a ya zhu ro//ma m+mA pa m+mA zhes brjod na stag 'gros par 'gyur ro//ti li ya ti li ya zhes brjod na/gse 'bros par 'gyur ro//i li mi li phu phu
6a1(rgyud): @#//zhes brjod na su' bas par 'gyur ro//naro stong 'dul ba'i phyag gis lag tu bstan na khyi 'gros par 'gyur ro//rdo rje dang/dkar mo dang/chu ma dang/rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma dang/bdag med ma
6a2(rgyud): dang/sa spyod ma Imkha' spyod par rnal 'byor las/rangs pa Ila sogs pa rtul shugs can gyis byed do//sngags kyi le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas lha'i le'u bshad par bya'o//
6a3(rgyud): dang po byams pa Isgom par bya//gnyis pa snying rje bsgom bya zhing//gsum pa dga' ba bsgom pa dang//kun gyi mtha' nas btang snyoms so//yang ni stong pa'i byang chub ste//gnyis pa la ni sa bon bsdu//
6a4(rgyud): gsum pa la ni gzugs brnyan rdzogs//bzhi pa la ni yig 'bru dgod//re bas nyi ma sngon du rnam bsgoms na//nyi der hUM 'byung sna tshogs yige ste//rdo rje de nyid kyis ra ba dang//gur bcing ba yang rnam pa(ra)
6a5(rgyud): bsgom pa nyid//dang por ro ni rnam bsgom pa//chos kyi dbyings kyi bdag nyid brjod//de ste rnal 'byor pa gnas nas//he ru ka nyid rnam par bsgom//de las byung ba'i nyid kyi 'khor//der ni 
6a6(rgyud): hUM gi rnam pa nyid//thabs dang shes rab rang bzhin can//hUM las byung ba'i rdo rje ni//kha dog nag po 'jigs chen po//rdo rje lte ba'i dbus gnas par //yang ni hUM gi de nyid bsgom//hUM gi rnam pa gyur
6a7(rgyud): ltas nas//zhe sdang bdag nyid rnam par bsgom//rdo rje skye ba nag po che//'dam skyes sngon po 'dra ba'i mdog//yang na sngo dang nyi ma'i mdog//dad pas nges par rnam par grol//nam khar rje btsun ltas
6a8(rgyud): nas ni;;rdo rje skye ba snying rje che//rgyan rnams thams cad 'dzin pa yi//lha mo brgyad po rnams kyis mchod//dkar mo ri dgas mtshan ma 'dzin//chom rkun bdud las rgyal ba'i snod//ro langs ma ni chu
6b1(rgyud): lag ma//g+ha sma ri ni sman 'dzin cing //bu ka si ni rdo rje lag//de bzhin ri khrod ma ro 'dzin//rdol pa mo ni cang de'u gdung//'di rnams kyis ni gtso bo mchod//'dod chags chen po'i rjes chags nas//
6b2(rgyud): g.yung mos sku la 'khyud pa nyid//a li zla ba ka li nyid//sa bon nang du son gyur pa//de nyid sems dpa' zhes byar brjod//mchog tu dga' ba'i rang bzhin can//namakha'i dkyil 'khor khyab pa yi//
6b3(rgyud): rang gi lus 'tshungs rnam par spro//bsdus nas snying khar dgug pa na//rnal 'byor zhe sdang bdag nyid gyur//sngo dang nyi ma mtshungs pa'i mdog//b+han du dmar po'i spyan dang ldan//skra ser gyen du 'khyil
6b4(rgyud): ba dang//phyag rgya lngas ni rnam par brgyan//'khor lo rna cha nor bu dang//lag ldub dang ni skye rags nyid//sangs rgyas lnga ni rnam dag pa//'di rnams phyag rgya rab tu grags//sdang mig brgyad
6b5(rgyud): gnyis lo yi tshul//g.yon na rdo rje thod pa dang//g.yon pa na yang khwa trang kha//g.yas na rdo rje nag pa nyid//hU~M gi yi ge 'don pa'i bdag//rnal 'byor ma brgyad yongs bskor nas//
6b6(rgyud): dur khrod du ni mgon po rol//ro gnas zhes bya'i rig pa 'dis//dur khrod zhes ni mngon par brjod//phyag bzhi bdud ni bzhi las rnam par dag pa ste//gong du brjod pa'i gzugs dang/sku mdog
6b7(rgyud): dang hUM las byung ma'o//phyag g.yon gyi dang po na/skyes pa'i thod pa lha dang lha min gyi khrag gis gang ba/de bzhin du g.yas kyi phyag na rdo rje phyag lhag ma dag gis shes rab la 'khyud pa/shes rab rdo rje
6b8(rgyud): phag mo ni bcom ldan 'das kyis gzugs can de//phyag drug pa la zhal gsum pa//g.yon dmar ba/g.yas zla ba dang nyi ma lta bu/dang po sngon po//gcer bu gong du brjod pa'i sku mdog dang gzugs can
7a1(rgyud): @#//rnams ni pha rol du phyin pa rnam pa lnga pa'o//phag g.yon pa'i dang po na rtse gsum pa/g.yas kyi dang po na rdo rje/phyag g.yon gyi gnyis pa na dril bu//phyag g.yas kyi gnyis pa na
7a2(rgyud): gri zug/lhag ma gis rdo rje lu gu rgyud ma la snyoms par 'jug pa/bcom ldan 'das ci lta ba de ltar shes rab ste//g.yas pa dang g.yon pa na gri khug dang thod pa/khams gsum gyi bdag nyid can
7a3(rgyud): gyi ro mnan pa'/lha'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////lha dbang bskur ba'i le'u bshad par bya'o//rang gi snying khar sa bon bsam mo//sa bon las 'od zer nag po lcags bkyu rnam pa'i
7a4(rgyud): gzugs kyis byung nas/des khams gsum du bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas thams cad spyan drangs la/lha mo brgyad kyis yang dag par mchod nas/gsol ba btab par bya'o//oM a b+hi sh+tu+in tu maM
7a5(rgyud): sarba ta tha ga ta e ti zhes/sangs rgyas de rnams he ru ka'i rnam pa'i gzugs kyis de bzhin gshegs pa lnga'i bdag nyid kyi bdud rtsi lngas gang ba'i bum pa lngas dbang skur bar bya'o//dbang
7a6(rgyud): skur ba na me tog gi char dbab par 'gyur ro//rnga'i sgra 'byung bar ro//gur gum gyi char dbab par 'gyur ro //rnga'i sgra 'byung bar 'gyur ro//gzugs kyi rdo rje ma la swogs pa rnams kyis mchod par bya'o//
7a7(rgyud): rdo rje'i klu'i spyan laswogs pa rnams kyis blang bar bya'o//dbang skur nas spyod pa na rang gi rigs kyi bdag por 'gyuro//'dis ni he ru ka rdzogs pa nyid do//thun gsum byin gyis rlabs pa'i bsgom pa
7a8(rgyud): rnam par sgoms nas/langs te lha'i gzugs su bzhag go//dbang gi le'u ste bzhi pa'o////de nas kho na nyid kyi le'u bshad par bya'o//ngo bo gzugs med mthong ba po yang med//
7b1(rgyud): sgra med thos pa po yang med//dri med snyoms pa po yang med//ro med myong ba po yang med//reg med reg pa po yang med//sems med sems la sa byung ba yang med//skye med ma sring mo nyid//rnal
7b2(rgyud): 'byor rig pas rtag tu mchod//gar ma chos ma rdo rje ma//rdol pa de bzhin bram ze ma//thabs dang shes rab cho ga yis//de nyid rig pas rtag tu mchod//ci ltar shes pa mi 'gyur bar//rab tu
7b3(rgyud): 'bad pas sten pa nyid//ma gsangs sprul dang chom rkun dang//stag dang me'i sdug bsngal byed//rigs lnga'i phyag rgya zhes bya ba//thar pa'i rgyus ni brjod par bya//rdo rje 'dis ni bdab pa nyid//
7b4(rgyud): des na phyag rgya brjod par bya//rdo rje pad ma de bzhin las//de bzhin gshegs dang rin chen nyid//snying rje chen po dam pa yi//rigs ni rnam pa lnga ru brjod//rdo rje'i phyag rgya g.yung mo nyid//
7b5(rgyud): de bzhin pad ma gar ma nyid//las ni chos mar rab tu bshad//de bzhin gshegs pa bram ze mo//rin chen rdol pa mo sh+se bya//phyag rgya lngar ni rnam par nges//'di rnams de bzhin
7b6(rgyud): gshegs pa'i rigs//mdor bsdus pas ni brjod par bya//dpal ldan de bzhin nyid gshegs shing//de bzhin slar yang gshegs pa nyid//'dis ni shes rab rigs pa yis//de bzhin
7b7(rgyud): gshegs zhes brjod par bya//rigs ni rnam pa drug brjod cing//mdor bsdus pas ni rnam pa lnga//sku gsung thugs kyi dbye ba yis//phye nas rnam pa gsum du 'gyur//rigs ni 'byung ba lnga rnams
7b8(rgyud): dang //phung po lnga rang bzhin rigs//dag par bya ba 'di yis ni//rigs zhes mngon par brjod par bya//bsgom pa po med bsgom pa 'ang med//lha med sngags kyang yod ma yin//spros pa
8a1(rgyud): @#//med pa'i rang bzhin las bsags pa lha ni yang dag gnas//rnam snang mi skyod don yod dang//rin chen dpag med sems dpa'o//tshangs pa khyab 'jug zhi ba dang//thams cad sang+sa
8a2(rgyud): rgyas de nyid brjod//tshangs pa la bsgrib bral sangs rgyas nyid//'jug phyir sangs rgyas khyab 'jug brjod par bya//rtag tu dge ba zhi ba nyid//thams cad sangs rgyas de nyid brjod//tshangasapa'i sgrib bral
8a3(rgyud): kun gyi bdag nyid gnas//dam pa'i bde bas de nyid de//bde ba rtogs phyir rnam sangs rgyas//gang phyir lus las byung ba nyid//lha zhes mngon par brjod par bya//sangs rgyas 'di la ba ga mnga'//bcom
8a4(rgyud): ldan 'das zhes brjod par bya//ba ga rnam pa drug tu brjod//dbang phyug laswogs yon tan kun//kun nas nyon mongs laswogs bdud//'joms phyir bcom ldan 'das zhes bya//gang phyir skye 'gro bskyed
8a5(rgyud): pa yi;;shes rab ma zhes brjod par bya//gang phyir bskal pa ston pa yi//shes rab sring mo zhes bya nyid//btso brlag ma dang bu mo dang//gar mkhan ma ma brjod par bya//sems can thams cad ma tshed
8a6(rgyud): pa'i phyir;;gtso blag ma zhes de bzhin brjod//yon tan 'jo phyir shes rab ni//bu mo zhes ni brjod par bya//snying rje chen po g.yo ba'i phyir//shes rab gar mar brjod par bya//gang phyi bcom ldan
8a7(rgyud): mi reg pa//de phyir g.yung mor brjod par bya//a li ka li rab rtags pa//brjod pa bzlas par yang dag bshad//rkang pa'i rjes ni dkyil 'khor yin//nyid phyir dkyil 'khor brjod par bya//lag
8a8(rgyud): pa sgyur ba phyag rgya yin//sor mo nyed pa 'ang de bzhin no//gang phyir rnam sems bsam gtan ni//gang bsams de ni bsam gtan no//ji ltar pha las bde thob pa//de'i bde ba rang gis bza'//
8b1(rgyud): bde ba gang gis che ba 'dir//de'i bde ba bsam gtan brjod//de kho na nyid kyi le'u ste lnga ba'o////de nas gzhan yang rab bshad bya//spyod pa pha rol phyin pa'i mchog//gang gis dngos
8b2(rgyud): grub mthar 'gro ba//kye'e rdo rje dngos grub rgyu//bsgom pa po yis rnam 'dzin pa//rna ba dag la rna ba bzang//spyi bor 'khor lo rnam par 'dzin//lag pa dag la lag gdub nyid//rkyed pa la yang skye 
8b3(rgyud): rags nyid//rkang pa la yang rkang ldub nyid//lag pa'i rtsa bar dpung rgyan nyid//mgrin par rus pa'i 'phreng ba nyid//dgo ba stag gi lpags pa nyid//bza' ba ma 'chi bcu phyed de//he ru ka sbyor skyes
8b4(rgyud): bu nyid//rigs lnga dag la mnyam ldan pa//rigs ni gcig bu rnam dag pa//rigs dag du ma nyid kyis ni//gang phyir dbye ba mtshon mi bya//shing cig 'am ni dur khrod dang//ma mo'i khyim dang mtshan
8b5(rgyud): mo dang//yang na dben pa 'am bas mtha' ru//bsgom pa bzang por brjod par bya//cung zad grod ni thob pa na//gal te spyod pa byed 'dod pas//gal te 'grub 'gyur 'dod yod na//'dis ni spyod pa
8b6(rgyud): spyad pa nyid//shin du bzhin bzangs mig yangs pa//rang gi dbang bskur snying rje can//rdo rje bu mo 'di khyer nas//spyod pa bya bar shin du rigs//rdo rje rigs kyi dngos med na//rang 'dod lha'i rig+sa
8b7(rgyud): las bya//I I gzhan gyi rigs las byang//byang chub sa bon las myong nas//sbyangs te khyer nas spyad par bya//gal te dga' bas klu blangs te//de tshe rdo rje mchog dang ldan//gal te dga'
8b8(rgyud): ba skyes pa na//thar pa I ni gar byaste//de tshe I gar//yo gis mnyam par bzhag las byed//'khor lo'i gzugs kyis mi skyod pa//rna ba'i bdag nyid 'od dpag med//mgul
9a1(rgyud): @#//gyi gnyen chen chen po////////par snang mdzad brjod//skye rags la ni don yod gnas//kha twaM kha gzugs shes rab ste//thabs kyi gzugs kyis cang de'u
9a2(rgyud): nyid;zhi sdang na ngag rnam 'byar pa//klu ni rnam dag sngags bskang I gyi bsgom pa byas pa nyid//de'i phyir na klu dang gar//rnal 'byor pa ni rtag tu byed//sman ni rnam par bza' ba ste//chu ni
9a3(rgyud): rtag par btung ba dang//rga shi zil gyis mi non cing//rtag tu srung bar mi 'gyur ba yin//rkun mo'i ska la gcod pan byas//de las hUM byung sbyar bar bya'o//sangs rgyas lnga'i thod pas rnams//
9a4(rgyud): rnal 'byor spyod pas rnam par 'dzin//thod pa sor lnga 'apa yi char//byas pas gcod pan 'dzin par byed//thabs dang ࿙shes rab rang bzhin las//skra'i skye rags nyid skor cig//
9a5(rgyud): thal ba skra'i mchod phyir thogs//rnal 'byor pa yi spyod pas 'dzin;cang de'u sgra ni bzlas pa ste//khwa taM kar skoms shes rab nyid//rdo rje thod pa'i sbyor ba yis//'di dag bzlas dang
9a6(rgyud): bsgom pa yin//rkam dang mongs dang 'jigs dang 'phro//ngo tsha'i 'bras bu rnam par spangs//bdag nyid kyis ni gnyid spangs nas//the tsom med par spyod par bya//lus kyi sbyin pa byin nas ni//
9a7(rgyud): phyi nas yang dag spyod pa spyod//bskal dang bskal min rnam spyad nas//de phyir sbyin pa byin mi bya//bza' bya de bzhin btung ba nyid//ci ltar brnyed pa rab tu bza'//yid 'ong mi 'ong rnam rtog phyir;;zhen
9a8(rgyud): pa tsam du mi bya'o//bza' dang bza' min spyod pa dang//de bzhin btung dang btung min nyid//'gro dang 'gro min spangs pa nyid//rnam rtog nyid du mi bya'o//dngos grub rnyed pa'i slob ma gang//yang dag
9b1(rgyud): ye shes snang ba'o//gnar med spangs pa'i rgyu'i phyir//grub pas bla ma mngon phyag 'tshal//bslab las spang las nges par grol//de bzhin ngo tsha'i 'bras bu nyid//dngos po kun gyi rang bzhin gyi//yo gis
9b2(rgyud): rnam spyad snying rje che//sbyin sregs mchod sprin dka' thub 'das//sngags dang bsam gtan rnam par spangs//dam tshig sdom las rnam par grol//rnal 'byor ldan pas spyod pa byed//nges par sngon
9b3(rgyud): du lha min gang//brgya byin lta bu yin na yang//de la 'jigs par mi byaste//seng ge'i gzugs kyis rnam par rgyu//sems can kun gyi don gyi phyir//rtag tu snying rje btung bar bya//rnal 'byor btung
9b4(rgyud): bas rnal 'byor pa//gzhan gyi btung bas gzi ba med//spyod pa'i le'u ste drug pa'o////de nas rda'i le'u bshad par bya'o//gang gis spun dang sring mor yang//
9b5(rgyud): the tsom med par shes par bya//gang zhig sor mo gcig ston pa//gnyis kyis legs par 'os pa yin//g.yon gis the bong bcangs pa des//snyun gyi phyag rgyar ra tu shes//gang zhig
9b6(rgyud): srin lag ster pa la//de la the'u chung rnam par sbyin//gang zhig gung mo ston pa la//de la 'dzub mo rnam par sbyin//gang zhigi srin lag ston pa la//de la mgrin pa rab tu bstan//gang zhig mgo bo
9b7(rgyud): bston pa la//de la rtse gsum rab tu bstan//gang zhin nu ma bston pa la//de la mtshams ni rab tu bstan//gang zhi ga so ni ston pa la//de la kha ni rab tu bstan//gang cig so     ni rab stan na//de la
9b8(rgyud): 'khor lo rab tu bsten//Izhig khro gnyer ro//Igcig phud grol bar brjad par bya//gang zhig kral bar ston pa la//de la rgyab ni rab tu bstan//gang zhIg rkang mthil ston pa la//lto yis
10a1(rgyud): @#//rnam par brtse bar bya// I rag rgya'i lan gis ni//dam tshig gis ni rnams par dbye//de la rnal 'byor mas smras pa//e ma bu ni snying rje che//gal te 'phreng
10a2(rgyud): ba'i lag ston//I ba zhes smra ba I//'phreng ba mngon par btang byas nas//dam tshig lam mnos rtul shugs bzang//de la phyi rol zhes 'du na//bzang po spyod yul gnas
10a3(rgyud): pa ni//de I'du ba'i rnal 'byor mas//gang smras de ltar thams cad bya////kye bcom ldan 'das 'du ba'i gnasu lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//gnas dang
10a4(rgyud): nye ba'i gnas dang ni//zhing dang nye ba'i zhing nyid dang//tshan 'do' nye ba'i tshan mdo dang//de bzhin 'du ba'i nye 'du ba//mthung spyod nye ba'i mthung spyod dang//dur khrod nye ba'i
10a5(rgyud): dur khrod nyid//'di rnams sa ni bcu gnyis te//sa bcu'i dbang phyug mgon po nyid//'di ni gzhan gyis brjod mi bya//kye bcom ldan 'das gnas la swogs pa gang
10a6(rgyud): lags//bcom ldan 'das kis bka' stsal pa//gnas ni dza lan d+ha rar gshad//de bzhin du ni u brgyan nyid//gnas ni ko l+li gi rang nyid//de bzhin du 'ang gam pa nyid//nye gnas
10a7(rgyud): ma la wi zhes brjod//sin d+hu na ga ra nyid do//zhing ni mu mu nir gshad do//zhing ni byed pa'i brang nyid do//de bi ko ta de bzhin zhing//zhing ni lcags pa'i brang nyid do//nye zhing ku la ta
10a8(rgyud): zhes brjod//de bzhin ar bu ta nyid dang//ba'i mchog sbyin kha bI ri//nye ba'i zhing ni mdor sdus pa'o//mtshan d+ho ha ra ke la dang//lan tsha brgya mtsho'i nang skyed dang//lam pa ka dang
10b1(rgyud): gan tsi nyid//de bzhin su'u ra Sh+Ta nyid do//nye ba'i mtshan d+ho ga li ka//gser dang ldan pa'i kling dang ni//kong ka na ya nye tshan dor//mdor sdus nas ni gshad par bya//'thung spyod
10b2(rgyud): grong gi mthar gnas pa//tsa ri tra dang ko sha la//b+hin rta gzhon nu'i grong khyer ro//rdo rje snying po snying rje che//nye ba'i mthung spyod de nye ba'o//dur khrod rab son dge 'dun dang//dur khrod rgya
10b3(rgyud): mtsho'i 'gram nyid do//skyed tshal ra ba'i rdzing bu'i 'bram//nye ba'i dur khrod brjod par bya//sems can kun kyi don kyi phyir//rnal 'byor ma rgyud kye rdo rje//rnal 'byor ma'i legs 'du ba
10b4(rgyud): nyin par kyang ni gshad par bya//kye bcom ldan 'das nyi ma gang lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//yi dgas zla phyed bcu bzhi dang//de bzhin du 'ang brgyad pa nyid//rgyal
10b5(rgyud): mtshan dang ni mtshon snan nyid//lan bdun por yang bza' bar bya//'bad pa'i snying rje bskyed pa yis//gsad par bya ba brjod pa nyid//snying rje med na mi 'grub pas//de
10b6(rgyud): phyir snying rje bskyed pa nyid//thams cad bdug pa bzhug par bya//cho ga'i rtso bos sgrub par bya//de la 'di ltar lta ba nyid//nyin mo bcom ldan rdo rje can//mtshan mo
10b7(rgyud): shes rab dag pa bya//pa pa rang zad yong ma yin//rtag tu mi bza' yod ma yin//'di la mi gsam yod ma yin//bzang ngan mi smra gang yang med//ji ltar bdag nyid de ltar gzhan
10b8(rgyud): ci ltar bzhag pa dang //'di ltar sems dpa'i rnal 'byor bdag//bza' dang btung ba lags spyod//yan lag ji snyed sgyur ba dang//
11a1(rgyud): tshig dang btsun pa ba'i bka dpal gnas bzhugs pas//de snyed sngags dang phyag rgya yin//shri ni gnyis med ye shes te//he ni rgyu bsogs stong pa
11a2(rgyud): nyid ma yin pa dang // ༒ //!//!//de snyed sngags dang gang rnams kyi//sha ni mkhas pas bza' bar bya//sems
11a3(rgyud): can de de bzang po bya//gnas gtan la phab pa'i le'u bdun pa'o//!//de nas rnal 'byor ma'i 'khor lo gshad par bya'o//nam mkha'i khamasu b+ha ga bsaM
11a4(rgyud): dbus su rnam pa ni//'khor lo sngon du ci rigs par//lha rnams ji ltar 'byung ba nyid//'khor lo sa dang chu ni sngo//ci ltar rigs par byin za dang //lha rnams kyi
11a5(rgyud): ni rlung chen dang //ji ltar sgom pa po 'byung ba//chos 'byung las skyes 'khor lo nyid//'phar ma gnyis dag skyon med pa//ze 'bru la ni gcig yin te//gru gsum gi ni
11a6(rgyud): phyi ma brjod//de dbus zhi ba cwa lnga'i//bdan gi bdag nyid rnam par bsam//de'i steng du zla ba yin//zla ba'i steng du sa bon nyid//phyi nas bdud las rgyal bas gnan//gnyis 'dus
11a7(rgyud): pa las bde chen po//a li zla ba'i gzugs kyis gnas//ka li gzugs kyis snang byed do//zla ba nyi ma 'dus pa las//dkar mo laswogs rab tu grags//zla ba me long ye shes ldan
11a8(rgyud): bdun gi bdun pa mnyam nyid ldan//rang lha'i sa bon phyag mtshan nyid//so sor rtog par brjod par bya//thams cad gcig 'gyur nan tan nyid//rdzogs pa chos dbyings dag pa ste//
11b1(rgyud): mkhas pas cho ga gsungs pa yis//rnam pa lnga po sgom pa nyid//a li ka li mnyam sbyor ba//rdo rje sems dpa' nyid kyi bdan//yi ge las byung gong bu las//hU~M phaT rnam pa 'ang gdod mi bya
11b2(rgyud): sems de'i gzugs rnyan las byung ba//dkyil 'khor bdag por rnam par sgom//phyag mtshan zhal bsogs go ma bzhin//zla ba chu shel nor bu'i 'od//thabs dang shes rab rang bzhin las
11b3(rgyud): 'di ltar thams cad rdzogs pa nyid//zla ba nyi ma'i rab dbye bas//a li shes rab ka li thabs//'di las kar ma laswogs pa//sa bon dbye bas so so yin//re zhig nang gi 'phar ma
11b4(rgyud): las;;rnal 'byor ma lnga der gnas pa//phung po lnga'i rang bzhin gis//rnal 'byor rig pas rtag tu bsgom//dbang por rdo rje gshin rje dkar//chu bdag chu'i rnal 'byor ma//lus ngan
11b5(rgyud): rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma//dbus su bdag med rnal 'byor ma/phyi rol gyi ni 'phar ma la//dkar mo chom rkun ro langs ma//g+ha sma ri dang pu ka si//ri khrod ma dang bdol pa mo//brgyad
11b6(rgyud): pa ToM b+hi ni ru brjod//'og lan ma dang steng ldan nyid//mkha' spyod ma dang sa spyod brjod//srid dang zhi ba rang bzhin gyis//snying rje chen po 'di dag gnas//lha mo 'di dag
11b7(rgyud): thaMs cad ma dang //dang chen phag rgya lnga yis rgyan//lag par dri khug thod pas khyab//'khor lo rna cha 'gul brgyan dang //lag par lag rdub skye regs nyid//sangs rgyas lnga ni rnam
11b8(rgyud): dag pas; I I I I I ji ltar bdag med rnal 'byor ma//de ltar thams cad rnam par gshad//lag pa gcig ni thod pas 'brel//g.yas pa na ni gri khug 'dzin;;
12a1(rgyud): //g.yon pas kha TaM la nyid do//stag gi pags pas rnam bkris shing//ro la zhon zhing gzi brjid 'bar//phyag gnyis gyen du dbu skyes ser//nga rgyal la sogs
12a2(rgyud): skyon drug rnam par kha ta kha//thabs kyis ro ni rnam par dag//'dis ni 'khor lo rnaMs bsgoms pas//dngos grub myur du thob par 'gyur//dang por nag po rab
12a3(rgyud): tu bsgom////nye par dmar po rnam par bsgom//gsuM pa la ni so sor bsgom//bzhi par rdzing bu de bzhin no//lnga par kha dog sngon po dang//drug par dkar ba'i lus
12a4(rgyud): can te//yan lag drug bsgoms rnal 'byor pas//dag 'bral thar pa de bzhin no//bskyed pa'i rim pa nyid dang ni//rdzogs pa'i yi yang rim pa nyid//rim gnyis mnyam
12a5(rgyud): par gnas nas ni//rdo rje can gyis chos 'chad do//bskyed pa'i ni rim gshad do//nam mkha'i khams ni pad ma la//b+ha ga zhes bya ye shes brjod//bsgom pa zhes bya snyoms 'jug
12a6(rgyud): nyid//de'i bde ba 'khor lo brjod//rim pa ji bzhin rang rig nyid//lha zhes bya ba byang chub sems//ci ltar byung ba khu ba yin//lhan cig skyes pa rnam pa gnyis//re zhig btsun
12a7(rgyud): mo shes rab ste//skyes bu thabasu brjod pa nyid//kun brdzob don dam phye ba nyid//phyi yis de'i rnam pa gnyis//re zhig skyes bu rnam pa gnyis//khu bas de ni rnam pa gnyis//
12b1(rgyud): skyes bu ci zhig shes rab las//de'i khu ba 'am bde ba nyid//de nyid phyir na dga' ba ni//bzhi pa yi ni dbye ba yis//skye med rim pa'i phyogs las ni//'di ltar lhan cig skyes 
12b2(rgyud): rnams bzhi//dga' ba dang po dpa' bo nyid//mchog tu dga' ba rnal 'byor ma//shin du bde dga' thams cad dngos//de bde thabs las thams cad rig//dga' bas bde ba 
12b3(rgyud): cung zad bde//mchog tu dga' ba de las lhag//dga' bral dga' bas chags bral yin//lhag ma lhan cig skyes dga' nyid//dang por reg par 'dod pa nyid//gnyis pa bde ba
12b4(rgyud): 'dod pa dang//gsum pa 'dod chags 'jig pa nyid//des na bzhi pa bsgom par bya//mchog tu dga' ba srid par brjod//dga' bral las ni mya ngan 'das//dbu ma dga' ba tsam
12b5(rgyud): nyid do//lhan cig skyes pas 'di nyid spangs//'dod chags med cing chags bral med//dbu mar dmigs par mi 'gyur ro//gsum rnams spangs par 'gyur ba las
12b6(rgyud): lhan cig skyes pa'i byang chub gsung//'di la thabs dang shes rab med//yang dag de bzhin snang ba'o//gzhan gis brjod min lhan cig skyes//gang du yang ni mi rnyed
12b7(rgyud): bla ma rgyud thabs ston pa yis//bdag gi bsod nams las shes bya//dman pa'i 'bring dang mchog rnaMs dang//gzhan dang gang rnams de rnams kyang//ro mnyam de nyid bsgo
12b8(rgyud): pa yis//Ina mnyam pa nyid du lta//dman pa gnas ni 'pha ba nyid//mchog gi dngos por brjod par bya//dbu ma gnyis kyis spangs pa nyid//gzhan rnams zhes bya
13a1(rgyud): @#//dbang po drug//gang rnams de rnams rtan dang g.yo//'di kun zhes bya nga nyid med//mnyam nyid mtshungs par 'dod pa nyid//ro mnyam de nyid bsgom pas ni//mnyam
13a2(rgyud): zhes bya ba mchus dang //de'i 'khor lo ra zhe brjod//sgom pa ro cig mnyam pa nyid//'dis ni don gyis brjod par bya//nga las 'gro ba thams cad 'byung//nga las gnas gsuM
13a3(rgyud): po yang 'byung //kun khyab pa ste//'gro ba'i rang bzhin gzhan ma mthong//de ltar rnal 'byor pas shes nas//shin du mnyam pa gang goms pa//bsod nams chung ba'i mi 'is
13a4(rgyud): kyang //de ni gru Ithe tsom med//bza' dang btung ba glag pa dang//nyal dang log dang sems dpa' dang//phyag rgya chen po mngon 'dod pa//de nas mngon du 'gro bar 'gyur//
13a5(rgyud): gang phyir yid kyis mi sgom par//'gro ba thams cad sgom par bya//thams cad chos ni yangs shes na//sgom pa nyid ni sgom pa min//brtan dang g.yo ba'i dngos po gang//rtsa
13a6(rgyud): lcug khri shing laswogs pa//bdag gi dngos po'i rang bzhin las//dam pa'i de nyid nges sgom bya//de rnaMs dam pa gcig yod min//rang gi bde ba chen po nyid //rang rig gnas ni byang chub 'gyur/
13a7(rgyud): rang gi phyir na sgom pa nyid//rang gi rig pas 'gyur ba las//dman pa las ni las skyes te//rang gyis 'phrog cing rang gis byed//rang gi rgyal po rang nyid gtso//'dod chags zhe sdang 'phra
13a8(rgyud): dog dang//de bzhin gti mug nga rgyal nyid//de kun de ni nyams dga' b+Ra//bcu drug char yang mi phod do//chos 'byung las skyes ye shes ni//mkha' mnyam lhan cig thabs dang bcas//thab+sa
13b1(rgyud): dang shes rab rang bzhin las//'jig rten gsum po de las skyes//khu ba'i rnam pa bcom ldan yin//de'i bde ba 'dod par brjod//gcig dang du ma bral ba de//skad cig gcig la mchog
13b2(rgyud): dga' ba//rang gi rig pa'i ye shes 'di//ngag gi las 'das spyod yul 'di//'di ni byin rlabs rim pa'i phyir//kun mkhyen ye shes de lta bu//sa dang chu dang me dang rlung dang ni//me dang
13b3(rgyud): nam mkha' nyid dag ni//rang bzhin rig pa'i tshor ba yis//kun kyi skad cig mi gnod de//mtho ris mi yul sa 'og gi//skad cig las ni gzugs gcig 'gyur//rang gzhan cha'i rnam
13b4(rgyud): rtog gis//gnod pa yis nyid ni nus ma yin//thams cad rig byed grub mtha' dang//de bzhin las rgya la sogs pas//dag pa'i dngos grub 'byung ma yin//yang ni srid mthar skye bar 'gyur/
13b5(rgyud): 'jig rten 'di dang pha rol tu//de med pas kyang dngos grub med//gang gis mi shes kye rdo rje//de ni del ba don med 'gyur//chu bo'i rgyun ni rab 'bab dang//mar me'i rtse mor
13b6(rgyud): rab bcings ltar//rtag tu de nyid rnal 'byor kyis//nyin dang mtshan du rnam par gzhag//rnal 'byor ma'i 'khor lo zhes bya ba 'du ba'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o////de nas mchog

14b1(rgyud): phyogs;;srid dang zhi ba'i rang bzhin gyis//lha mo 'di gnyis rnam par gnas//gzugs la dkar mo rab tu bshad//sgra la chum rkun ma rab grags//dri'i cha la ro langs ma//ro la
14b2(rgyud): g+ha smra ri rabs grags//reg pa sa spyod ma zhes gshad//chos kyi dbyings las mkha' spyod ma//rtag tu 'di rnaMs rnam dag pas//de nyid rnal 'byor pas 'grub 'gyur//phyag gis dag pa

14b4(rgyud): brjod//me ni rtum mor shes par bya//rlung ni g.yung mor shes par bya//zhe sdang zhes bshad rdo rje ma//'dod chags go ri'i rnal 'byor ma//phra dog rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma//ser sna gsang bI
14b5(rgyud): dkar mo nyid//gti mug de bzhin rdo rje gshad//zhe sdang lasogs rnam sbyong ba//bskyed pa'i rim pa'i phyogs las ni//'dis ni phung po sbyang bar bya//gang dang gang gis 'jig
14b6(rgyud): rten 'ching 'gyur pa//de dang des ni bcing ba las grol te//de nyid mi shes 'jig rten mi grol zhing//de nyid rnam spangs dngos grub rnyed mi 'gyur//de phyir dri med sgra med   
14b7(rgyud): gzugs nyid med//ro dang sems kyi rnaM par dag pa med//reg med chos med thams cad rnam par dag//rang bzhin dag pa'i 'gro bal 'gro bar shes//rnam par dag pa'i le'u zhes byas ste//

15a1(rgyud): @#//'byor pas ni chos 'dzin sbyang //'bad pa'i hUM las rdo rje can//byas te phyi nas dkyil 'khor bri//tshal dang skye bo med gnas dang//byang chub sems dpa' khyim dang ni//dkyil 'khor
15a2(rgyud): khang pa'i nang du yang//dkyil 'khor dam pa rab tu gzhengs//rdul tshan dam pa'i tshon dang ni//yang na 'bring po nyid kyis te//rin chen lnga'i phye ma dang//yang na 'bras la sogs paso//de nas
15a3(rgyud): dkyil 'khor gsum dang //the bong gsuM ni lhag par bya//der ni rigs lnga las byung ba'i//rig ma bzang mo rnam par gzhugs//yang na ji ltar gang rnyed de//de ltar bcu drug lon pa nyid//ci srid

15a5(rgyud): nyid la yang bya bar bya//rang rig ye shes nyid du 'gyur//rang gzhan yang dag rig pa spangs//mkha' mnyam bdun bral stong pa nyid//dngos dang dngos med bdag med mchog
15a6(rgyud): thabs dang shes rab shin du 'dres//chags dang chags bral rnam par 'dres//de nyid srog chags rnams kyi srog//de nyid yi ge dam pa nyid//de nyid 'gro ba'i bdag nyid de//de nyid thaMs cad khyab
15a7(rgyud): pa po//thams cad lus la rnam par gnas//dngos dang dngos med de las 'byung//gzhan dag gang rnams //rnam par shes pa kun kyi tshul//skyes bu sngon rabs dbang phyug dang//bdag
15a8(rgyud): dang gso ba sems can dang//dus dang gang zag nyid dang//dngos po kun gi rang bzhin 'di//sgyu ma'i gzugs yang dag gnas//dang po dga' ba tsam nyid de//gnyis pa'i grangs las
15b1(rgyud): mchog dga' nyid//gsum pa las ni dga' bral gshad//bzhi pa lhan cig skyes par brjod//rdo rje snying po la sogs kun//mkhas pas de skad de thos nas//mchog tu ngo mtshar gyur
15b2(rgyud): nas ni//rgyal zhing sa la 'gyel bar 'gyur//byang chub sems dpa' rnaMs kyis gsol pa//dang po dga' ba 'gro ba'i gzugs//de bzhin mchog dga' 'gro ba nyid//dga' bral dga'
15b3(rgyud): ba'i 'gro ba nyid//gsuM la lhan cig skyes pa med//bcom ldan bka' stsal kye rdo rje//sangs rgyas kun kyi sku cig pa//rdo rje snying pos rtogs bya'i phyir//the tsom legs par sel ba ni//'dod chag+sa
15b4(rgyud): tsha med cing chags bral med//dbu ma dmigs par mi 'gyuro//gsum po spangs pa nyid kyi phyir//lhan cig skyes pa byang chub mchog//yang na de bzhin thams cad bdag//yang na kun
15b5(rgyud): gyis rnam par spangs//de yang dga' bral dang por ma chen//dga' ba gsuM po rnam par spangs//dang por srin dang 'dra ba yin//grub pa sgyu ma lta bur 'gyur//nyal dang log pa'i
15b6(rgyud): khyad par las//rtag tu rmi lam 'dra bar 'gyur//dngos grub mtshan nyid mi phyed pa'i//phyag rgya rnal 'byor pa yis 'grub//ston pas dkyil 'khor 'di skad gsungs//grub bzhi yang dag 'bar ba
15b7(rgyud): nyid//sgo bzhi gzi brjid chen po ste//do shal do shal phyed pas brgyan//'phreng ba sna tshogs lnga yab ldan//bka' brgyad kyis nges par brgyan//rdo rje srad bu mnyam par ldan//me tog sna tshogs
15b8(rgyud): nye bar brgyan//bdug pa sar med bzhi ni dri//bum pa brgyad po la sogs par ldan//de yang yal ga la sogs brgyan//'grin par gos kyis kab pa dang //rin chen lnga ni yongasu bzhug+sa/rnam
16a1(rgyud): @#//par rgyal ba shar du sbyin//rang don lha'i gzugs kyis ni//sar pa legs par bkal ba dang //shin tu tshad ma mdzes pa yis//srad bus shes rab can gyis bdab;;
16a2(rgyud): 'khor lo'i bdag po'i bzlas pa'i bum//dkyil 'khor can gis de bzhin khri//gong du gsungs pa'i sngags nyid ni//mkhas pas nor 'dzin rnam par sbyang //gong gi a bsogs sngag+sa
16a3(rgyud): kyis ni//de la rtar ma rnam par sbyin//ci ltar bsam gtan de bzhin 'dir//bsrung ba ji ltar gsungs pa nyid//gang zhig dbang ni rab dbye ba//rang gi dkyil 'khor cho gas sbyin//mchod
16a4(rgyud): dang gsol ba btab pa nyid//ci ltar gshad pa de bzhin 'dir//'phar ma gnyis bzang rnam bris nas//myur du dkar mo la sogs bri//shar du dri khug laswogs bri//lho dang nub
16a5(rgyud): du de bzhin du //byang (dang)me'i mtshams dang ni//bden bral rlung du de bzhin no//dbang ldan du ni ci gsungs gang//de lta de ltar 'og dang steng //rdo rje sems dpa'i snyems byas

16a8(rgyud): pa yis//khyad par cung zad yod ma yin//mchog dga' sgom pa med cing bsgom pa po yang med//lus kyang med cing gzung med 'dzin pa po yang med//sha dang khrag kyang med cing

16b5(rgyud): ko la sbyar bas//reg pa sra ba'i chos kyis ni//sa ni de las skyes par 'gyur//byang sems khu ba'i rnam pa las//chu'i khams ni 'byung bar 'gyur//skyod pa las ni

17a1(rgyud): @#/ O /yar med Idkyil 'khor yang ni med//de ni sngags bzlas de dka' thub (  )medang sbyin sreg med//de ni dkyil 'khor can dang de ni dkyil 'khor nyid//mdor sdus nas ni 
17a2(rgyud): sems na sdus pa'i gzugs can no// O //dbang gi le'u zhes byas te bcu pa'o// O //mnyam par ma rungs pral ba can//rtag tu lhung bar byed par 'gyur//dbang gi lha

17b1(rgyud): de nas rtag//dga' bral dga' ba la smod pa//lan bdun pas ni grub par 'gyur//skad snyan mig dang ldan pa dang//dri lus gzi brjid chen po dang//de'i grib ma bdun du 'gyur//

17b7(rgyud): gshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba//ci gsungs sbyin sregs byas nas ni//dkyil 'khor dam pa gzhengs pa dang//snga par sku gzugs sbyar ba dang//sta gon

18a1(rgyud): //A+o~M badzra puSh+pe a hU~M swA hA//A+o~M badzra d+hu be a hU~M swA hA/A+o~M badzra a lo ke hU~M swA hA//o~M badzra g+han d+he a hU~M swA hA//A+o~M badzra ne pe te a hU~M swA hA/sna tshogs hU~M gi rdzogs pa dang /

19a3(rgyud): gnas//mtshams med lnga ni byed pa dang//srog chags gsod la dga' ba dang//gzhan yang skye ba dman pa dang//rmongs dang ma rungs las byed dang//gzugs ngan yan lag ma tshang ba/
