19b4(rgyud): ma spangs ba b+ho lar 'gyur//ka ko la dbus yang dag gnas//'gram gnyis dril bu nyid du 'gyur//zem 'bu b+ho la can du 'gyur//lhag ma bdag nyid chen po nyid//dga' chen he ru
19b5(rgyud): ka'i gzugs//he ru ka sbyor skyes bu nyid//'bad pa med par skyes bur 'gyur//gsal bar nus pa'i rnal 'byor pas//de phyir phyag rgya'i dngos grub 'gyur//'byung
19b6(rgyud): 'byung dang 'jigs pa dag gis kyang//thabs dang shes rab gnod mi 'gyur//gang phyir thabs ni 'byung ba nyid//'jig pa shes rab srid mthar byed//des na rab 'jigs 'od
19b7(rgyud): ma yin//de nyid las ni 'byung ba med//la la 'jigs pas 'jig par 'gyur//'jig pa'i dngos med zad pa med//bskyed pa'i rim pa'i rnal 'byor gyis//brtul shugs can
19b8(rgyud): gyis spros pa bsgom//spros pa rmi lam ltar byas nas//spros pa nyid ni spros med byed//ci ltar sgyu ma rmi lam dang//srid pa bar ma ji lta bur//rtag tu goms par byas pa las//dkyil
20a1(rgyud): @#//'khor nyid ni de bzhin 'dod//phyag rgya chen po'i dbang skur n+sa//ji ltar shes pa'i bde chen po//de'i 'di ni byin rlabs yin//gzhan las dkyil 'khor 'byung ba med//bde 
20a2(rgyud): ba gnag cing bde ba ser//bde ba dmar po bde ba dkar//bde ba ljang khu bde ba sngo//bde ba rgyu dang mi rgyu kun//bde ba shes rab bde ba thabs//de bzhin gnyis sbyor las skye bde//bde ba
20a3(rgyud): dngos po dngos med bde//rdo rje sems dpa' bde bar brjod//rdo rje snying pos gsol pa//rdzogs pa'i rim pa'i rnal 'byor 'di//de'i bde ba bde chen brjod//rdzogs pa sgom pa med pa ste//
20a4(rgyud): skyed pa yis ni ci zhig 'tshal//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//e ma byang chub sems dpa' che//dad pa'i shugs kyis rab tu nyams//lus kyi dngos po gang lags bde//bde
20a5(rgyud): bar smra bar mi nus so//khyad dang khyed byed tshul gyis ni//bde bas 'gro ba khyab par byed//ci ltar me tog la gnas dri//me tog dngos med shes mi 'gyur//de bzhin
20a6(rgyud): gzugs bsogs dngos med pas//bde ba nyid kyang dmigs mi 'gyur//dngos nga dngos po nga ma yin//dngos po rtogs phyir sangs rgyas nga//gang zhig le los snun pa dang//rmongs
20a7(rgyud): pa gang gis nga mi shes//rdo rje btsun mo 'ab+ha ga ni//e'i rnam pa'i cha byad gzugs//sangs rgyas rin za ma tog//rde ba can du rtag tu bzhugs//'chad pa po nga chos kyang ngo//rang rig chogs
20a8(rgyud): ldan nyan pa dang//'jig rten bston pa grub bya nga//'jig rten 'jig rten 'das pa nga//lhan cig skyes dga' rang bzhin nga//mchog dga' dga' bral dang por ni//mun pa la ni mar me ltar//
20b1(rgyud): de bzhin nus kyang yid ches kyis//btsun mo'i ba ga bden gnasu//ston pa sum bcu rtsa gnyis mtshan//gtso bo dpe' byad brgyad bcur ldan//khu ba zhes bya rnam par gnas//de med pas na bde med 'gyur//
20b2(rgyud): bde ba med na de med 'gyur//nus pa med phyir ltos dang bcas//lha'i rnal 'byor las bde ba//de phyir sangs rgyas dngos po min//de nyid dngos med tshul yang min//zhal phyag rnam pa gzugs can nyid//mchog
20b3(rgyud): tu bde bas gzugs med pas//de phyir 'gro kun lhan cig skyes//rang bzhin lhan cig skyes par 'dod//rnam par dag pa'i sems kyis ni//rang bzhin mya ngan 'das pa nyid//lha'i rnam pa'i gzugs kyis ni/
20b4(rgyud): bzhin lag kha dog gnas pa ni//skye ba tsam gis rnam par gnas//'on kyang bag chags phal pas so//dug gi dum bu gang nyid kyis//skye bo thams cad 'chi bar 'gyur//dug gi de nyid
20b5(rgyud): shes pa des//dug gis dug ni byin par byed//ci ltar rlung gis zin pa de//bza' bar sran mar sbyin par bya//bzlog pa'i sman ni rtags pa las//rlung gis rlung la snun par byed//rnam
20b6(rgyud): rtog gis ni rnam rtog dang //srid pa nyid kyi srid pa dag//ci ltar rnam par chu zhugs pa//chu gzhan kyis ni dbyung bar bya//de bzhin na dngos po'i rnam rtog kyang //rnam par nges par sbyang bar bya//
20b7(rgyud): ci ltar tshad pas tshig pa yang //me yis yang ni gdung bar bya//de bzhin 'dod chags mes tshig pa//'dod chags me yis gdung bar bya//skye bo mi 'azad pa'i las//gang dang gang ni 'ching 'gyur
20b8(rgyud): pa;;thabs dang bcas pa de nyid kyis//srid pa'i 'ching ba las grol 'gyur//chags pas 'jig rten 'ching 'gyur pa//'dod chags nyid kyis rnam grol 'gyur//bzlog pa'i bsgom pa 'di nyid ni;
21a1(rgyud): //sangs rgyas mu stegs de mi shes//'byung ba lnga'i rang bzhin las//dbang gnyis sbyor bas lnga ru 'gyur//dga' ba chen po gcig nyid las//dbye bas lnga ru 'gro bar 'gyur//
21a2(rgyud): bo la ka ko la sbyar bas//reg pas sra ba'i mtshan nyid de//sra ba gti mug ngo bo'i phyir//gti mug rnam par snang mdzad brjod//gang phyir byang sems gsher ba nyid//gsher ba chu'i khamasu brjod//
21a3(rgyud): chu ni mi skyod gzugs kyi phyir//zhe sdang 'dren pa mi skyod pa//gnyis kyi skyod pa'i sbyor ba las//drod ni rtag tu skye bar 'gyur//'dod chags dpag med rdo rje yin//'dod chags drod las 'byung bar
21a4(rgyud): 'gyur//ka ko lar ni gang zhig sems//de ni rlung gi rang bzhin sems//phra dog don yod grub pa yin//don yod rlung las 'byung ba yin//bde ba'i 'dod chags khrag du 'gyur//dga' ba
21a5(rgyud): nam mkha'i mtshan nyid de//nam mkha' ser sna rdo rjer 'gyur//ser sna nam mkha' las 'byung nyid//sems ni chen po gcig nyid la//rang bzhin lnga yis rnam par mtshon//rigs
21a6(rgyud): ni lnga po de nyid las//stong phrag rgya po de nyid las//de phyir 'di dag rang bzhin gcig//bde chen mchog du phan pa nyid//'dod chags la sogs seMs lnga yis//dbye bas lnga ru 'gro bar 'gyur//gang
21a7(rgyud): ga klung ba cu je snyed kyi//rigs gcig la ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i tshogs//rigs kyi tshogs la rigs ni du ma rnams//de rnaMs rigs la rigs ni rnam pa brgya//de rnaMs la 'ang 'bum phrag rigs chen
21a8(rgyud): rnaMs//bye ba'i rigs la grangs ni med par 'gyur//de yis rigs la grangs med rigs rnaMs nyid//mchog tu dga' ba'i rigs las 'byung ba rnams//kyai rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma dra ba sdom pa las//dngos
21b1(rgyud): gru ba brtan la phan phab pa'i////le'u zhes byaste gnyis pa'o////de nas rdo rje can gyi rnal 'byor ma rnaMs //rgyud thams cad kyi kleng bzhi zhes bya ba'i tha bas bka' stsal pa//sdom pa dang
21b2(rgyud): ni dbang dang yang//de bzhin gsang ba'i skad nyid dang//dga' dang skad cig dbye ba dang//gzhan yang bza' ba lasogs pa//de la sdom pa bka' stsal pa//sangs rgyas kun gi sdom pa yin//e
21b3(rgyud): baM rnam par rab tu gnas//e baM rnaM pa'i bde chen po//dbang las yang dag shes par 'gyur//de nas rnal 'byor ma rnaMs kyis/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' la 'di skad gsol to//
21b4(rgyud): bcom ldan ston pa 'gro ba'i bla//mkha' 'gro ma yi sdom pa nyid;e baM rnam pa ci zhes brjod//rim pa ji ltar gshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/e'i cha byad bzang po
21b5(rgyud): gang//dbusu baM gis rnam par rgyan//bde ba thaMs cad kyi ni gnas//sangs rgyas rin chen za ma tog//skad cig dbye bas che ba nyid//dga' bde nyid las skye bar 'gyur//skad
21b6(rgyud): cig shes nas bde shes pa//e baM rnaM par rab tu gnas//sna tshogs dang ni rnam smin dang//rnam nyid de bzhin mtshan nyid bral//skad cig bzhi ni rab shes pa//de ltar rnal 'byor pas
21b7(rgyud): shes par 'gyur//mkhyud dang 'o byed lasogs pa//sna tshogs rnam pa sna tshogs gshad//rnam par smin pa de las zlog//bde ba'i ye shes za ba nyid//bdag gi bde ba zos pa'o//
21b8(rgyud): gros ni rnam par nyed par brjod//mtshan nyid bral ba gsuM las gzhan//tshags dang chags dang bral rnam par spangs//sna tshogs dang po'i dga' ba nyid//rnam smin la ni mchog dga' nyid//
22a1(rgyud): //rnam rnyed dga' bral dga' ba nyid//mtshan bral lhan cig skyes dga' nyid//slob dpon gsang ba shes rab dang //bzhi po de yang de bzhin no//dbang ni bzhi'i grangs kyis ni//dga' ba
22a2(rgyud): la sogs rim shes bya//rgod pa dag la slob dpon nyid//lta ba gsang ba de bzhin no//lag bcad nas ni shes rab nyid//gnyis gnyis mkhyud la de 'ang nyid//seMs can rnaMs kyi dngos grub phyir//
22a3(rgyud): dbang ni rnam pa bzhi ru gshad//gtor dang klugs pa zhes bya nyid//des na dbang zhes brjod par bya//shes rab bcu drug lon pa la//lag pa dag gis yang dag 'khyud//rdo rje dril bu mnyam sbyor bas
22a4(rgyud): slob dpon dbang du rab du brjod//shin tu bzhin bzangs mig yangs ma//gzugs dang lang mtshos rnam par brgyan//the bo sri na lag dag gis ni//slob ma'i kha ru ltung bar bya//mnyaM
22a5(rgyud): pa'i ro de shes pa'i phyir//ston pas shes rab rab tu mchod//mchod par byas nas gtad par bya//ston pas smras pa seMs dpa' che//phyag rgya bde ba ldan pa khyer//khra dog khro ba
22a6(rgyud): rnam spangs pa'i//slob ma cher gyur shes nas ni//ston pas de la snang ba sbyin//dbang po gnyis sbyor rdo rje 'dzin//ji ltar dbang skur gsol gdab pa'i//slob ma'i bya ba gshad par bya
22a7(rgyud): bla ma phyag rgya ldan mthong nas//stod dang mchod pa ci bzhin bya//bcom ldan zhi ba chen po kye//rdo rje rnal 'byor gcig gzhed pa//mi phyed phyag rgya sgrub pa po//rdo rje rnal 'byor las 'byung
22a8(rgyud): ba//ci ltar khyod ni bdag nyid che//mnga' bdag bdag la 'ang de ltar mdzod//'khor ba dam gi tshogs dag du//bying ba bdag med bdag la skyobs//zhim pa'i za ba'i dung ba dang//chang
22b1(rgyud): dang sha chen mchog dag dang//bdug pa lha gshos 'phreng ba dang//dril bu rgyal mtshan byug pa yis//'di rnams mchod pa rnams kyis ni//slob mas rdo rje 'dzin pa bachod//mchog tu
22b2(rgyud): dga' ba yang dag thob//sna tshogs spangs pa'i skad cig la//ston pas smras pa sems dpa' che//bde ba chen po rnam par bzung//ji srid byang chub bar du ni//seMs can don gi rdo rje 'dzin
22b3(rgyud): slob ma snying rje can mthong nas//rdo rje can gyis 'di skad smras//'di nyid ye shes chen po nyid//thams cad 'dus la rnam par gnas//gnyis dang gnyisu med pa'i tshul//dngos dang dngos
22b4(rgyud): med bdag nyid gtso//gtan dang g.yo ba khyab nas gnas//sgyu ma'i gzugs can nyid du 'dod//dkyil 'khor 'khor lo'i thabs kyis ni//rtag tu nges par 'gro bar 'gyur//de nas rdo rje snying pos
22b5(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma rnaMs la bzod par gsol nas//bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//dkyil 'khor 'khor lo ci zhes brjod//sangs rgyas kun bdag grong khyer la//bcom ldan
22b6(rgyud): bdag ni 'khrul 'gyur las//rim pa ji ltar gshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//dkyil 'khor snying po zhes brjod do//byang chub seMs ni bde chen po//len cing
22b7(rgyud): 'dzin par byed pa'i phyir//'dus pa dkyil khor nyid du brjod//'khor lo zhes gnas nam mkha'i khaMs//yul la sogs pa rnam par sbyong//po la ka ko la sbyor bas//de'i bde ba shes par 'gyur//
22b8(rgyud): rdo rje snying pos gsol pa//sdom pa zhes bya gang gis dang//dam tshig gis ni gnas par bgyi//bcom ldan 'das kyis  bka' stsal pa//khyod kyis srog chags gsad pa dang//rdzun gi tshig kyang smra ba
23a1(rgyud): @#//dang /khyod kyis ma sbyin bar yang blangs//pha rol bud med sten par gis//sems gcig srog chags gsod pa nyid//gang phyir srog ni semasu brjod//'jig rten sgral ba zhes bya
23a2(rgyud): ba//rdzun gi tshig du rab tu grags//btsun mo'i khu ba ma byin bar//gzhan gi bud med rang 'tshung+sa 'dzas//de nas rnal 'byor ma thams cad kyis/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//
23a3(rgyud): yum de rnaMs yang ci logs//yudabang po rnams ni gang lags/skye mched rnaMs ni gang lags/phung po rnaMs ni gang lags/khams rnaMs de yang ci lags/'di rnams kyi rang bzhin ni ci lag+sa 
23a4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//yul drug ni gzugs dang sgra dang dri dang ni//ro dang de bzhin reg bya nyid//chos kyi khaMs kyi ranga bzhin nyid//'di rnaMs yul rnaMs drug du brjod//dbang po
23a5(rgyud): rnaMs ni 'di drug ste//mig dang rna ba sna dang ni//lce dang lus dang de bzhin yid//'di ni dbang po drug po ni//gti mug rdza sogs lnga na//yul dang yul can dag gis ni//skye mched bcu gnyis
23a6(rgyud): dag tu 'gyur//phung po lnga ni gzugs lasogs mthar ni rnaM shes rdo rje che//dbang po yi ni yul nyid dang //dbang po rnam par shes pa nyid//rnal 'byor ma rnaMs go cha'i phyar//khams ni bco brgyad dag tu brjod//
23a7(rgyud): rang bzhin gdod nas ma skyes pa//rdzun min bden min de bzhin du//thams cad chu'i zla ba bzhin//rnal 'byor mas ni 'dod shes gyis//'di lta ste btsub shing dang /btsub bstan dang /skyes bu'i
23a8(rgyud): lag pa'i rtsol ba las/blo bur du me skye bar 'gyur/me ni btsub shing dang /btsubabastan dang /skyes bu'i lag pa'i rtsol ba las/blo  bur du me skye bar 'gyur /me de ni gtsub la yang mi gnas/
23b1(rgyud): gtsub bstan la yang mi gnas/skyes bu'i lag pa dag la yang ma yin te/rnam pa thaMs du yongasu btsal na/gcig la yang yod pa ma yin pas/me de la yang bde ba yang ma yin/brdzun pa yang ma
23b2(rgyud): yin no//de ltar chos thams cad rnal 'byor ma'i yid la gyis shig/de nas rnal 'byor ma bdag med ma lasogs pa/rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma thaMs cad bdud rtsi lnga thogs shing/dam tshig gi rdzas thogs
23b3(rgyud): pa;bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' la mchod pa byed/dbang po gnyis sbyor bas rjesu chags par byed/bdud rtsi lnga'i ro yang mthung bar byed do//de nas bcom ldan 'das dges nas/
23b4(rgyud): rang byin gyis rlab pa stan pa/kye kye rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma//sangs rgyas kun gyis phyag mdzad ma'i//de nyid bdag gis gsangs mdzad pa'i//rdo rje mchod pa'i byin brlabs kyis//gshad pas 'dod
23b5(rgyud): pas rab tu nyon//lha mo thams cad dga' bar 'gyur//rangs par gyur te pus mo g.yas pa'i lhang nge sa la btsugs nas/bcoM ldan 'das ga la ba der thal mo sbyar ba btud nas;
23b6(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyi gsung nyan to//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/bza' btung ji ltar rnyed pa dang//'gro dang mi 'agro mi slong zhing//khrus dang gartsang sgra mi bya ste//grong
23b7(rgyud): gi chos ni rang tu yang//blo ldan sngags nyid mi zla zhing//bsam gtan nyid ni dmigs mi bya//gnyid ni spang bar mi bya ste//dbang po rnaMs ni mi dgag go//b+ha la thaMs cad gza' bar bya//kha 
23b8(rgyud): dog lnga la mnyam par spyad//dogs pa med pa'i sems kyis ni//btsun mo thams cad dga' bar byed//mdza' bo sdug par mi bya zhing//de bzhin sdug la zhe sdang nyid//rngo shing 'jim pa'i
24a1(rgyud): @#//bdag nyid kyi༐ lha rnaMs la ni phyag mi bya//g.yung mo bdol pa ko lpags mkhan//phyag dar mkhan swogs rag ring dang//bram ze rje'u rmangs rigs rnaMs//bdag gi lus ltar reg pa nyid༐
24a2(rgyud): bdud rtsi lnga dang bu ram chang//dug dang nim pa bu cas skyes//skyur dang dngar dang ska lasogs//kha ba lan tsa tsha ba dang//dri nga dri zhim mchi brnag dang//byang chub sems kyis rab tu bza'//gnyis med ye
24a3(rgyud): shes seMs kyis gyis ni//mi za cung zad yod ma yin//rang byung me rtog rnyed nas ni//pad ma'i snod du gzhag pa dang//lud pa dang ni snabs dag gis//sros nas gartul zhugs can gyis btung//smad g.yog+sa
24a4(rgyud): kha dog sna tshogs dang//ro sreg thal bas de bzhin brgyan//me tog yi dgas gnas rnyed pas//mgo skyes legs par bcing bar bya//de nas rdo rje snying pos gsol pa//thaMs cad gyi (ma)rnam dag pa//bcom ldan
24a5(rgyud): 'das kyis sngar gsungs na//nges par dbang po drug gi grangs༐༐ /rnam par dag pa ma mdzad pa//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa༐༐ mig la gti mug rdo rje mo//rna bar zhe sdang rdo rje ma༐༐
24a6(rgyud): sna la ser sna rdo rje ma//kha la 'dod chags rdo rje ma//lus la 'phra dog rdo rje ma//yid (ma)bdag med rnal 'byor ma//dbang po rnaMs ni dag pa'i phyir//'dis ni go cha sems dpa' che//rdo rje snying
24a7(rgyud): pos (yang)gsol pa//gsang ba'i skad ni ci zhes bgyi//rnal 'byor ma'i dam tshig che//nyan thos lasogs mi shes pa//rgod dang dga' ba dag gis dang//'khyud dang de bzhin gnyis gnyis dang༐༐
24a8(rgyud): rgyud dang rnam pa bzhi rnaMs kyis//dgongs pa'i skad ni mi sgrags pa//bcom ldan nges par gsung du gsol//dgongs pa'i skad ni skad chen po//dam tshig rda ni rgyas par ru//rdo rje snying po ngas 
24b1(rgyud): gshad kyis//khyod ni rtse gcig yid kyis nyon//ma tha na chang b+ha la sha//'du ba ma la dza zhes brjod//'gro ba khe ta ro ni sha//rus pa'i brgyan ni ni ra shu//'ong ba phre kha nang du brjod//cad de'u khri
24b2(rgyud): ba'i ta ru brjod//bskal med du rdu ram zhes brjod//skal ldan gi li ny+dza raM zhes mang brjod//reg min Ti na Ti mang brjod//thod pa pad ma b+ha dza rnaM//bza' ba tri pi na zhes brjod//tshod ma ma la ti ni ta 
24b3(rgyud): nam//shaM pa tsa ru sa maM brjod//tsi ba ka du re zhes brjod//rang 'byung si te shes par bya//khu ba ka pu ru zhes brjod//sha chen sha li dzam zhes brjod//dbang po gnyis sbyor kun tu ru zhes//
24b4(rgyud): rdo rje b+ho la zhes brjod//pad ma ga ko la zhes brjod//kha dog dbye bas tshe phye ba las//rigs ni rnam pa lnga ru bshad//'di ni gsang ba'i skad yin te//phyag rgya lnga ni sdus pa nyid//g.yung
24b5(rgyud): mo rdo rje'i rigasu bshad//gar ma de bzhin pad ma'i rigs//mchod ma ma ni rin chen rigs//gnyis bskyes de bzhin gshes par brjod//chos ma las kyi rigs nyid de//phyag
24b6(rgyud): rgya 'di rnaMs dngos grub sbyin//'di rnaMs khu ba rdo rjer 'gyur//brtul shugs can gyis mchod nas btung //rdo rje snying po sems dpa' che//gsang ba'i skad ni 'tshar che bas//nga yis 
24b7(rgyud): khyod la gang gshad pa//de rnams thaMs cad gus pas bzung //kyai rdo rje 'di ni dbang skur gang //gsang ba'i skad kyis mi smra bar//'di'i dam tshig nyaMs par ni//'gyur bar 'di la the tsom med;;
24b8(rgyud): mtsho ba dang ni rkun ma dang //bdon dang dug dang rims kyis kyang //gal te sangs rgyas gsang bI skad//mi gsung na yang 'grongs par 'gyur//rang gi daM tshig rigs rnyed na//galate tshig 'di mi smra na//gnas bzhi
25a1(rgyud): @#//las skyes rnal 'byor ma//de'i tshe na 'gro bar byed//kyai rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma 'dra ba'i sdom pa las/rgyud thams cad kyi kleng bzhi dang//gsang ba'i skad ces bya ba'i le'u ste gsum pa'o//
25a2(rgyud): de nas rdo rje snying po lasogs pa rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma thams cad the tsom du gyur//yid gnyisu gyur te ba tsa ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' la 'di skad ces gsolato//kye ba c-i ldan 'das the tsom gsal du gsol;
25a3(rgyud): spyod pa'i le'u las gang gshad pa//klu dang gar ni dngos grub sbyin//de la bdag ni the tsom mchis//klu ni ci lags gar ni ci//lha dbang skur ba'i le'u gang gsungs pa//bdag med la swogs sa bon nyid//de la
25a4(rgyud): bdag ni 'khrul pa 'khungs//gang gis sa bon sa bon gang//rigs kyi le'u las gang bshad pa//bcu drug gnyis kyi bdag nyid rtso//bco ldan bdag ni 'khrul gyur pas//de rnaMs rnaM dag gshad du gsol//bco ldan 'das kyis
25a5(rgyud): bka' stsal pa//ko la i ri TI a po la//mu mu ni re ka k+ko lA g+ha na ko pa TA Tho badzra i;ku ru ni gi a i na ro la ni ta hi pa la khA dzA hi/ge TaM ma A nAdz+dza a i/ta le ka li ny+dza ra sa ni a i/d+hu d+hu ra dz+dza
25a6(rgyud): a i tsa u sa ma ka tsu ri si hA/si hA ka bu spu ri lA i a i mA la/i in d+ha Na sa li dz+dza;ta hi b+ha ru kha bi a i/phra kha Na khe Ta ka ra n+te/a shu d+hA a shu d+hA na ma n+ni a i/ni rang su a rga tsa to wi;
25a7(rgyud): ta hi dza sa ra pa wi sa ni a i/ma la a dze kun d+hu ru ba T+wa a i/Ti T+Ti ma d+ha hi na badzra a i////dran pa dang ni mi 'phrogs rnal 'byor pas//he ru ka'i gzugs kyis kang//chags bral
25a8(rgyud): min goms sems kyis ni//'dod chags sems kyis bsgom pa nyid//rdo rje chos dang sangs rgyas dang//rnal 'byor ma dang ma mo yis//klu dang gar ni 'di dag gis//legs par klu bklang
25b1(rgyud): gar kyang bya//'di nyid kyis ni phyogs srung zhing//de bzhin bdag kyang bsrung ba nyid//'dis ni 'jig rten dbang du byed//'dis ni sngags kyi zlas pa nyid//gang du gus dang klu len dang//gang du gus byas
25b2(rgyud): gar byed dang//tshogs kyi bdag por gang byas nas//de la dri ni mtshon par bya//sgog pa'i dri ni dang po nyid//de nas yang ni bya rgod dri//ga bur ma la ya tsa nyid//ngang pa dang ni bung pa'i sgra//klu'i mtha' nas nyan par
25b3(rgyud): bya|ba lang na tso'i sgra yang ni//phyi rol tu ni mtshon bya ba//klu'i byin rlabs/mtshan nyid do//phyag rgya rtags dang mtshan ma ste//'dis ni rigs ni mtshon par bya//rigs 'chos sgoM pa'i sbyor ba las//dngos grub med cing sgrub
25b4(rgyud): po med//zhe sdang phyag rgyas bdag med ma//gti mug phyag rgyas rdo rje ma||ser sna rdo rjes dkar mo nyid//phyags pa chu ma phyag rgyas bdab//phra dog phyag rgyas mkha' 'gro ma//zhe sdang phyag rgyas pu ka si//gti mug
25b5(rgyud): phyag rgyas ri khrod ma//ser sna phyag rgyas gdol pa mo//'dod chags phyag rgyas g.yung mo nyid//yang ni dkar mo zhe sdang las//gti mug phyag rgyas chom rkun ma//ser sna'i phyag rgyas ro
25b6(rgyud): langs ma//'dod chags phyag rgyas g+ha ma ri//gti mug phyag rgyas sa spyod ma//'dod chags phyag rgya mkha' spyod ma//'dod pas phyag rgya shes par bya//a li dang po bdag med ma//a li
25b7(rgyud): gnyis pa rdo rje ma//a li gsuM pa dkar mo nyid//bzhi pa chu'i rnal 'byor ma//lnga pa rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma//drug pa pu ka si zhes brjod//bdun pa nyid ni ri khrod ma//brgyad pa sme sha can du brjod//dgu pa yang ni
25b8(rgyud): g.yung mo nyid//yang na lnga gnyis dkar mo nyid//bcu gcig chom rkun mar ru brjod//bcu gnyis ro langs ma zhes brjod//bcu gsuM pa ni g+ha sma ri//bcu bzhi pa ni sa spyod ma//bco lnga pa ni mkha' spyod ma//
26a1(rgyud): @#/ O /rnal 'byor ma'i sa bon nyid//rigs kyi le'u las rtsa rnams gang//bcu drug gnyis kyi bdag nyid gsungs//rtsa ni gnyis gnyis rnal 'byor ma//re re rnam pa gsum du brjod ࿒
26a2(rgyud): brkyang ma ro ma kun 'dar ma//bdag med rnal 'byor ma zhes brjod//gang phyir bcu drug cha med pas//'bad pas kun gyi lhag ma spang//de ci'i slad du zhe na/don kyi bya ba mi byed phyir//bco lnga'i
26a3(rgyud): cha'i bdag nyid kyi//zla ba byang chub sems su 'gyur//bde ba chen po a li'i gzugs//rnal 'byor ma rnaMs de'i cha//rdo rje snying pos (yang)gsol pa//rnal 'byor ma kun las byung ba//lhan cig skyes
26a4(rgyud): dga'i rang bzhin nyid//'grib med btung mchog nam mkha' nyid//ga bur nyis par cis ma spangs//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa (yang)࿒ci skad smra pa de bzhin no//rdo rje snying pos (yang )gsol pa/thabs
26a5(rgyud): gang gis byang chub kyi sems bskyed pa lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'-i stsal pa/dkyil 'khor 'khor lo'i thabs (dang ni//rang byin rlabs pa'i rim pas kyang//kun rdzob
26a6(rgyud): don dam gzugs can gyi//byang chub sems ni rab tu bskyed//kun rdzob kun d+ha lta bu nyid//don dam bden pa'i gzugs can no//bud med ko la bde ba can//e baM rnam pa'i rang bzhin du|bde ba
26a7(rgyud): bsrung ba nyid kyi phyir//bde ba can zhes rab tu grags //sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang//rdo rje 'dzin pa rnaMs kyi gnas//'di nyid 'khor ba zhes bya ste//'di nyid mya ngan 'das pa nyid|'khor
26a8(rgyud): ba spangs nas gzhan du ni//mya ngan 'das par rtog mi 'gyur//'khor ba gzugs dang sgra la swogs//'khor ba tshor laswogs pa//'khor ba dbang po rnam nyid de//'khor ba zhe sdang lasogs pa|
26b1(rgyud): 'di rnams chos ni mya ngan 'das//rmongs phyir 'khor ba'i gzugs can nyid//rmongs med 'khor ba dag pas ni//'khor ba mya ngan 'das par 'gyur//byang chub seMs ni mya ngan 'das//kun rdzob
26b2(rgyud): don dam tshul can no//shin du bzhin bzangs ming yang ma//gzugs dang lang 'tshos rnam par brgyan//sngo bsangs dal la rigs bzangs ma//si ga la bu 'byung ba'i gnas//des ni chang yang btung
26b3(rgyud): bar bya//de nas rang yang btung ba nyid//rang gzhan don ni rab sgrub phyir//phyi nas phyag rgya rjes chags bya//bo la ka ko lar bcug nas//dbang po gnyis sbyor brtul shugs can//der ni sbyor las
26b4(rgyud): byung ba//kha phur mkhas pas mi spangs te//de las lag tu mi blang zhing//nya phyis rdung chos nyid du min;;nges par blo dang sdoms kyi phyir//mi 'chi lce 'is blang ba nyid//ga phur nyid ni bdag med ma;;bde ba
26b5(rgyud): bdag med tshul can nyid;;de'i bde ba phyag rgya che//lte ba'i dkyil 'khor nyid du gnas//dang po'i dbyangs yig rang bzhin ste//blo zhes sangs rgyas rnaMs kyis brtags;;rdzogs pa'i
26b6(rgyud): rim pa'i rnal 'byor las//de nyid bcom ldan shes rab ma;;de ni ring min thung ba min//grub bzhi ma yin zlum po min//dri dang ro dang mya ngan 'das//lhan cig skyes dga' byed pa
26b7(rgyud): can;;de las skyes pa'i rnal 'byor pa//de'i bde ba za ba nyid//de dang lhan cig phyag rgya che//bde ba sbyin pa'i dngos grub 'gyur//gzugs sgra de bzhin dri dang ni//ro dang de bzhin rag bya nyid;;chos
26b8(rgyud): kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin yang//shes rab nyid kyis spyad par bya//de nyid lhan cig skyes pa'i gzugs//bde chen bzang po'i rnal 'byor ma//de nyid dkyil 'khor 'khor lo dang//ye shes lnga'i
27a1(rgyud): //rang bzhin can//de ni me long ye shes gzugs//mnyam nyid ye shes dngos po can//yang dag gyur ba so sor rtog//de nyid bya ba nan tan te//chos dbyings shin du rnam par dag//
27a2(rgyud): de nyid dkyil 'khor bdag po nga//de nyid bdag med rnal 'byor ma//chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin can//rdo rje snying pos gsol pa//'khor lo sgom pa'i lam dang ni//lha rnams ji ltar 'byung ba
27a3(rgyud): thaMs cad bcom ldan 'das gsungs na//sdom pa 'ang bdag la bshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//rnal 'byor ma lus dbus nas su//a' rnam pa'i sdom pa gnas
27a4(rgyud): ci ltar phyi rol de bzhin na//sdom pa de bzhin rab tu dbye//bo la'i bde ba phyag rgya che//rdo rje skye mched thams cad nyid//'dis ni gsang ba'i snyoms 'jug pas//phyi rol gnyis
27a5(rgyud): gnyis bstan pa med//sku gsum lus kyi nang du ni//'khor lo'i gzugs kyis brjod par bya//sku gsung yongasu shes pas ni//bde ba chen po'i 'khor lor brjod//chos dang long spyod
27a6(rgyud): sprul pa dang//de bzhin du 'ang bde chen nyid//skye gnas snying mgrin mgo bor yang//sku gsung du po ni rnam par gnas//ma lus pa'i seMs kyis su//skye ba gang la brjod bya ba//gang phyir sprul pa
27a7(rgyud): gnas btan phyir//de la sprul pa'i sku nyid yin//gang gi skyed sprul byed dang//sku ni sprul pa can zhes brjod//sems ni chos kyi rang bzhin te//chos kyi sku ni snying khar 'gyur//ro rnaMs drug gi
27a8(rgyud): gzugs can kyi//bza ba seMs par long spyod brjod//mgrin par long spyod 'khor lo dang//spyi bor bde ba chen po gnas//e baM rnam pa du mthun nyid//rnam smin chos kyi 'khor lo las//long spyod skyes bu
27b1(rgyud): byed pa nyid //bde ba'i 'khor la byed pa nyid//rgyu mthun bswogs pas rab phye bas//'bras bu rnam pa bzhi zhes brjod//las kyi rlung gis bskul ba'i//las la bcom ldan shes rab brjod//ji ltar
27b2(rgyud): byas pa de bzhin spyod//rgyu mthun zhes ni rab tu grags//las chud 'bras bu che ba'i phyir //rnam par smin pa de las bzlog//rnal 'byor dag phyir dri med nyid //skyed pa bskyes bu med pa nyid//gang phyir
27b3(rgyud): sprul pa gnas pa'i phyir//sprul pa'i 'khor lo gnas brtan yin//chos ni smra bas byung ba'i phyir//chos kyi 'khor lo thams cad yod//gang phyir 'grin par kun rig phyir//long spyod 'khor
27b4(rgyud): lor kun kur nyid//gang phyir bde chen mgor gnas pas//bde ba'i 'khor lor dge 'dun che//sde pa zhes bya sku ru brjod//lto ba gnas su brjod par bya//skye gnas 'dod chags bral bar 'gyur;
27b5(rgyud): mngal gi khru ma chos gos nyid//de bzhin ma yin mkhan po nyid//mgo bor thal mo sbyar ba phyag//'gro ba'i bya ba khrims kyi gnas //sngags kyi bya ba a dang haM//skye gnas 'khor lo'i '-i rna ba a
27b6(rgyud): bde chen gi yang rnam pa haM//lcer bu skra dang kha spu gregs//sngags don skyes pa dge slong nyid//'di dag brkyen kyis sems can rnaMs//sangs rgyas nyid du the tsom med//zla ba bcu yang sa
27b7(rgyud): rnaMs nyid//sems can sa bcu'i dbang phyug go//de nas rnal 'byor ma bdag med ma logs pa'i lha mo rnams la 'di lta ste//sman ma dang ma ma ki dang/gos kar mo dang/sgrol ma dang/khro nyer can dang/btsun
27b8(rgyud): d+ha dang/pa rna ri khrod ma dang/mgo zhal ma dang/de dag la swogs pa ri rabs kyi rdul phra rabs la sogs pa dang/mnyam pa'i rnal 'byor ma rnaMs 'di skad thos nas/mchog tu ngo mtshar bar gyur cing;brgyal ba dang
28a1(rgyud): @#/ O /bskrag pa dang/sa la 'gyel ba dang/'dar bar 'gyur ro//yang rdo rje can gyis lha mo de rnams thams cad gzigs nas/bslong ba'i phyir yang dag par ston pa dang/sa dang chu rlung byin za'i༐
28a2(rgyud): lha mo snod can khyod nyon la /gang zhig sus kyang mi shes pa/de nyid nga yis spro yis nyon//bcom ldan 'das kyis gsung rmi lam lta bur nye bar thos nas//de rnams thaMs cad srog rnyed par 'gyur te//bcom
28a3(rgyud): ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//sems can rnaMs ni sangs rgyas nyid//'on kyang blu bur dri mas bsgribs//de nyid gsal nas sangs rgyas nyid//lha mos gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin ste|༐
28a4(rgyud): bden (pa)ma mchis shing rdzun pa 'ang ma mchis so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal(   )pa//gang zhig mi shes 'jig rten pa//tsa na dug zos pas rgyas par 'gyur//rmongs spangs de nyid
28a5(rgyud): yid kyis ni//de'i de nyid yongasu spyod//de bzhin zhi ba'i thabs shes shing //kye rdo rje 'angal sos na//ma rig bsogs pa mi 'dzin cing//gti mug lasogs bcings pas min|༐
28a6(rgyud): rang dang rang gis rtogs pa las//sangs rgyas ma yin sems can ni//gcig kyang yod pa ma yin no//dmyal ba yi dgas byol song dang//lha dang mi min mi rnaMs dang//gshang ba'i srin bu lasogs pa//lha
28a7(rgyud): dang lha ma yin kyis yang༐ ༐bde ba gang phyir mi shes pas//rtag tu rang bzhin bde ba can//'jig rten khaMs ni gang du yang //sangs rgyas gzhan du rnyed mi 'gyur//sems ni rdzogs pa'i sangs
28a8(rgyud): rgyas nyid༐ ༐sangs rgyas gzhan du bstan du med//gdol pa smigs mkhan lasogs pa//gsod don zhu thub sems tshol dang //dri'i don du sems dpa'o//de rnaMs kyi rdo rje rab shes nas//'grub 'gyur
28b1(rgyud): 'di la the tsom med;;byis pa mi shes pas sgribs pa//'di'i 'gro ba mi shes pa //'gro ba drug gis srid pa'i mthar //gang zhig rmongs pa 'khor bar 'gyur//rdo rje snying po rdo rje che//kye'i rdo rje'i thabs rnyed
28b2(rgyud): nas;;gang zhig yul rnaMs rnam sbyong ba//des na bla ma brnyed par 'gyur//rdo rje snying pos (yang)gsol pa/sa ni pu ka sir bshad pa//gang phyir gti mug sra ba nyid//sku ni rnam snang nyid gsungs pa//ci ltar mi
28b3(rgyud): skyod phyag rgya lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//lus spangs (sa)ni sems kyis ni//mdzes pa gzhan du mi 'gyuro //de phyir rnam par sdan mdzad sems//sku la thugs kyi phyag rgya btab//rdo rje snying
28b4(rgyud): gsol pa/chu khams ri khrod mar bshad pa//mi skyod khu bas gzugs nyid//gtso bo mi skyod phyag rgyas ni//ri khrod ma la phyag rgyas rigs//bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' stsal pa/sems spangs nas ni lus
28b5(rgyud): kyis ni;;gnas pa bzhin du mthong ba mi 'gyur//de phyir sems ni gti mug 'gyur//thugs la gti mug phyag rgyas btab//rdo rje snying pos (yang)gsol(yang)//me ni gdol pa mor bshad pa;;
28b6(rgyud): 'dod chags phyag rgya gardol pa mo//rigs ni phyag rgya gzhan min ni;;ji ltar rin chen phyag rgya lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal( )pa//gang phyir 'dod chags khrag bshad pa;;
28b7(rgyud): khrag ni rin chen 'byung gnas nyid//drod ni khrag gi rang bzhin phyir//'dod chags ser snas phyag rgyas btab//rdo rje snying pos (yang)gsol pa//gang phyir g.yung mo rlung nyid de//don yod rlung ni gzugs can
28b8(rgyud): nyid;;don yod phyag rgyas g.yung mo la//gtso bo phyag rgyas rigs par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//'dod chags ma rtogs 'phra dog ni;;gzhan du 'byung ba ma yin te//de phyir
29a1(rgyud): //'dod chags phyag rgya yis//mkhas pas g.yung mo phyag rgyas btab//gang phyir gzugs ni sra ba nyid//dkar mo rnam snang mdzad gsungs pa//sngar gsungs nyid kyi rim pa yis
29a2(rgyud): sems kyi rim bdag pos phyag rgyas btab//de nyid rim pas chom rkun ma//de nyid ro langs ma nyid dang//de'i rim pas g+ha ma ri//ma log pa las phyag rgya'o//kyai rdo rje rdo rje 'dzin//snyoMs 'jug
29a3(rgyud): gnas pa'i lha nyid las//sems can don phyir gtor ma che//de la bdag med mas zhus pa//e baM rnam par zhugs nas ni//bgegs dang log par 'dren pa las//seMs can rnaMs kyi srog srung
29a4(rgyud): phyir;;rdo rje sems dpas gtor ma bstan//oM i d+ha dza ma dza la yak+sha/b+hu dang p+ti tsa yu rak+sha/tsa na dangs su dza ma/da pa pa ta pa tA laM/a Ta sa p+pa swa tA/A+e daM pa laM m+b+hu ny+dza dzaM g+ha phu l+la du ba maM sa b+hi g+haM
29a5(rgyud): a m+ha sarba ka dza sa rta kha n+he khu ne phe Ta ga ta/A+oM a ka ro mu kham sarba d+har+m+ma ni/a r+ya nud pan nad to/A+oM a hUM swAhA//gal te gtor ma 'dis 'byung po thams cad mchod par byed na/
29a6(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma rnams legs par 'gyuro//de rnams bde ba dri ma med pa dang//lha mo rnams dang 'gro ba rab tu 'byor pa dga' bar 'gyur ro//gal te 'dir 'byung po'i tshogs kyi phyir//rtag tu gtor
29a7(rgyud): ma byin na dbang dang mngon spyod dang//dga'i sde 'jag pa dang/bskrad pa dang/gsad pa dang/dgug pa dang/zhi ba dang/rgyas par yang 'gyur ro//rdo rje snying pos gsol pa//phyag rgya gang gis mkha' spyod ma//
29a8(rgyud): gang gi phyag rgyas sa spyod ma//gtso bo bdag gis sngar ma 'tshal//bcom ldan ci ltar phyag rgya bgyi//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//gsang gsum 'khor lo'i dbus su ni//sku gsung thugs su
29b1(rgyud): phye ba las//steng dang 'og dang dbus gnas kyi//'khor lo'i dbus su rnam par gnas//sa spyod sku'i phyag rgya can//'og zhal sku'i rdo rje ma//mkha' spyod 'dod chags phyag rgya can//steng zhal gsung gyi rdo rje ma//
29b2(rgyud): thugs kyi rang bzhin bdag med ma//sems ni bdag med tshul can nyid//seMs ni dbus kyi gnasu ste//des ni bdag med ma dbus skye//rgyas par rab tu phye ba las//rigs ni rnam pa drug du brjod//
29b3(rgyud): rnam gsum rnaM pa lnga nyid du//gshad kyis rnal 'byor ma nyid nyon//mi skyod rnam snang rin chen 'byung//dpag med phra dog rdo rje sems//zhe sdang gti mug ser sna dang//'dod chags 'phra dog bde ba dag
29b4(rgyud): 'dis ni rim pas bsgom pa nyid//rdo rje seMs dpa' spangs nas ni//spyi nas rigs ni rnam pa lnga//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug gis//de rjes rnam pa gsum du 'gyur//mi skyod zhe sdang gzug can
29b5(rgyud): nyid//thugs kyi bdag po rigs cig nyid//zhe sdang rdo rje'i byin rlabs 'di//rigs ni drug dang lnga ru brjod//kye rdo rje las rgyud thams cad kyi phyag rgya sdus pa'i don zhes bya ba'i
29b6(rgyud): le'u ste bzhi pa'o////phyag ni bcu drug zhal brgyad po ////zhabs bzhi dang ni 'jigs pa po//dpa' bo thod pa'i 'phreng ba can//bdag med ma nga 'gul nas 'khyud//
29b7(rgyud): phyag rgya lnga ni 'dzin lha ma//bdag med ma ni nyid kyis zhus//bco lngas yongasu bskor ba yi//bdag gi 'khor lo khyod gshad na//khyod ni 'khor lo ji ltar lags//gtso bo bdag gis
29b8(rgyud): sngar ma 'tshal//de nas dges dor rdo rje 'dzin//rgyal po snang ba thams cad kyi//ngo bo bdag nyid de dag kun//dkyil 'khor yang dag rab gshad pa//bdag med ma la 'od mdzad nas//
30a1(rgyud): //rdo rje thod par stsal nas ni//lha'i bu mo mnyes mdzad de//dkyil 'khor yang dag rab tu dbye//'khor lo ci ltar sngar gsungs pa//do shal do shal phyed pas mdzan//mtshas
30a2(rgyud): bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//rdo rje srad bus yang dag brgyan//'dod chags chen po rjes chags p+sa//lhan cig skyes dga'i rang bzhin las//khyod dang lhan cig bzhin bzangs ma//de'i dbus
30a3(rgyud): na nga yod de;;zhal brgyad pa la zhabs bzhi pa//phyag ni bcu drug dag gis brgyan//bdud bzhi nyams par gnan pa nyid//'jigs pa 'di ni 'jigs pa po//'go bo'i 'phreng ba'i do zhal
30a4(rgyud): mdzad;;nyi mar bzhugs shing rol mor bcas//kha dog nag ci po 'jigs pa po'i//sna tshogs rdo rje spyi bor 'dzin//nyid kyi zhal nas hUM spro zhing//thal bas lus la byug pa nyid;;
30a5(rgyud): bdag med lhan cig mnyam sbyor zhing//dga' ba gnyis sbyor snyoms 'jug pas//dpa' rlangs med pa'i bde ba thob//rlom sems med pa'i rang bzhin can//rtsa ba'i zhal
30a6(rgyud): ni nag po che//g.yas pa la ni kun d+ha mtshungs//g.yon pa dmar zhing cher 'jigs pa//spyi bo'i zhal ni brtsigs pa can//spyan ni nyi shu rtsa bzhis phye//lhag pa'i zhal ni bung pa bzhin;;grong
30a7(rgyud): khyer nyams dga' khyod dang nga //dga' bas shin du rol ba las//dkar mo dbang po'i phyogs byung nas//shar kyi sgo ru yang dag gnas//bsrub dang bsrub pa'i sbyor ba las//chom rkun ma yang
30a8(rgyud): phyung nas ni//phyung nas lho'i sgo ru ni//chom rkun mas ni sgo bsrungs ma//b+ho la kak+ko la sbyor bas//ro langs ma yang phyung nas ni//phyung nasanub kyi sgo ru ni;;bdud 'joMs ma ni rnam par gan+sa
30b1(rgyud): dga' ba chen po snyoms 'jug las//g+ha ma ri yang phyung nas ni//phyung nas byang gi sgo la ni//'jigs pa'i gzugs can ma nyid gnas//gnyis kyis skyod pa'i sbyor ba las//pu ka si yang phyung nas
30b2(rgyud): ni;;phyung nas dbang ldan mtshaMsu ni //drag po'i gzugs can ma nyid gan//yang ni bsrubs pa'i sbyor ba las//'tshed pa'i 'tshaMsu ri khrod ma//srin po'i 'tshamasu gdol pa mo//rlung gi 'tshamasu g.yung
30b3(rgyud): mo nyid;;de nas rdo rje chags chen las//rig mar bcas pa khu bar 'gyur//sna tshogs klu'i mchod pa las//de nas lha mo rnaMs kyis skul//rje btsun snying rje yid kyis gzhengs//pu ka si ni bdag la skyobs//
30b4(rgyud): stong pa'i rang bzhin nyid spongs la//bdag la bde chen sbyor bzhed mdzod//khyod med na ni bdag 'gum pas//kyai rdo rje khyod bzhengs cig//stong pa'i rang bzhing nyid spongs la//ri khrod ma 'bras grub par 
30b5(rgyud): mdzod;;dga' rtso 'jig rten 'gron gnyer bas//stong pa nyid la zhugs sam ci//khyod med phyogs ni mi 'tshal bas//gdol pa mo bdag zhu bar bgyid//bdag gis khyod kyi thugs
30b6(rgyud): 'tshal gyis//mig 'phrul dnga' ba khyed bzhengs cig;;g.yung mo bdag nyid dran nyams pas//snying rje rgyun chad ma mdzad ciga//khyod ni mg-i 'phrul lta bur gzhengs//glang po rta bong
30b7(rgyud): glang rnga mo//mi dang seng kha sgo byi la//g.yas pa'i thod pa brgyad rnaMs pa//gnyis mthung laswogs rim shes bya//sa dang chu dang rlung dang ni//me dang zla ba nyi ma nyid//mthar byed dang ni nor sbyin nyid;;g.yon
30b8(rgyud): pa'i thod pa brgyad na'o//sgyeg cing dpa'o mi sdug pa//rgod cing drag shul 'jigs rung ba//snying rje la rngam dang zhi ba las//gar gu ro dang ldan pa nyid//A+oM dang hU~M gis rdo rje che//khu ba'i gzugs las
31a1(rgyud): @#//bzhengs nas ni//zhabs rnams sa la bdebs pa dang //lha dang lha min sdigs par byed//gaM tsaM paM g+haM paM shaM laM TaM//'di'i sa bon phyung nas ni//bdag gi sa bon dag gis gis ni;;
31a2(rgyud): a hUM sngon po 'bar 'jigs pas//ma mo'i 'khor lo gro nyams dgar//kha dog nag po 'jig chen po//bdag med bde ba sbyin pa po//gtso bo 'di ltar sgom pa nyid//dkar mo'i g.yas ni dri khug ste;;
31a3(rgyud): de bzhin g.yon na ro hi ta//chom rkun ma g.yas cang de'u//g.yon pa'i phyag na phag pa nyid //ro langs ma g.yas ru sbal ste//g.yon pa yis ni pad ma'i snod//g+ha ma ri'i g.yas na sbrul//g.yon pa
31a4(rgyud): rnal 'byor lhung zed 'dzin//pu ti g.yas seng ge ste//g.yon pas dag sta de bzhin//ri khrod ma g.yas dge slong ste//g.yon na gsil byed de bzhin no//gdol pa mi g.yas 'khor 
31a5(rgyud): lo ste;;g.yon pas thod pa de bzhin no//g.yung mo g.yas na de bzhin rje nyid//g.yon na sdig 'dzub de bzhin no//dkyil dkrung byed pa'i gar kyis bzhugs//dkar mo la sogs phyag
31a6(rgyud): gnyis brjod//spyan gsuM skra ni gyen du ste//phyag rgya lnga yis rnam par brgyan//'go ri kha dog nag mor 'gyur//chom rkun ma ni gcod dang mtshungs//ro langs ma ni gser gtsos bzhin//g+ha ma ri ni mar gang
31a7(rgyud): bzhin//pu ka si ni dbang sngon mtshungs//ri khrod ma ni zla nor 'od//gdol pa namakha'i sngo bsangs ma//g.yung mo sna tshogs pa zhes brjod//tshangs pa dbang po nye dbang dgra//gshin rje dang ni nor bdag
31a8(rgyud): dang;;bden bral dang ni thag bzangs ris//'go'u ri la sogs pa'i bdan//b+ho la zhib par mdzad nas ni//dam du 'khyud pas 'o mdzad pas//bcom ldan gus pas mchod nas ni//bdag med ma
31b1(rgyud): yis sngags zhuso//bud med rnaMs kyi dbang med sngags//de bzhin bdug pa sdigs pa dang //klu rnaMs sdigs par byed pa nyid//lha dang lha min rnaMs med pa//de ni nga yis gshad bya yis//lha mo bde
31b2(rgyud): ba bzhin du nyon//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' la//bdag gis gzhan du ma stan to//'di'i sngags kyis gang 'byung zhing rdo rje seMs dpas gang mdzad pa//lha mo shin du 'jigs pa ste||dam
31b3(rgyud): chogs pas khyod la gshad//'bar ba'i 'phreng ba 'khrugs pa yis//dkyil 'khor rab tu gzhengs nas ni//rdo rje snying po dbang byin phyin//lha'i bu mo mchog bkug pa//bzlas pa khri yis gsal
31b4(rgyud): ba dang||skra ring ba dag bza' ba yis//kyai rdo rje sbyor ldan pas||btsun mo thams cad dgug par bya//bzlas pa 'bum gis rnal 'byor bdag//kyai rdo rje rnal 'byor gis//dogs pa med pa'i sems
31b5(rgyud): kyis ni||'di yis las rnaMs thaMs cad byed//rigs byed rnaMs kyis (ras) sbyin//zla byed thig les rnam par brgyan//de nas|a ShA na nA ya|pi g+ho ta d+ha ke she ba sa pa rta ni/tsa tur bi sha ti nA+e tA ya|de'i
31b6(rgyud): rjes la|Sho sha ta sha byu tsa ya|kri Sha dzi mu ta wa|pu she ka pa la mA lA nI ka d+ha ri nI|a rtad ku ra tsi ta ya|a d+he nu daM ShI ni|ma ra ya ma ra ya|ka ra ya ka ra ya|gar dz+dza ya/gar dz+dza ya|ta dz+dza ta rdza ya
31b7(rgyud): Sho sha ya Sho sha ya|sa pa ta sa ga ra na|b+han d+ha b+han d+ha|na ka a ShA ka na/gri ha na gri ha na sha tu ha ha|hi hi hu hu he he ho ho ha phaT//swAhA//de nas lha mo de dges nas//bsrub dang bsrub pa'i sbyor ba
31b8(rgyud): las||daM du khyud dang 'o byed pas//dkyil 'khor yid du 'od bzhus/de la stan pa ye shes che//rdo rje pad ma mnyam sbyar nas//dges pa'i thugs kyis mnyam bzhag pas//nyid kyis dkyil
32a1(rgyud): //'khor bri bar mdzad//'phar ma gcig dang sgo bzhi pa//sna tshogs 'od kyis yang dag 'khrugs//rta babs bzhi dang yang dag ldan//rdo rje srad bus rnam par rgyan;;re kha lnga dang
32a2(rgyud): yang dag ldan;;de nas bum pa brgyad po dri//rin chen lnga'i phye ma 'am//yang na 'bras la sogs pa yis//dur khrod kyi ni so phag gam//de bzhin dur khrod sol ba yis//de dbus pad ma 'dab
32a3(rgyud): ma ni;;brgyad pa ze bar bcas pa bri//snying por bcas pa'i thod pa gnyis//mdog dkar cha ni gsuM pa bri//dbang ldan du ni sha ra ba;;me'i mtshamasu dge slong bri//bden bral du ni 'khor lo bri
32a4(rgyud): rlung du rigs kyi bdag po bri//shar sgor de bzhin dri khu gnyid//g.yasu cang de'u dri ba nyid//nub du ru sbal bri ba nyid//lto;;phye byang du de bzhin no//lha mo kha dog dbye ba yis//
32a5(rgyud): phyag mtshan brgyad du rab tu bsgrags//dbusu thod pa dkar pos yang//sna tshogs rdorjes tshan pa bri//de nas rnam rgyal buM pa nyid//yal ga'i rtse mo gos bzangs can//lto bar
32a6(rgyud): rin chen lngas bkang la//sa lus skyes pa sa yongs bkang gzhag//mang du rab du brjod pas ci//ji ltar de nyid sdus pa'i//dkyil 'khor cho ga de bzhin bya//bcu gnyis brgyad gnyis lon
32a7(rgyud): pa'i;;rig pa bde chen brgyad po nyid//do zhal rkang dub gis brgyan nas//dkyil 'khor du rab tu bzhugs//ma dang sring mo nyid dang yang//bu mo sring mo'i bu mo dang//de bzhin zhang po'i chung
32a8(rgyud): ma dang//ma'i spun dang sgyug mo dang//de bzhin pha'i sring mo nyid//rig ma brgyad du rab tu grags//dam du 'khyud dang 'o byed pas//'di rnaMs rnal 'byor pa yis mchod//de lwa ga bur btung
32b1(rgyud): ba nyid/de nas dkyil 'khor gsang gtor bya//de nas rnal 'byor pas btung bya//dngos grub myur du thob par 'gyur//de la chang yang btung ba dang //sha dang sa lu skyes pa bza'//
32b2(rgyud): des kyang gos dang bral byas nas//ba kar 'o ni yang yang byed//des kyang bo la zhib par bya//gar bya klu yang blang bar bya//bo la ka ko la sbyar nas//de la brtsed mo rtse bar bya//de nas thun chod
32b3(rgyud): gnyis pa las//gos kyis mig ni bkab nasu //der ni slob ma bzhug pa nyid//phyi nas dkyil 'khor rab tu stan//mtshan mo skye bo med khyim du//slob dpon la sogs rab phye bas//dbang ni ji ltar gsungs
32b4(rgyud): pa nyid//de la dbang ni sbyin par bya//ji ltar mchod dang stod gshad pa//slob ma bzang por sngar shes bya//de la de nyid bstan pa ni//dga' bral dang po mchog mtha' can//thams cad rgyud du sbas pa ste//
32b5(rgyud): mtha'i mtha'i phye ba nyid//rdo rje mchod par rab sbyar nas//de la lha mo de zhus pa//lha de'i skad cig ci ltar lags//gtso bo chen pos gshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/
32b6(rgyud): der ni thog ma dbus mtha' med//sred med mya ngan 'das pa 'ang med//'di ni mchog du bde chen nyid//bdag med gzhan yang med pa nyid//long spyod kyis ni rlabs gnyis la//rang gi
32b7(rgyud): g.yas dang lag g.yon gi//the bo dang ni srin lag gang//des ni rnal 'byor pa yis gtsir//des ni ye shes skye bar 'gyur//de la ci zhig skye zhe na//gzhon nu'i dga' ba ci lta 'am//lkugs pa'i rmi lam
32b8(rgyud): ci lta ba//mchog gi mtha' dang dga' bral dbus//stong dang stong min he ru ka//kyai rdo rje las kyai rdo rje mngon par 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i le'u lnga pa'o////lha mo la ni dam mkhyud cing//bo la thod
33a1(rgyud): @#//par bcug nas ni//dam du nu ma bzung mdzad de//skyes pa'i sna ni bzhibs nas kyang//tshems kyis ma mchu btsir nas ni//mchan khung sen mo'i rma mdzad de//mnyam sbyar bde ba
33a2(rgyud): myang mdzad nas//phyag rgya lnga ni rab phye bas//bla ma slob dpon 'dod lha la//phyag 'tshal don du 'khor du 'dzin//bla ma rdo rje 'dzin pa la//smod tshig mi nyan pa'i phyir//rna ba dag du rna cha 'dzin;;
33a3(rgyud): sngags kyi bzlas pa 'ang 'gul rgyan cing//gdu bu srog chags gsad pa 'ang spang//phyag rgya sten pa skye rags nyid//sangs rgyas lnga'i phyag rgyas ni//lus la rtag tu phyag rgyas btab//de nas rab tu
33a4(rgyud): gshad mdzad nas//tshems kyis ma 'chu btsir nas ni//kyai rdo rje zhes bya ba//lhan cig skyes pa'i gzugs can nyid//de la lha mo de zhus pa//gtso bo cho ga gang gis dang//de bzhin bya ba
33a5(rgyud): gang gis ni//kyai rdo rje'i dris sku'i//byed pa bde ba chen pos gsungs//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//'dir ni 'bri mkhan dam tshig can//bsgrub po dam tshig can gis
33a6(rgyud): ni;;bris sku 'jigs pa bri ba nyid//skyes pa'i thod pa kha dog lngas//ro'i sgra'i pir nyid kyis//bris sku bla ma bri ba nyid//gang ga skud pa 'khal ba dang//gang gang ras yug mthag pa dang//de yang dam
33a7(rgyud): tshig can ngas pas//dam batshig byin rlebas rnal 'byor pas//zla ba nya ba'i mar ngo bzhin//bcu bzhi pa la khyim dben par//phyed nas ma rus sems kyis ni//chang gi btung ba bcud zad las;
33a8(rgyud): lus ni rang su bsams nas ni//de bzhin gcer bur 'gyur bas yang//'bags pa dag ni ma dag pas//de nas dam tshig rab tu bza'//bzhin bzangs snying rjer ldan pa dang//gzugs dang lang
33b1(rgyud): mtshos skal bzangs ma//me tog bcas shing bsgrub por dga'//rang gi phyag rgya g.yon du bzhag//kyai rdo rje las bris sku'i cho ga'i//le'u ste drug pa'o////de nas de la lha mos
33b2(rgyud): zhus;;bo la ka k+ko la sbyar nas//tshems kyis ma 'chu btsir nas ni//glegs bam yang ni ji ltar gyur//rdo rje pad ma mnyam sbyar nas//dges pa'i glegs baM rab phye bas//glegs baM nga yis
33b3(rgyud): gshad bya yis//lha mo bskal ba chen mo nyon//sbrang chen snag tsha byas nas ni//mi'i rus pa'i snyug gus bri//glegs baM sor ni bcu gnyis pa//gro gar dam tshig can gis bri//glegs baM dang ni
33b4(rgyud): bris sku nyid//gal te bskal pa med mthong na//skye ba 'dir ni dngos grub med//'jig rten pha rol spyod yul med//yang dag rab sbyin ldan pa la//res 'ga' tsam zhig bstan pa nyid//glegs
33b5(rgyud): baM lam gi spyod yul du//sku dang 'chan khung nyid du sba;pa gar ling kha rab bzhag nas//yang dang yang du 'o 'dzad nas//bde chen mnyam pa myang mdzad nas//rdo rje can gyis
33b6(rgyud): bza' ba bstan//tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor bza' ba ni;nyon cig lha mo mig yangs ma//gang du zos pa 'dod don du//basgrubs pa tsam gyis dngos grub 'gyur//dur khrod rI
33b7(rgyud): bya skyibs dang //de bzhin mi med gror khyer dang //yang na dben pa 'am rgya mtsho'i mtha'//bza' ba 'di ni rab tu bza'//de la bdan du brtag pa ni//ro'i rigs gzugs can dgu dang ni;
33b8(rgyud): yang na stag gi pags pa dang //dur khrod ras ni de bzhin no//dbusu kyai rdo rje'i gzugs//ji ltar sngon bzhin gnasu //phyogs dang phyogs pral dag du yang //de nas rnal 'byor ma rnaMs
34a1(rgyud): /dgod;;dam tshig ma la ta d+ha nam//stags gi pags pa'i steng du bza'//'dab nas rgyal po'i sa lu ni//bza' ba der ni bza' ba nyid//der ni bza' ba zos nas kyang//der ni ma mo
34a2(rgyud): mchod par bya//sring mo'i bo mo sgyug mo dang//gal te ma sring yin na yang//de rnams rtag tu legs mchod nas//tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor grub par 'gyur//skyes pa'i thod chen cha gcig par//chang ni
34a3(rgyud): bzang pos yongs bka' ste//bla ma la dbul skal chen ma;;phyag 'tshal nas rang yang (yang)btung//pad ma'i lag pas blang ba dang//de nyid kyis ni lag pas dbul//yang nas yang du rab tu btung//der ni
34a4(rgyud): bsgrub pa pos bya//kyai rdo rje las bza' ba'i le'u ste bdun pa'o////de nas rnal 'byor mas zhus pa//phyag rgya chen po ji lta bu//kun brdzob rnam pa'i gzugs kyis ni;;bde
34a5(rgyud): ba thob pas gshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//shin tu ring min thung ba min//gnag pa ma yin dkar ba min//pad ma'i ('dab dro sha'i)rnam pa 'dra//ro ni dbugs ni dri zhim
34a6(rgyud): ldan;;rdul dang dri zhim ldan 'gyur te//ri tags lte ba'i dri dang mtshungs//pad ma la 'ang dbang mchog dri//skad cig pad ma lta bu byung//de'i bur sil ha dag dang//mkhas pas dri bzangs mtshon par bya
34a7(rgyud): ud pa la'i dri 'byung zhing//a ka ru ni nag pos 'tshungs//dpa' zhing (mig so)mnyam nyid dang//snyan par smra zhing yid 'ong ma//sgra bzangs dbus su gnyer ma gsuM//phal pas pad ma can du 'gyur//lhan cig
34a8(rgyud): skyes pa'i gzugs can ma//de rnyed nas ni dngos grub 'gyur//de nas rnal 'byor ma bdag med mas gsol pa//bcom ldan smon lam ji ltar btab par bgyi//bcom ldan 'das kyis bga' stsal pa//rigs
34b1(rgyud): skye dang dam tshig can//snyo med kyai rdo rje ston//bla ma la gus snying rjer ldan//skye zhing skye bar 'gyur bar shog//lag pa rdo rje dril bu 'khrol//zab mo'i chos ni klog pa dang//btsun mo'i khu ba mnyam zas
34b2(rgyud): can;;de nas lha mo de dges nas//tshig ni 'di nyid yang zhus pa//skal med seMs can 'dul dka' ba//gang gis 'dul bar gyur pa lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//dang po gso sbyong
34b3(rgyud): sbyin par bya//de rjes slob pa'i gnas bcu nyid//de la bye brag smra ba stan//mdo sde pa yang de bzhin no//de nas rnal 'byor spyod pa nyid//de'i rjesu dbu ma stan//sngags kyi rim pa kun shes nas;;de
34b4(rgyud): rjes kyai rdo rje brtsam//slob ma gus pas blangs nas ni//'grub 'gyur 'di la the tsom med//kyai rdo rje las 'dul ba'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o////de nas kha sbyor byed pa'i//mtshan nyid yang dag
34b5(rgyud): rab tu gshad//gang gis rnam par shes tsam gis//sgrub pos dngos grub thob par 'gyur//bsgrub bya'i lte ba'i rtsa ba la;;brtul shugs can kyi lag pas gdon//he ru kha mnyam rnal
34b6(rgyud): 'byor gis//nang gi ma rungs sems kyis ni//'di ni sgom pa tsam gyis ni//sangs rgyas dang ni nges par 'jig//bstan la gnod par byed pa dang//bla ma sangs rgyas 'jig pa dang//bla ma
34b7(rgyud): thub pa la zhus nas//snying rjes bsad par bya ba nyid//ci ltar phyugs kyi gzugs mthong na//khrag ni skyug pa nyid dang yang//'dar zhing 'go skyes grol ba dang//kha ni 'og tu rnam par sgom//de'i lam du
34b8(rgyud): khab ni me'i//gzugs can 'jug par bsam pa ste//snying khar byin sa bon nyid//dmigs nas skad cig gsad par byed//rgyud 'di las ni sregs lugs med;;phyag rgya bcing dang bya ba med;;rgyud chen
35a1(rgyud): //glags pas 'grub pa yin//bsam gtan tsam gis 'grub par 'gyur//rtog med dngos grub sbyin pa'o//srid pa'i sbyor ba nyams dga' ba//gsang ba'i mchog ni gshad bya yis//nyon cig lha
35a2(rgyud): mo bzhin bzangs ma//rin chen gzi brjid 'bar ba yi//'phreng ba 'khrugs pa lha mo ltos//ma phug pas ni sbyor bral 'gyur//phug pas sbyin pa dga' ba'o//de ltar 'khor ba 'dod pa yi//yon tan lnga
35a3(rgyud): ldan rin chen nyid//ma dag pas ni dug du 'gyur//dag pas bdu rtsi lta bur 'gyur//'khor ba'i rnam pa he ru ka//'gro ba sgrol ba'i rtso bo nyid//gang gi gzugs kyis 'gyur ba nyid//de ni nga yis smra yis
35a4(rgyud): nyon;;snying rje'i spyan ni dmar po//byams pa'i thugs ni yan lag gnag//sdu ba'i dngos po bzhi'is ni//zhabs ni rnam pa bzhi zhes brjod//zhal brgyad rnam par thar pa brgyad//phyag
35a5(rgyud): ni stong pa bcu drug nyid//gug bskyed kyis ni sangs rgyas lnga//bdug pa 'dul phyir 'khro bo nyid//sha ni pu ka si ru brjod;;de bzhin khrag gis ri khrod ma//bdol pa'i ni khu bar brjod;;
35a6(rgyud): g.yung mo rkang dang tshil bu dag//pags pa byang chub yan lag bdun//rus pa bden pa bzhi po nyid//bdag med mas gsol pa//sngags ni btu bar ji ltar 'gyur//gang gis sngags kyi skyes pa'o
35a7(rgyud): las kyi dngos grub 'gyur ba nyid//bde ba chen po gshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje can//chen po rdo rje snying po gzi brjid chen pos bka' stsal pa//sngags ni khyod la ngas gshad kyis;;
35a8(rgyud): lha mo shes rab chen mo nyon//dang por rnam par snang mdzad sbyin//u Sh+ma rnaMs kyi bzhi pa la//pu ka sis brgyan stong pas mnan//mdog dkar mthar ni sa har sbyar//'di ni bzlas pa 'buM gyis
35b1(rgyud): ni;'gro ba rtag tu rengs par byed//dang por yi ge'i bdag po bzhag//de nas mkhas spyod ma de nas//mthar ni swahA sbyar byas nas//sangs rgyas rnaMs kyang dbang du byed//rig byed rnaMs kyi dang po
35b2(rgyud): sbyin;dang po'i ni gnyis pa la//stong byas mthar ni swahA sbyor//sangs rgyas kyang bskrod shes rab can//dang por rnaM par snang mdzad sbyin//gnyis pa'i ni gsuM pa la//ra dang ldan zhing chu mas rgyan;stong
35b3(rgyud): bcas swahA yang dag ldan//mi rnams thaMs cad sdang bar byed//dang po'i yi ge'i dbang phyug bzhag//lnga pa nyid kyi gsum pa la//stong pa mkha' 'gro ma dang ldan//mthar ni swehA mngon spyod che//yang ni
35b4(rgyud): yi ge'i thu bo sbyin//hU~M ni khrag dang 'dra ba'i//mthar ni swahA thig le mchog//dga' bzangs ma stsogs rtag tu 'gugs//gti mug rigs ni dang por sbyin//mgu ni yang dag rab tu sbyar//mthar ni swahA
35b5(rgyud): yang byas nas//lha dang lha min gsod par byed//dang po ra rnam par snang mdzad bzhag//dang po nyid kyi dang po la//rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma dang sbyar//mtha' sbyar ba'i gnyis pa la//rdo rje
35b6(rgyud): mkha' 'gro ma dang ldan//yang ni dang po'i rtse mo la//rdo rje mkha' 'gro mdang ldan//mthar gnas pa'i gsuM pa la//yang ni de nyid yang dag ldan//steng du chum rkun ma yis rgyan//de
35b7(rgyud): rjes hri'i rnaM par 'gyur//mthar yang swahA spyod pa nyid//ku ru ku le'i sngagaso //oM ni dang po'i bzhi pa yi//gsuM pa chom rkun ma yis rgyan//mthar gnas pa'i bzhi pa la//pi tsu badzra sbyar ba dang //dbus ma rnaMs
35b8(rgyud): kyis bzhi pa la//pu ka ses ni rnam par brgyan//stong pas mnan cing sum 'gyur nyid//lnga pa yi ni gnyis pa dang //gsum pa'i dang por mthar swahA//kyai rdo rje'i snying po'o//snang mdzad dang por badzra gnyis
36a1(rgyud): @#//sbyar nas lnga pa'i bzhi pa nyid//mthar nas rnams kyi dang por ldan//g+hasma ris ni rnaM par brgyan//dbus ma rnaMs kyi bzhi pa la//rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma yis brgyan//stong pas mnan
36a2(rgyud): cing sum 'gyur nyid//lnga pa yi ni gnyis pa dang //gsuM pa'i dang po mthar swahA//phyag bzhi pa'i sngagaso//dang po rnam snang dang po yi//dang po nang gyi kar mos brgyan//gsum pa nyid kyi dang po la;;
36a3(rgyud): nang gi kar mos rnam par brgyan//gnyis 'gyur lnga pa'i gsum pa dang //gnyis pa'i gsuM pa ma yis brgyan//hU~M gi rnam pa gsum sbyar nas//lnga pa nyid kyi gnyis pa dang //gsum pa'i dang
36a4(rgyud): po mthar swahA//phyag drug pa'i sngagaso//dang po rnams snang bzhi pa'i//dang po byin bza' yang dag ldan//ro langs ma yis rnam par brgyan//mthar gnas pa'i gsuM pa la//g+hasma ri yis rnam
36a5(rgyud): par brgyan//dang po yi ni dang po la//mthar gnas pI dang po dang //rdo rje ma yis rnam par brgyan //k+sha ni chom rkun ma yis brgyan//lnga pa nyid kyi dang po dang //dbus sma rnaMs kyi bzhi
36a6(rgyud): pa la;rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma yis brgyan//stong pa mnan cing sum 'gyur te//phaT dang mthar ni swahA nyid//phyag gnyis pa'i sngagaso//bdag med dang po'i dang po dang //gsum pa nyid kyi dang po
36a7(rgyud): dang;;bzhi pa yi ni dang po dang //lnga pa'i ni dang po dang //mthar gnas rnams kyi dang po dang //dbusam rnaMs kyi dang po nyid//dang por rnam snang mthar swahA//grong khyer bkrugs pa'i sngags
36a8(rgyud): kyi bzlas pa ni 'bum mo//mthar gnas pa rnams kyi gnyis pa dang //k+sha'i rkang pa gnyis 'gyur nyid//hU~M gi rnaM pa gsum dang ldan//dang po rnam snang phaT nyid kyis//rnam pa nang bcug mthar
36b1(rgyud): swahA|sa sbyang ba'i sngagaso//dang po rnam snang de rjesu//rdo rje ma'i mtha' ru hU~M//bza' ba dang btung ba byin gyis rlab pa'o//dang por rnam par snang mdzad nyid//rdo rjes a ko ro mu khaM//dbus ma rnaMs kyi gsuM pa dang ;;
36b2(rgyud): mthar gnas pa'i bzhi pa ni//steng du me yis rnam par brgyan//rta ma nA da rdo rje ma byin la//r+ya nu t+ya n+na ta ta/oM a hUM paT swahA//'byung po thams cad kyi gtor ma'i sngagaso//kyai rdo rje las sngags btu ba'i////
36b3(rgyud): le'u zhes byas ste dgu pa'o////de na chos kun sdom gcig pa'i//rdo rje sems dpa' zhes byas gsungs//bdag med ma la 'o mdzad nas//bzlas pa'i yul ni rab tu dbye//shel gis rengs pa'i bzlas pa
36b4(rgyud): nyid||dbang la btsan dan dmar po nyid//lung thang gis ni dngon spyod yid//ni rang sus ni sdang ba la//rta'i rus pas skrad pa nyid//braM ze rus pas rgyug pa nyid//glang po'i rus pas char ba la//ma he yis kyang
36b5(rgyud): gsad pa la//rengs pa'i 'o ma btung ba nyid//dbang gi rang gi mdun pas spyad//gsad pa la ni sil ba nyid//dgug pa la ni bzhi mnyam nyid//sngags las sa lu skyes pa nyid//skrad pa
36b6(rgyud): nyid la gla bar brjod//yang na mtha'i swa dang//dang po'i swa dang//na dang po dang/ha dang po nyid kyang de bzhin no//kyai rdo rje las ba zlas pa'i le'u ste bcu pa'o////kyai rdo rje las dam mkhyud
36b7(rgyud): cing|chos kyis ma 'chu btsir nas ni//lus can rnaMs kyi rigs kyi gzugs//de la bdag med mas zhus pa//ba gar ling ka bzhag nas ni//'khor lo'i 'den pas 'di skad gsungs//lus can rang gi
36b8(rgyud): rigs gshad kyis//shes rab pha rol phyin ma nyon//bud med dam ni skyes pa'i//gang gis srin lag rtsa ba la//rdo rje dgu par la gyur pa//mi skyod pa'i rigs mchog nyid//rnam snang mdzad kyi 'khor
37a1(rgyud): @#//lor gyur;dpag med 'gon gyi 'dam skyes nyid//rin chen 'byung gi rin chen nyid//las kyi rigs kyi ral gri nyid//rnal 'byor pa gang nag po yin//de'i lha ni mi skyod pa//rnal 'byor pa gang
37a2(rgyud): dkar po che//de'i lha ni rnam snang mdzad//rnal 'byor pa gang sngo sangs che//de'i lha ni don yod yin//rnal 'byor pa gang ser po che//rin chen bdag po rigs kyi lha//rnal 'byor pa nyid dkar dmar nyid;
37a3(rgyud): 'od dpag med pa rigs kyi lha//rnal 'byorapa gang dkar dang ser//de ni sems dpa'i rigasu 'gyur//skye bo rnal 'byor mthar byin pas//nga rgyal mi bcag tho mi 'tsham//kun rdzob gzugasu gnas
37a4(rgyud): nas ni//de rnaMs bder gshegs rigasu 'gyur//bud med rnaMs kyi mtshan nyid 'di//ci ltar skyes pa de bzhin no//kun rdzob tha snyad tshul gyis ni//de'i rigasu 'ang de 'gyuro//ba ga ling ga'i 'o mdzad n+sa;
37a5(rgyud): de la rdo rje che mnyes nas//bdag med ma'i 'o mdzad nas༐ lha mo rab tu mchod pa nyon//tshal dang skye bo mi gnas dang//rang gi khyim gi nang du yang//gcer bu phyag rgya cher byas nas//rnal
37a6(rgyud): 'byor rig pas rtag tu mchod//'o dang 'khyud pa byas nas ni//de bzhin ba gar reg pa nyid//skyes pa'i sna ni bzhibs pa nyid//ma mchu sbrang rtsi btung ba nyid//rtag tu myos pa'i mtshan ma las//bo las ldan p+sa
37a7(rgyud): lag pas bya//khyogs dang rla zhes bya ba dang//de bzhin shin tu dkar ba yis//yang yang rdo rjes 'dod pa bya//steng dang 'og tu lta ba nyid//de na dngos grub rgyas pa ste//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam p+Ra 'gyur
37a8(rgyud): de la ga bu tung bar bya//de la ga bur rgyu phyir//sha ni bza' ba nyid kyi phyir//khyad par tu yang chang nyid//kyA+e rdo rje las lhan cig skyes pa'i sbyor ba'i don kyi le'u zhes bya ba ste bcu gcig pa'o////de nas
37b1(rgyud): rdo rje can gi dbang//bzhi pa'i don ni bka' stsal pa//rdo rje rab gnas rdo rje nyid//che dang dril bu chen por bzung//de ring rdo rje slob dpon 'gyur//slob ma sdu ba nyid du 'gyur//ci ltar 'das pa'i sangs rgyas
37b2(rgyud): kyis//byang chub sras rnaMs dbang skur ba//bdag gi gsang ba'i dbang gis ni//sems kyi rgyun gyis dbang skur ro//lha mo dga' sbyin gnas sbyin ma//sna tshogs gzugs can yid 'ong ma//khyer cig khyer
37b3(rgyud): cig sems dpa' che//khyer la mchod pa rtag du gyis//ye shes 'di ni ches 'phra zhing//namakha'i khaMs ltar bur//rdul bral mthar phyin zhi ba nyid//khyod rang yang ni de'i pha//rdo rje dang pad ma byin gi rlabs
37b4(rgyud): pa'i sngags ni////oM pad ma su kha d+ha ra ma ha ra ga su khaM dang/dza du ra n+na b+ha gwi swa hUM hUM hUM//kA r+yaM ku ru shwa me/oM badzra ma ha dA+e Sha tsa dur nan dang yang ka kha ka m+lai kha ka na so tha na/hUM hUM hUM kA r+yA ku ru shwa me/sbyi
37b5(rgyud): bor ni oM gi rnam pa'o//snying khar ni hUM gi rnam pa'o//ze 'bru la ni a'i rnam pa'o//rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po sgyu ma rtag pa zhes bya ba//rtag pa sum bcu rtsa gnyis las byung ba//rtag pa gnyis
37b6(rgyud): kyi mtshan nyid////kyA+e rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma dra ba'i sdom ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po////rdzogas+ho////rgya gar mkhan po g+ha ya d+ha rI zhal snga nas dang/dge slong shakya ye shes kyis bsgyur cing zhus te
37b7(rgyud): gtan la phab pa'o////@#//rgya gar skad du/Shi hi badzra tan tra ra dza b+hud d+ha/sarba ka ya bag tsi ta ko sha g+hu he na ma/bod skad du/dpal rgyas pa rdo rje'i rgyud /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sku gsung
37b8(rgyud): thugs kyi gsang ba'i mdzod ces bya ba/dpal dgyes pa rdo rje la phyag 'tshal lo///'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang
38a1(rgyud): //thugs rdo rje'i sa rang bzhin rnam par dag pa'i gnas na/thun mong dang thun mong ma yin 'khor zhing gi bdul snyed dang thabs gcig tu bzhugaso//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis sems can
38a2(rgyud): gi sgo gsum sku gsung thugasu rang bzhin gyis/rnam par dag pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs te/sngags 'di gsungaso////oM a ka ro mu khaM swa rba d+ha rma naM/ar+ya nud pa na na t+yad/oM a hUM/
38a3(rgyud): rnam pa thams cad thog ma nas//ma skyes skye ba gang yang med//de nyid gsang ba chen po'i mdzod//sku gsung thugs kyi mtshan nyid do//sarba wa shud d+ho ra ga sap+ta wa shud d+ha hAM/swa b+ha wa shud d+ho d+he sha sap+ta wa shud d+ho hAM
38a4(rgyud): rgyu rkyen gang gis mi skye bas//'dod chags rang bzhin rnam par dag//zhe sdang ngo bo nyid kyis med//zhe sdang rang bzhin rnam par dag//gti mug gis 'byung 'bras bu med//gti mug rang bzhin rnaM par
38a5(rgyud): dag/gdug gsum ye nas rnam par dag//khaMs gsum bta na dang g.yor bcas pas//snang ma myong zhing grub ma nyod//de phyir ra 'khor ba'i ming yang med//thog mar ma skyes rang bzhin dag/
38a6(rgyud): blo bur sangs rgyas ming yang med//sangs rgyas sems can gnyis ma yin//don dam kun rdzob gnyis ma yin//rang rig rang bzhin rig ma yin//'od gsal la sogs ma yin te//de dag kun kyi ming
38a7(rgyud): yang med//smra bsam brjod du med pa ste//dper na rkugs pas don myong na//de nyid bcom ldan dges rdor rje//ras 'dra shel 'dra'i rang rig la//lo tog rtsi bsogs rnam par 'phrul//kham gsum bdan dang
38a8(rgyud): g.yor snang zhing//'dod pa'i byang chub sna tshogs dang//de dag spyod pa sna tshogs dag//de nyid g.yeng dang rnam rtog ste//rang gis 'phrul nas rang gis 'ching//rang gis bsod cing rang sdug sngal//rang rig
38b1(rgyud): ye shes spros bral la//sgra dang tha snyad 'jug na 'phrul//rang bzhin rang gis 'od bsal ba//ma rig mun pas bsgribs na 'phrul//dngos po stong pa ro mnyam la//blang dang dor ba med na 'phrul;;'dod
38b2(rgyud): yon rang gis 'gegs pa las//de dag rtog pas 'gegs na 'phrul//rang rig 'dod pa'i stong pa la//'dod yon lnga la brten na 'phrul//chos nyid dman dang gya nom spang//sgro dang bskur pa 'debs
38b3(rgyud): na 'phrul//sgom pa nyid kyang rtog pa la//mi sgom pa yang rtog pa ste//sgom dang mi sgom 'dzin na 'khrul//sangs rgyas sems can gnyis med pa//de dag gnyisu 'dzin na 'khrul//don dam kun rdzob
38b4(rgyud): gnyis med la//bden pa gnyisu 'dzin na 'khrul/sangs rgyas 'khor ba'i chu bo dgal//skye 'gag gang ltar 'dod kyang 'phrul;;dbyings dang ye shes gang 'dod 'khrul//stong nyid ro la mi gan+sa
38b5(rgyud): par;;dngos por mtshan m+Ra gnas na 'khrul//sems kyi glang po mi 'dul nas//g.yeng ba'i gnasu 'dug na 'khrul////thams cad goms pa'i stobs las byung//mi sgom pa yang 
38b6(rgyud): 'khrul pa nyid//rnam rtog rlung gis bskyod pa la//rang rig ye shes 'dod na 'khrul//rang sems rang gyis ma mthong bar//che ba'i nga rgyal smra na 'khrul//rang rig nyams su ma nyong bar//dkar
38b7(rgyud): po'i chos rnaMs stong na 'khrul//theg chen bzab mo'i mdo sde dang/man ngag stobs kyis zhugs pa la//'khrul pa med cing ma 'khrul med//'khrul dang ma 'khrul 'dzin na 'khrul//'khrul dang 'khrul
38b8(rgyud): bzhi lasogs ma'i//gzhan yang 'khrul pa'i bye phrag rnams//bla ma'i zhal las shes par bya//rang gi mngon pa'i nga rgyal spang//bla ma dam pa rtag tu sten//theg chen zab mo mdo sde lta//sgom pa po med sgom
39a1(rgyud): @#//pa med/bsgom du med pa'i don bsgoMs na//ma bsgoms pa nyid bsgoMs pa'i mchog//rang sems rang rig mthong 'gyur na//dngos dang mtshan ma gang ma mthong //ma mthong ba nyid mthong
39a2(rgyud): ba'i mchog//gang ltar ltar yang mi rung ba'i//lta ru med pa'i don la lta//ma ltas na yang lta ba'i mchog//rnam rtog ye nas rang gis sregs//bsreg du med pa'i sbyin sreg bya//ma sregs pa nyid sreg pI
39a3(rgyud): mchog/mchod pa po med mchod bya med//mchod du med pa'i mchod pa ni//ma mchod na yang mchod pa ste//rang rig mchod pa mchod pa'i mchog//dka' ba med pa'i dka' thub bya//stong pa'i dka'
39a4(rgyud): thub 'daka' thub mchog//srung du med pa'i don srung ste//rang rig brtul zhugs rtul zhugs mchog//sbyin du med pa'i sbyin pa btang /seMs kyi sbyin pa sbyin pa'i mchog/tshul khrims la sogs de bzhin
39a5(rgyud): de ltar 'gyur ba'i mkhas pa la//stong par mi 'gyur gang yang med//de las mya ngan 'das pa 'byung //bdag blo mya ngan 'das par 'gyur//de la 'khor ba bsgrub du med//sa dang laM
39a6(rgyud): rtags de la med//sangs rgyas gzugs min sgra ma nyin//dga' ba'i ye shes de la med//de dag thams cad seMs nyid de//rang sems rang gis shes pa dang //rang sems rang gis rtog pa
39a7(rgyud): na/sangs rgyas ma mthong rang sems mthong //chos can ma mthong chos nyid mthong //bdag du lta bar tshol rnaMs dang //lta ba rnaMs las log rnams dang //mthar ltung dbu mar gang 'dod pa'i//lta

39b1(rgyud): phan yon no//de nas yang dag rab gshad pa//sems can ma 'khrul sangs rgyas 'phrul//gzhan rig ma 'khrul rang rig 'khrul//bag chags ma 'khrul sems nyid 'khrul//sangs rgyas las 'khrul seMs
39b2(rgyud): can de//rang rig las 'khrul gzhan rig snang//sems nyid las 'khrul bag chags 'byung//des na sems can 'khrul pa dag//don du snang ba rab tu skye//skye ba med bzhin skye ba'i 'khrul//lcags
39b3(rgyud): sregs sa bzhi rang gi sems//me tshogs byed pa gzhan na med//bkres bskom sdug sngal rang gi sems//zas skom 'gegs pa logs na med//bdegs rtsogs gshan gzhu rang gi sems//
39b4(rgyud): srog gcod shan pa logs na med//'gro ba sems can thams cad kun//rang gi semasu ma shes na//des ni sangs rgyas spangs pa yin//sems las ma rtogs sangs rgyas dang//sems
39b5(rgyud): las ma rtogs chos gzhan yang//sangs rgyas kyis kyang ma gzigs shing//ma gsungs gsung bar mi 'gyuro//de na sa seMs can thams cad kyis//sems kyi gsang 'di gsang
39b6(rgyud): ba'i mchog//sku gsung thugs thugs kyi gsang ba'o//sems kyi mdzod 'di gsang ba'i mdzod//rang rig ye shes me'i mdzod/de bas dngos grub 'dod pa yis//sems kyi gsang chen dga'
39b7(rgyud): rab 'di//sangs rgyas dang ni bla ma dag//srog gi phyir yang mi btang ngo//ma yengs rtag par gnas byas te//seMs kyi tshangs pa'i gnas bsgrags pas//log pa'i lta ngan zil gis gnon//sems
39b8(rgyud): kyi seng ge gzugs brtan te//mu stegs ri dgas ngan 'jigs bya//sems kyi rin chen sgron ma yin//ma rig mun pa'i rang bzhin gsal//seMs kyi zla ba shar ba yis//'dod chags la sogs bdud
40a1(rgyud): @#//pa sel//sems kyi thugs rje'i char phab pas//nyon mongs tha ba tshim par byas//rang las ma rtogs sems nyid dang//sems las ma rtogs sangs rgyas ni//yod min btsal bar 
40a2(rgyud): mi bya'o//de nas yang dag par gshad pa//dam tshig dang ni phyag rgya ste//rdo rje skye ba mi 'da' ba//phyag rgya dam tshig du ni gshad//rang sems ye nas ma skyes pa'i//phyag rgya de yis brgyas btab pas//skyed 
40a3(rgyud): stsogs rtogs pas de mi phyed//de nyid las kyang 'da' mi byed//sems nyid stong pa'i phyag rgya la//mtshan ma'i rtog pas de mi phyed//de nyis srung ba'i dam tshig go//mtha' bral dbu mar gang gnas pa//
40a4(rgyud): mi phyed phyir na de phyag rgya//de las gzhan pa dam tshig go//spros bral bsam gis mi khyab pa'i//phyag rgya de ni dam tshig ste//srog la bab kyang bsrungs cig ces//sangs rgyas rnaMs la 'doms par byed//
40a5(rgyud): dam tshig las ni rdo rje mchang//'das na de las bzlog pa'i//mu sdegs nyan thos rang rgyal dang//lta ba dman pa'i seMs can la//sems kyi dam pa rin chen 'di//smra bar mi bya gsang mi bya 
40a6(rgyud): brjod par mi bya kleng mi slang//de ltar byas na dam tshig nyams//lta ba dman pa'i soms can dag//sems kyi gsang 'dis 'jigs skrags nas//sgro dang bskur pa'i mtha' gnas te//rang gi seMs la 
40a7(rgyud): bskur pa 'debs//de yis yod pa'i dngos po 'jig//dngos po mtha' las sgro yang 'dogs//de yis 'khrul pa'i shes pa bskur//lta ba ma byas dam tshig nyams//lta ba ma rungs 'dzin byed pa//de las
