40b1(rgyud): mi bya grol mi bya//blang bar mi bya dor mi bya//sbyor bar mi bya mi sbyor min//de ltar rtag tu brtson par bya//gang la seMs kyi dam pa rin chen te//rtogs pa de la kun kyis kur bar bya//phyogs bcu'i dpa'o mkha'
40b2(rgyud): mgro ma tshogs kyang //de la rtag tu skyab dang bsrung dang sba//sngags shing stod dang mchod pa lasogs byed//des ni tshad med skye bo'i tshogs rnams dang //dgra dang bdud kyi sde ni rab tu bcom//bde mchog bde
40b3(rgyud): blag nyid du thob par byed//'khor ba'i chu bo 'di yis sgrol ba las//thabs gzhan gang gis nges par sgrol ma yin//nyon mongs g.yul las 'di yis rgyal ba las//dpa'o gzhan gyi g.yul las rgyal
40b4(rgyud): ma yin||mkhor ba'i mching ba'i srad bu 'di yis 'grol ba las//mtshan ma'i las gzhan gang gis grol ma yin//tshul khrims nyaMs dang dam tshig 'chal rnaMs kyi//sdam zhing gso ba'i mchog kyang 'di
40b5(rgyud): yin no//bkres skom 'jigs sogs sdug sngal du ma yi//ma gon dang skyabs kyi mchog kyang 'di yin no|nad dang da dug bsogs sdug sngal du ma yi//bso ba'i sman gi mchog
40b6(rgyud): kyang 'di yin pas//'gro rnaMs kun la rgyud 'di legs gtad kyis|legs par bcang zhing legs par sba bar bya//legs par rgyud cing legs pa spel bar bya//bdag nyid smin cing
40b7(rgyud): 'gazhan yang grol 'gyur pa//rgyud 'di'i mithu dang rtsa la ni 'di yin no//de nas rgyud kyi don 'di shin tu ngo mtshar che bas sa chen po yang rab tu g.yos te//nam mkha' chos kyi sgra dbyangs las//mchod
40b8(rgyud): pa'i sprin dang sgra chen po yang rab tu byung ste//chos thams cad thog ma nas ma skyes pa//zhi ba rang bzhin med pa ma grub pa lasogs pa'i sgra dang /rgyud kyi don de la ngo mtshar skyes te/yid rangs nas
41a1(rgyud): @#//rang mi snang bar gyur to////dpal dgyes pa rdo rje'i rgyud /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang/thugs kyi gsang ba'i mdzod ces bya ba rdzogas+ho//|//
41a2(rgyud): lo tsa ba dge ba'i blo gros kyis sgyur ba'o////@#//rgya gar skad du /ar+ya d+ha ki ni badzra pan sha tsa ri sa hA ra man ta la ma ha;tan tra ra dza kal pha na ma //bod skad du /'phags pa mkha' 'gro ma rdo rje
41a3(rgyud): gur zhes bya ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dkyil 'khor sdus pa'i rtogs pa/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma la phyag 'tshalo//mkha' dang bebs po gsal ba dang//gsal ba me dang rab dbyed dang//sna
41a4(rgyud): tshogs rdo rje zhi gnas dang//sems can khams dang yid rol dang//'jig rten seMs can dang snod dang//sa 'og sa stengs sa bla dang/bar snang yid dang lus dang ngag//gcig dang gnyis dang mkha'
41a5(rgyud): bde dang//drug dang lnga dang drug gnyis dang//'gro ba rnaMs ni bzhi po dang//nyi shur gcig gis ma tshang dang//khams gsuM po ni ma lus dang//pha dang bu dang ma yang rung//phyi rol don dang
41a6(rgyud): nang dang yang//btsun mo kun dang chos dang ni//chos thams cad la yang bdag med dang//'bras bu rgyu dang bya ba shes//bsgrub bya bsgrub pa rdo rje bdag//'di ltar kun bzhi rnam shes dang//chos kyi dbyings dang ma
41a7(rgyud): skyes dngos//sems can khaMs dang mkha' khams dang//bdag med bdag gi 'du shes dang//dngos dang bral yang dngos med min//bde ba can dang gru gsum dang//rdul med dang ni skyon bral
41a8(rgyud): yang;rdul bral dang ni sdig bcom dang//rang bzhin rba rlabs med pa dang//mtha'i bde ba bde rig dang//gsang ba mchog dang dgyes pa dang//thams cad bdag nyid bdag bzhugs dang//kun dngos
41b1(rgyud): sangs rgyas sems dpa'o//'di ni bcom ldan rang 'byung ste//mkha' 'gro ma ni rdo rje gur//lhag pa'i lha ni gcig bu'o//dges dor 'bum lnga'i bdag nyid las//dkyil 'khor rnam pa lnga gsung+sa
41b2(rgyud): te;rdo rje can dang rtag pa dang//de bzhin rdo rje'i gzi brjid nyid//pad ma gar dbang phyug gi dang//rta'i rgyal po lnga pa'o//rdo rje mkha' 'gro'i dkyil 'khor du//mgo ri tso ri pe ta li//'ga' ma ri dang
41b3(rgyud): pu ka si//sha pa ri dang tsan Ta TaM phi mo//rdo rje rtag pa'i dkyil 'khor du//skam pa mo dang zhags ldan nyid//nya dol mo dang lcags kyu 'dzin//me tog lasogs dgra'i char//rdo rje nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor
41b4(rgyud): du;nyi phyag ma dang mar me ma//rin chen sgron ma klog phyag ma//sgyeg mo lasogs dgra'i char//pad ma gar dbang dkyil 'khor du//pad ma ma dang chos 'byung ma//lcags sgrog ma dang sdems ma dang//kling
41b5(rgyud): bu lasogs sgra'i char//rang rang dbyibasu sbyar bar bya'o//rta'i rgyal po'i dkyil khor du//rdo rje sgo lcags lde mig ma//sgo 'dzin ma dang yol 'dzin ma//spyan bsogs de bzhin dgra rnamasu//
41b6(rgyud): rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma rnaMs kun//g.yon pa'i zhabs rnaMs rkyang b+Ra bzhugs//snying khar khu tshur g.yon sdig 'dzub//sna tshogs kha dog cher 'bar ma//shin du zhe sdang zhe sdang che//mgo ri
41b7(rgyud): lasogs rab tu grags//shin du gti mug rmongs chen phyir//skam pa lasogs cher 'dzin ma'o//shin tu gzi brjid cher bar bas//nyi ma lasogs rab tu grags//shin du 'dod chags chags chen
41b8(rgyud): chen phyir//pad bsogs chos kyi dkyil du'o//'phra dog chen po sgrib chen phyir//sgo lcags ma bsogs myur 'gror ro//rgyal ba mgon po rtag pa ni//gti mug la thams cad mdzad pa'o//rdo rje nyi ma rgyal
42a1(rgyud): @#//chen po|rnam par rtog pa'i rab rib 'joms//'dod pa chen po'i gar dbang ni//chags ldan rnams kyi byang chub tshol//rang 'byung rdo rje 'phra dog che//theg pa gzhan ni spangs pa'o||rdo rje
42a2(rgyud): sems dpa' rgyal chen ni mi skyod ye shes 'byung gnas te//skye 'gro mi zad 'gron gnyer phyir//bsgrib lnga ni 'joms pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa rnam dag pa//rdo rje zhe bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//zhe
42a3(rgyud): sdang gti mug nga rgyal dang//skye bo chags ldan ser sna can//bsdu phyir rdo rje can 'khor lo//du ba'i rnam par dga' bar 'gyur//shin tu bdug cing drag pa la//zhi bas phan par mi 'gyur ba//de ltar rdo
42a4(rgyud): rje can gzigs te//rang nyid dges pa'i rdo rje mdzad//shin tu stong par zhen rnams dang//med pa'i rtul shugs zin rnams kyi//don du sangs rgyas gti mug 'jug/rdo rje can rdzogs
42a5(rgyud): sangs rgyas 'gyur//shin du nga rgyal bdug pa rnams//skye 'gro'i nga rgyal zad mdzad pa//rdo rje nyi rgyal rang nyid ni//nga rgyal chen po'i mun pa 'joms//skye bo chags bral byang
42a6(rgyud): chub spangs//seMs can gang zhig bde byed par//chags pas byang chub rab bsgrub phyir//pad ma gar dbang phyug du gyur//kun mkhyen lam la gnas nas ni//gang zhig lam gzhan du 'gro ba'i//de'i byang chub
42a7(rgyud): rab sbyin phyin//mnga' bdag rdo rje rang 'byung nyid//dbang po gang dang gang lam nyid//de de'i rang zhin phyir ni 'gro//phan pa mchog gi sbyor ba yis//sangs rgyas kun kyi rang bzhin byed//gang dang gang gi bsam pa
42a8(rgyud): yis|mi rnaMs kyis ni yid sbyar nas//des de'i dngos 'gro na//nor bu sna tshogs gzugs ji bzhin//gang dang gang gi 'dul ba yis//sems can rnaMs ni 'dul bar 'gro//de dang de'i rnam pa yis//chos ni ston
42b1(rgyud): par mdzad pa'o//thugs kyi rdo rje las byung ba'i//sems can sna tshogs rang bzhin can//sems ni rang gi rnam rtog gi//dri mas skye bo sbags par byas//de phyir kun tu rab 'bad pas//thugs kyi rdo rje rnam par
42b2(rgyud): sbyang//sems dag pa ni bde ba yin//nyon mongs dug ni thams cad 'joms//gal te stong pa thabs yin na//de tshe sangs rgyas nyid mi 'gyur//rgyu las 'bras bu gzhan med phyir//thabs ni stong pa nyid ma yin
42b3(rgyud): lta ba rnaMs las log rnams dang//bdag tu lta ba tshol rnaMs kyi//bdag du 'dzin pa bzlog pa'i phyir//rgyal ba rnams kyis stong pa bstan//de phyir dkyil 'khor 'khor lo zhes//bde ba'i thabs kyi sdoM
42b4(rgyud): pa ste//sangs rgyas nga rgyal rnal 'byor gyis//sangs rgyas nyid yun mi ring 'gyur//ston pa sum bcu rtsa gnyis mtshan//mnga' bdag dpe' byed rgya bcur ldan//de phyir thabs des bsgrub bya ste//thabs ni
42b5(rgyud): stong pa'i gzugs can no//rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog tu rgya che ba sems can 'jug pa'i le'u ste dang po'o////de nas rdo rje khams su ma khyab//rnal 'byor ma ni rdo rje gur;da
42b6(rgyud): tshig mchod pa yid 'ong ba//rang gi 'khor la bka' stsal pa//klu ni rdo rje can la dbul//sangs rgyas la ni gar dbulo//de bzhin dri ni nyi ma'o//bza' btung 'jig rten dbang phyug la'o//rta mchog la ni
42b7(rgyud): gos mchod de//de ni dam tshig dam pa'o//rdo rje mchod pa byas pa yis//rdo rje can kun mnyes par byed//a ka ru nag dges rdo rjer//btsan tan dkar po rtag pa la//'jig rten gtso bo de nyid dmar//kha che
42b8(rgyud): rdo rje snang ldan la//rta'i rgyal po thams cad de//hU~M rdo rje las byung ba'i//dri'i sbyor ba 'di yis ni//sangs rgyas mchod cing thob par 'gyur//kha dang tsha ba rdo rjer dbul//mngar ba rdo rje rtag pa la//skyur ba rdo rje
43a1(rgyud): //nyi ma la'o//de bzhin lan tsa 'jig rten rtsor//rta'i rgyal po thams cad dam//ska ba rab tu rtag par bya'o//rdo rje 'dzin pa dges dor la//shing skyes me tog nag po yi//ma li ka dang ma
43a2(rgyud): la te//kun da sangs rgyas rtag la dbul//ud pa la dang dri ma med//gsal byed la ni nges par sbyar//ud pa la dmar dang chu skyes dang//ban du dmar dang pa ta la//pad ma gar dbang phyug la
43a3(rgyud): dbul//rta mchog rdo rje can la kun//rdo rje can gi khru gsum ste//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor khru bzhi pa//khru lnga pa ni snang byed la'o//gar dbang phyug gi khru drug pa//rta'i rgyal po khru bdun pa'o//za na rdo
43a4(rgyud): rje nyi ma la'o//lam du 'jug na rta mchog la'o//sangs rgyas 'gon la gnyid log na//gar dbang phyug gi bde ba na'o//klu gar de bzhin rol mo dang //sa bzhi yongasu 'dzin dusu//rdo rje sku
43a5(rgyud): ru gnas byas te//gang gi rnal 'byor rab tu bsgrub//in tra ni la rdo rje ste//zla ba nor bu thub chen du//nor bu dmar gsal kha' mdzod kyi//pad ma rag spyan ras gzigs ma
43a6(rgyud): ra ka ta rta'i steng //rin chen sna lnga'i bdul chon mchog//de nas brtsam ldan rdo rje can 'chang //chen po can mkha' 'gro rdo rje gur//'byung po rnaMs kyi 'jigs chen po//zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la bzhugs
43a7(rgyud): te/dam tshig yang dag gsal bar mdzad//sa bon kun tu bzang po'i bdag//in tra ni la'i 'od dang mnyam//'jigs pa chen po phyag bzhi pa//'khro bo'i rol pa'i gzugs can te//dbang ldan 'byung
43a8(rgyud): po la gnan te//g.yas na rdo rje 'dzin pa'o//phyag g.yon sdigs par mdzad pa ste//gnyis po bdag gi phyag rgyar sbyor//zhabs g.yon rkang pas yang dag gnas//mchi ba ba cung zad brtsigs par
43b1(rgyud): mdzad//mgo bo'i 'phreng ba'i 'o dod ldan//dbu skra 'jigs shing zla ched ldan//ri bo'i rtser ni sgom byas nas//'byung po rnams kyi 'jigs chen 'grub//gnod sbyin 'byung po du ma dang //klu'i bu mo gnod
43b2(rgyud): sbyin mo//mi mo lha'i mdzes ma rnams//bsgrub pa po la 'grub par 'gyur//sngags ni;A+oM bud ta da ma ra b+hU ta/i ti pa te;a k+sho bya ti rI Ti na/sarba b+hu ta;tre ta bi shA tsa na;sa da ya 'ahU~M phaT zhes pa'o//
43b3(rgyud): de nas rdo rje 'dzin na rdo rje//mkha' 'gro ma ni rdo rje'i gur//stobs chen ting 'dzin la bzhugs te//char chen rab du 'bebs par mdzad//char pa med na stobs chen las//chu'i tshogs ni dbab par bya//pad
43b4(rgyud): ma 'dab brgyad bsam byas la//de nas klu brgyad 'god par bya//stobs po che ni rab sbyar nas//de ltar rnga mar rab tu gnan//kha dog nag po cher 'bar ba//'jigs yang ni 'jigs par mdzad;;
43b5(rgyud): phyag brgyad pa la zhal bzhi pa//rdo rje mkha' lding bdan du mdzad//phyag rnams kyis ni klu bdag bsnams//yige pu ta'i sngags bzlos shing//hU~M gnyis kyis ni bar bcug ste//phaT ni mthar
43b6(rgyud): ru sbyar byas la'o//dang po'i zhal ni nag po ste//gnyis zla ba'i 'od ltar mdzes//gsum pa kha dog ser po'i 'od//bzhi pa dmar bar brjod pa'o//rdo rje sems dpas rnam brgyan cing//sbrul gi
43b7(rgyud): brgyan gyis yan lag brgyan//klu rnaMs kyis ni char dbab phyir//stobs po che ni rab sgom bya'o//de nas rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//sangs rgyas kun kyi rang bzhin 'chang//rdo rje gur ni gsung ba'o//sangs rgyas de nyid rab
43b8(rgyud): gsal mdzad//rdo rje pad mo 'o byas shing//ri bor can ni zos pa dang//rdo rje chu mchog mthung byed pa'o//sangs rgyas nyid mkhyen 'di nga'i yin//dag pa'i gnas dang gnas chen po//sangs rgyas kun kyis rab gang ba;;
44a1(rgyud): @#//rdo rje rin chen kun byas par//gang 'gyur nga ni 'chad pa yin//rtag pa'i grong khyer yid 'ong bar//rdo rje lug rgyud mkha' 'gro ma//sdug kun che phyir sangs rgyas kun//rdo rje lcags sgrog gis
44a2(rgyud): bcings nas//mnyes dang ldan par g.yo 'gyur te//sangs rgyas rnams dang 'dod bya thob//rgyal ba mgon po byams pa ni//ye shes spyan gyis rnam par gzigs//rdo rje lug gu rgyud bcings pas//rgyal ba'i tshogs
44a3(rgyud): rnaMs bcing bar bya'o//gang zhig dngos grub mi ster na//lcags kyu yin ni bcings te dgug//rdo rje lcags kyu 'gu ri che//rdo rje zhags pa co'u ri ma//rdo rje lcags sgrog pe ta li//rdo rje dril bu g+ha sma ri//
44a4(rgyud): sangs rgyas rnams kyi dbang mdzad ma'o//bzlas dang sgom la mngon dga' ba'i//dngos grub gal te mi ster na//de tshe mkha' 'gro ma bzhi yis//sangs rgyas rnams la stobs kyis bsgrub//
44a5(rgyud): de nas rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//mkha' 'gro ma ni rdo rje gur//'bum lnga da ga du nges bsdus dang //mtha' yas rgyud du rab tu phye//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor 'bum khrag gcig//rdo rje can gis
44a6(rgyud): yang de bzhin 'bum//rdo rje nyi ma'i 'buM gis ste//rdo rje chos kyi yang 'bum gyis ste//'buM phyed kyis ni rdo rje rta//'byung po 'jigs chen stong phrag lngas//las kyi tshogs ni lnga yis so//ku ru ku
44a7(rgyud): le'i khri gsum gyis//lnga'i sangs rgyas kyi gar//lhag ma stong phrag lnga yis ni//rgyud kun cho ga'i rim pa'o//mkha' 'gro dgyes pa rdo rje dang //mkha' 'gro mar ni bcas pa rnaMs//'bum khrag lnga yis
44a8(rgyud): de bsduso//de nas blo ldan rdo rje 'dzin//mkha' 'grom ni rdo rje gur//sangs rgyas gar kyi ting 'dzin bzhugs//gar dang klu la rab zhen pa'i//sems can rnaMs ni bsdu ba'i phyir//sangs rgyas gar
44b1(rgyud): gi rol pa mdzad//sangs rgyas gar kyi rdo rje yis//'dod chags can gyi byang chub bsgrub//de nas rdo rje can byon te//me tog snyil ba rab sbyar nas//sems can kun la phan 'dogs pa'i//dam
44b2(rgyud): tshig der ni gshad par bya//sems can rnaMs ni bsdus pa yis//bsdus pa'i sems can ji snyed pa//sgong skyes dngal nas skyes pa dang//drod sher las skyes rdzus skye 'am//gzugs can rnaMs sam
44b3(rgyud): gzugs med dam//'du shes can nam 'du shes ni//med pa ma yin pa rnaMs dang//'du shes med pa rnams sam//nga yis sems de dag kun//phyag rgya chen po'i go 'phang la//rab tu dgod par bya'o zhes;;
44b4(rgyud): dam tshig che gshad de nas ni//mig yon mi g.yo 'gon phyung ste//mi skyo da ye shes mnyam ldan pa//ston pa la ni rnam brjod do//su khyod dam tshig yi ge hU~M//'o na thub chen 'da' 'phen nam//bdag
44b5(rgyud): min dpal ldan thugs rje stobs//stobs bcom min na su//su khyod da dam tshig yi ge oM//thub pa skyes mchog bram ze 'am khyod;;bdag min rnam par snang mdzad dpal//khyod ni me tsau
44b6(rgyud): 'dod bral lam//su khyod dam tshig yi gi oM//mkha' lding mkhyen nam mnga' bdag su//bdag min gar gi dbang phyug dpal//khyod ni gar makhan gzha' rgad mkhan//su khyod dam tshig yige traM//khyod
44b7(rgyud): ni rtugs te tsha ba mjoms//bdag min rdo rje nyid pal mtshan//'khor lo'i rnam khyod ji ltar min//su khyod dam tshig ye ge oM khaM//khyod ni stong pas bkris pa'o//bdag min rdo rje 'gyogs 'gro yin//khyod ni
44b8(rgyud): rta ste pha rol 'gro//su khyod dam tshig snying rje che//e ma mdzes ma'i chang 'chong ma//bdag min mi g.yo ma ma ki//zhang po'i khyim 'dzin ma 'am su//su khyod dam tshig zhi ba nyid//bud med g.yo sgyu 'dzin
45a1(rgyud): //pa su//bdag min lha mo spyan ma yin//mig sde g.yo ba dang bcas sam//su khyod dam tshig 'dod chags che//smad 'tshong ma la su smrar yod//bdag min lha mo gos dkar mo//ji
45a2(rgyud): ltar dkar mor 'dzin pa min//su khyod dam tshig dbang chen mo//btsun mo'i pho nya ma 'am su//mig g.yon dag ni sgrol ma'o//mig bcos ma ste long ba 'am su//mig yon dag ni nor dang ldan;;
45a3(rgyud): khrel 'dzem med pa nyid legaso//khrel med sangs rgyas nyid ma yin//tshig nyung lha dang mthun pa yin//de nas rdo rje gcod pa po//mig yon gis ni rab gsungs pa//rdo rje sems dpa' sdang ba che;;
45a4(rgyud): rmongs pa thub pa rnam snang mdzad//'dod la zhen pa 'jig rten rtso//gnam sems rdo rje snang byed yin//skyon kun byed pa don yod ste//'dren pa lnga po skyon ldan no//sgong skyes dang la nas skyes
45a5(rgyud): pa 'am//dod skyes de bzhin du//gzugs can rnams sam gzugs med dam//rdzus te skye ba nyid rnams ni//thams cad sangs rgyas bya'o zhes//de ltar khyed kyis gsungs pa las//
45a6(rgyud): dang por sangs rgyas khyod 'gyur na//de rnaMs ci ste rgyas ma 'gyur//des na de phyir rdzun kyi tshig//ji ltar mnga' bdag gis gsungs te//sems can rnaMs la sangs rgyas nyid//gang slus khyod ni ngo
45a7(rgyud): mtshar che//'chi ba'i tshe'aM//mu ge 'am//bas kyi rgol ba'i gnod pa dang//rab gnas dkyil 'khor sbyin sreg dang//char mi dbab dang zhi ba la//sngags dang sgom par ldan pa ni//gang zhig nga ni rtog rol na;;de'i
45a8(rgyud): 'dun du nga 'dug ste//de la log par nga mi 'gyur//slob dpon nga dang lha mo bzhi//mi g.yo'i bdag nyid brtsod pa'o//dkyil 'khor khang pa'i gnasu ni//sangs rgyas gar la rol bar bya'o//khaMs bzhi de
45b1(rgyud): bzhin rtsa ba dang//'ji ba'i yang rgyan rnaMs kyis ni//sangs rgyas rnams kyi rol mo gar//bde zhing dga' dang bcas par bya//chos 'byung grong khyer myams dga' bar//klu ni dam tshig rdo rje ste//gar gyi rdo rje 
45b2(rgyud): goms pas so//rdza rnga skyegs bsogs tha mar ste//dngos grub chen po rab ster ba//'di ni gsang ba chen po'o//rdo rje sems dpas gsung bzhin du//rab tu 'bad pas srung bar bya'o//klu sna tshogs dang
45b3(rgyud): rol mo'i sgra dang//gar dang 'jigs pa chen po 'jug pa dang//char dbab pa dang/mi g.yo ba'i bdag nyid chen po stan pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//mkha' 'gro ma ni rdo rje
45b4(rgyud): gur;;rdo rje gur gi ting 'dzin bzhugs//'byung po chen po'i gtor ma gsungs//namakha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//pad ma 'dab brgyad bsgom byas la//dbang po shar kyi 'dab mar bsam//mthar
45b5(rgyud): byed lho ru dgod par bya//chu bdag nub kyi 'dab mar ro//byang du gnod sbyin dgod par bya//mer ni sbyin za chen po ste//de bzhin ro zan bden bral du'o//rlung du rlung nyid bsam par bya//'byung
45b6(rgyud): po'i rtso bo dbang ldan du'o//zla nyi tshangs lasogs pa dang//tshangs ris kyi ni dam pa dang//tshangs pa 'dun na 'don nyid dang//dga' ldan mtha' bral ba rnams dang//rgyal po chen po bzhi rnams dang//gzhan
45b7(rgyud): 'phrul dbang byed pa rnams dang//gang zhig 'phrul dga'i lha rnaMs dang//dag pa'i gnas na gang gnas dang//bde ba can der 'dug rnams dang//'og min na ni gnas rnams dang//'od zer can gi 'jig rten khaMs
45b8(rgyud): sum bcu rtsa gsum na gnas pa rnaMs//chos 'byung nang du son pa ste//thams cad steng du bsam par bya//stobs kyi rgyu dang dung skyong dang//de bzhin mtha' yas nor rgyas bu//'jog po dang ni rigs ldan nyid;
46a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pad chen 'dab ma bzang po can//lha min sems can dmyal ba rnaMs//chos 'byung gnas su 'og du'o//A+o~M las tshangs la sogs pa ste//hU~M las duM bu blangs la sogs//a las stobs
46a2(rgyud): kyi rgyu bsogs pa'o//lag gnyis gdod cing can rnams te//steng du lha rnams dkar po che//dbang po la sogs khyab 'jug 'dra//stobs kyi rgyu bsogs dmar po che//rang 'od rig ma lus sprad ldan||
46a3(rgyud): rdo rje dril bu lag pa brel//g.yas rkyang pas ni bstan la gnas//tshangs bsogs rtag pa'i phyag rgyas bdab//rdo rje rgya byin bsogs pa la//stobs kyi rgyu bsogs gar kyi rtso//thams cad rigs gsuM
46a4(rgyud): gnasu skye//ser mos dgug par byas nas ni//chom rkun ma'i ( )zhags pas bcug/ro langs mas ni sgrog gis bcings//za nus dril bus rab byas te//de nas mchod yon zhabs 
46a5(rgyud): gsil dbul//sngags 'di yis ni mnyes par bya//A+o~M A hU~M//de nas re shig mthor thung dbul//sngags 'di yin ni gshegasu gsol//A+o~M badzra sad twa sa ma ya/ma nu pa la ya;Ti to me
46a6(rgyud): b+ha ba su po Sho me b+ha ba/su to Sho mi b+ha ba/shwa sho to mi b+ha ba/a nu rag to me b+ha ba/hri ta ya me a ti ti sta/sarba sid ti ma me pra yang tsa/sarba kar ma su tsa me/shu mi tsi ta shri yang ;ku ru hUM ha ha ha ha ho ba ga ban
46a7(rgyud): sarba ta thA ta ga ta ma me mun tsa ma badzra b+ha ba ma ha sa ma ya sad twa a mu zhes pas swahA//de nas rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//mkha' 'gro ma ste rdo rje'i gur//ting 'dzin rdo rje ming bzhugs te//rdo rje ming ni gsal bar
46a8(rgyud): mdzad|ming gis dbang skur dusu ni //ming gis slob ma rnam dbang skur//rdo rje ming gis dbang skur nas//skye ba 'dir ni sangs rgyas nyid//rdo rje gzhad pa dang/rdo rje rnam par sgyeg pa dang/rdo rje ldem pa
46b1(rgyud): dang;rdo rje rol pa dang/rdo rje bde ba dang/rdo rje pad ma'i myu gu dang/rdo rje a la la dang/rdo rje pad ma'i lcags kyu dang/rdo rje ye shes dang/rdo rje yid bzhin nor bu dang/rdo rje yan lag med pa dang/rdo rje
46b2(rgyud): shes rab nyu gu dang/rdo rje mchog du dga' ba dang/rdo rje dga' ba bde ba dang/rdo rje snang bar byed ba dang/rdo rje 'bebs pa dang/rdo rje namakha' dang/rdo rje pha rol phyin pa dang/rdo rje chags pa ste/rdo rje'i ming
46b3(rgyud): de rnaMs ni dag pa'i gnas /phyag rgya chen po grub pa rnaMs kyi'o//bud med rnams kyi 'byung ste/rdo rje snyems ma dang/rdo rje mtshon cha ma dang/rdo rje e ma'o bde ma dang/rdo rje bdag med ma dang/rdo rje spyan ma dang
46b4(rgyud): rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma dang/rdo rje ser mo dang/rdo rje sgyu 'phrul ma dang/rdo rje 'bar ma dang/rdo rje lcug ma mo dang/rdo rje ro langs mo dang/rdo rje lcags kyu ma dang/rdo rje 'phreng sgor ma dang/rdo rje 'phreng ba mo dang/
46b5(rgyud): rdo rje sgyeg mo dang/rdo rje gzhang ma mo dang/rdo rje sdams ma dang/rdo rje kha gyes ma dang/rdo rje pad ma mo dang/rdo rje 'byung gnas ma dang /rdo rje gos gtor ma dang /rdo rje chags pa mo dang/
46b6(rgyud): rdo rje sgrol ma dang /rdo rje do shal ma dang/rdo rje rnam par sgyeg ma dang/rdo rje blo ldan mo dang/rdo rje kong ka mo dang/rdo rje kha chu ma dang/rdo rje dbugs 'byin ma dang/rdo rje lag pa mo ni mkha'
46b7(rgyud): 'gro ma rnaMs kyi 'o//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi mtshan du gyur pa 'byung ste//rdo rje sems dpa' dges dar rdo rje//rang 'byung rnam snang chen po che//rdo rje a ra li thugs rjes 'gro//de
46b8(rgyud): bzhin rdo rje khro bo nyid//rdo rje bdud rtsi bdud rtsi 'khyil//he ru ka ni snying rje stobs//rtag pa'i rdo rje gti mug ste//sna tshogs 'gyur ba rnam snang mdzad//a yig sgyu 'phrul dra ba yang//rdo rje
47a1(rgyud): //skur ni rab tu grags//sa'i snying po gti mug che//tho bas dbang phyug gshin rje gshed//de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub//sangs rgyas zhes bya mtshan du grags//rdo rje nyi ma 'ang rin chen
47a2(rgyud): rtso//de bzhin rin chen rgyal po nyid//rdo rje gzi brjid rgyal po che//rdo rje nyi ma 'ang snang byed do//rta 'grin 'jig rten tso bo dang//spyan ras gzigs dang rdo rje chos//pad ma gar dbang 'dod chags dang
47a3(rgyud): 'jig rten 'gon por rab tu grags//rdo rje 'dzin dang rta mchog dang ni rta rgyal dang//rta bdun rgyal dang rta rgyal dpal//rdo rje de bzhin rta mchog dang//de bzhin rdo rje las nyid dang//don yod dang ni don yod grub//
47a4(rgyud): rdo rje myur 'gror rab tu grags//rdo rje 'jin dang rdo rje sems//rnam rgyal phyag na rdo rje dang//dpe' med ro mnyam bde ba dang//dam pa dang po bde chen po//chags dang zhe sdang gti mug dang//de bzhin
47a5(rgyud): dam tshig seMs nor bu//de rnaMs rigs rnaMs drug du brjod//'dod pa thar pa rab bsgrub pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mtshan mchog tu bstan pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////
47a6(rgyud): rdo rje snying po rtag pa'i rgyal po'o//sgyu 'phrul rtag pa gnyis pa'o//rdo rje gar ni gsum pa ste//bzhi pa sna tshogs rtag pa zhes//rdo rje rtag pa lnga pa'o//rtag pa drug pa stobs po che//rtag pa bdun pa mi g.yo
47a7(rgyud): 'gon//gzungs kyi rtag pa brgyad pa ste//dgu pa mkha' 'gro ku lu la//'jig rten rtag pa bcu pa'o//rnam par snang mdzad bcu gcig pa//dngos grub rtag pa bcu gnyis pa'o//sgrol ma'i rtag pa bcu
47a8(rgyud): gsum pa'o//rdo rje phag mo bcu bzhi pa'o//bcwo lnga pa ni gzi brjid che//bdag med rtag pa bcu drug pa//bcu bdun pa ni gshin rje gshed//hU~M mdzad kyi ni bcwo brgyad pa'o//so sor 'phreng ma bcu dgu pa
47b1(rgyud): sbyin sreg brtag pa nyi shu pa//rab gnas nyi shu gcig pa ste//dkyil 'khor chen po rtag pa ni nyi shu rtsa gnyis pa ru brjod//gtor ma'i rtag pa nyi shu gsuM//nyi shu bzhi pa sta gon no//mngon par rtog pa nyi shu lnga;
47b2(rgyud): de bzhin phyag rgya nyi shu drug//byed pa nyi shu bdun pa'o//klu ni nyi shu brgyad pa ste//gar kyi dbang phyug rtag pa ni//sum bcur gcig gis ma tshang dang//rtag pa suM bcu pa yang gshad//stong phrag sum bcu'i
47b3(rgyud): grangs kyis ni//mkha' 'gro mkha' 'gro ma rnaMs kyi//rgya mtsho chen po 'bum phrag lnga//dges pa rdo rje ('dzin)'dzin//e ma ho rgya chen rgyud bstan pa'o//'bum lnga dges pa'i rdo rje las//snying po 'di ni yongasu bsdus
47b4(rgyud): pa'o//'di ni rgyud kyi des pa ste//rdo rje mkha' 'gros gsungs pa'o//dgyes pa'i rdo rje rnal 'byor rgyud//dang por rgyal ba rnaMs kyis gshad//phyi nas rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud//de nyid mo gzugs bsdu phyir ro//bcoM
47b5(rgyud): ldan 'das ji ltar na spros pa las bsdus pa lags/bskal pa bzang po 'di la byang chub sems dpa' rnams dang/so so'i skye bo rnams tshe thung bar 'gyur te/ji ltar de rnaMs kyis 'dzin 
47b6(rgyud): nus par 'gyur/ji ltar de rnams kyis thos par 'gyur/ji ltar snyen bkur nus par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das so so'i skye bo rnams/ji ltar tshe thung bar 'gyur ba lags//rdo rje 'dzin pas
47b7(rgyud): bka' stsal pa/gang rnas slob dpon du gyur ba de srid du/de rnams rtsa ba'i ltung ba can du 'gro bar 'gyur ba dang/gang dag 'jug pa de rnaMs chogs 'dod pa la (gcig du)mngon par zhen cing mngon du zhen par 'gyur te/
47b8(rgyud): de'i phyir na rgyud thams cad kyi rgya cher bstan pa ma yino//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rdo rje 'dzin pa'i rdo rje'i gsung gsan te/cang mi gsung ba thob par gyur nas tshig 'di gsung so;;
48a1(rgyud): @#//dur khrod dang ni chu 'gram dang//nags tshal 'bab chu grong khyer du//snying rje chen po dges rdo rje//nga snyen bkur la dngos grub ster//mtsho skyes rnam pa'i mchog 'gro dang//sku gdung mchod rten ba
48a2(rgyud): zab mo dang//rkang skyob gsil ba'i 'og tu ni//da ni sangs rgyas dngos grub ster//chong dus dang ni grong rdal nyid//yang thog rnam pa'i khang pa ru//rdo rje nyi ma nga rtag cing//gus ldan rnams la dngos grub ster//
48a3(rgyud): rgyun du klu dang gar byas dang//tshang skyong ma'i khyim rnaMs su//pad ma gar dbang nga nga 'di ni//gus ldan rnams la dngo grub ster//myur 'gro rtag tu gzhon 'gyur cing//btsun bcung bzad lam gron ba na//don
48a4(rgyud): yod rta ni rtag byas na//rdo rje rnaMs kyis dngos grub ster//de nas bcom ldan dor 'dzin che//mkha' 'gro ma ni rdo rje'i gur//rnam rgyal phyag rgya'i ting 'dzin bzhugs//phyag rgya phyag mtshan yang dag gsung+sa
48a5(rgyud): sku gsung thugasu rtag pa'i phyir//phyag rgya rnam gsuM shes par bya//sku'i lag pa'i phyag rgya yin//sngags bzlos ngag gi phyag rgya'o/thugs kyi mtshan nyid phyag rgya yin//de nyid bsam gtan dkyil
48a6(rgyud): 'khor la'o//phyag g.yas na ni skam pa ste//de bzhin g.yon na sdig 'dzub po//skam pa ma drag mkha' 'gro ma//bdug pa rnaMs kyi khur ba 'don//phyag g.yas na ni zhags pa ste//de bzhin bdug pa rnam par
48a7(rgyud): 'gugs//zhags pa ma der shes bya ste//g.yon pas bdug la sdig 'dzub po//g.yas pa na ni nya dol ste//bdug pa bkra bar rtsom pa mo//'di ni nya dol mar gshad de//g.yon pas bdug la sda 'dzub mdzad//phyag
48a8(rgyud): g.yas na ni lcags rkyu ste//bdug pa rnams ni 'gugs par rtson//rdo rje kyo mar 'di rab gshad//g.yon pas bdug la stig 'dzub mdzad//phyag g.yas na ni nyi ma ste//gti mug mun nag rnam par sel//g.yon
48b1(rgyud): na sdig 'dzub 'dzin par mdzad//'di ni rdo rje nyi mar gshad//phyag g.yas mar me'i snod 'dzin te//de bzhin g.yon na sdig 'dzub mdzad//rnam rtog rab rib sel mdzad ma//'di de rdo rje mar mer gshad//phyag
48b2(rgyud): g.yas ni rin chen ste//sems can rnaMs la spyi ni gsol ma'o//g.yon na sdig 'dzub 'chang ma ste//'di ni rdo rje sgron mar gshad//phyag g.yas na ni klog 'khyug ste//rnam par rtog pa'i chu bo 'joms//de
48b3(rgyud): bzhin g.yon na sdig 'dzu ba ma//'di ni klog phyag mar me zhes bya//phyag g.yas ni ni pad ma ste//pad ma'i chags par brjod par bya//g.yon na sdig 'dzu bldan pa ste//pad ma'i sdig mkha' 'gro ma 'dir brjod//
48b4(rgyud): g.yas na pad ma b+ha ga ste//bde ba chen po rnam 'phel ma'o//g.yon na sdigs 'dzub 'dzin ma sde//'di ni chos 'byung mar brjod//phyag g.yas na ni lcags sgrog ste//sdug kun byang chub kun 'ching ma'o//
48b5(rgyud): g.yon na sdig 'dzub 'dzin mdzad ma//rdo rje lcags sgrog ma 'a dir brjod//sangs rgyas rjes chags 'ching pa yis//bdag nyid 'bad pas 'ching ma ste//byang chub chags pas rab
48b6(rgyud): sgrub ma//'dir ni rdo rje sdems mar gshad//phyag g.yas ni lde mig ste//ye shes blangs nas bkrol ba'o//g.yon na sdig 'dzub 'dzun ma ni//rdo rje lde mig ma 'dir bshad//phyag g.yas nas ni
48b7(rgyud): sgo mo ste//ye shes skyes pa ma bkag pa'o//g.yon na sdig 'dzug 'dzin pa mo//'di ni rdo rje sgo mor bshad//phyag g.yas na ni yol ba ste//rang 'od btsun mo bsrung ba ste//g.yon na sdig 'dzu ba 'dzin pa mo//rdo rje
48b8(rgyud): yol ba mor bshad do//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//mkha' 'gro ma'i dam pa'i tshogs//mkha' 'gro lnga po rab sgrub phyir//'kha' 'gro ma'i rgyud gsungaso//de nas byang chub sems dpa' de rnaMs thams cad mkha'
49a1(rgyud): //'gro ma'i rdo rje'i gsung thos nas 'jigs shing 'dar ba thob par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das 'di nyi tshe dam tshig du gsungs pa lags sam//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' thaMs cad la
49a2(rgyud): 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' thams cad 'jig rten gi khaMs 'od zer can zhes bya ba na//rdo rje des pa brjod pa zhes bya ba'i legs par sbyar ba yod de/'di ni de'i yin te//dbyings las
49a3(rgyud): nam mkha' 'gro ma zhes//skad kyi dbyings 'dir rnam rtags pas//thams cad mkha' la spyod grub pas//mkha' 'gro ma 'di rab gragaso//de nas byang chub sems dpa' thabs cad gsung//'di thos nas cang
49a4(rgyud): mi smra ba'i ngo bor gyur to//gyur ba de nas tshig 'di skad ces gsol to//e ma ho mkha' 'gro ma'i rgyud//rgyud rnaMs kun las dam pa ste//rtogs sla 'ang rtogs par dka' ba dang//rig na mi rig pa nyid do//de nas
49a5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa mi skyod pa lasogs pa rdo rje thaMs cad kyis byang chub sems dpa'i rnams kyis bskul pa'i phyir 'di skad ces gsungaso//bcom ldan 'das byang chu ba seMs dpa' thams
49a6(rgyud): cad rgyud kyi rgyal po rdo rje gur 'dir/rnal 'byor ma rnams dang/mkha' 'gro ma rnaMs 'dir ji ltar bsdu bar bya ba yin//byams pa lasogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnaMs kyis gsol pa
49a7(rgyud): thog mar rdo rje 'dzin pa yis//sgong skyes lasogs ma lus pa//bdag gi srid las sgral lo zhes//byang chub tu smon sngon brjod na//ci phyir btsun mo rnaMs bor bya//srog chags gsod la 'bad ldan dang
49a8(rgyud): lta ba las ni log rnams dang//btsun mo rnams ni sdu ba'i phyir//bde ba'i thabs ni rgyas min zhing//gzhung nyung ba la don rgyas pa//blo dman rab tu rtogs byed pa'i//sngags rnams kyi khyad par phyir//
49b1(rgyud): rgyud kun kyi ni snying po'i//mkha' 'gro ma'i rgyud gshad do//rdo rje nges par brjod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//'di ni gshad pa tsam gis ni//bdag med rnal 'byor ma nyid kyis//sngon du gar gis mchod
49b2(rgyud): byas te//rang gi 'khor la grub sder gsungs//sangs rgyas dang ni rdo rje sems//gal te rdo rje chos dang ni//rta mchog rdo rje nyi ma ni//bdag ni spangs ni mi 'grub po//nga nyid sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//skyed
49b3(rgyud): pa'i gzugs kyis gnas pa'o//de rnaMs kyis ni mdzes ma nga//de rnams la nga dngos grub ster//smigs mkhan dang ni rkang 'gyogs dang//ral gri sa 'og bsgrub pa dang//rilu mkha' la spyod pa dang//
49b4(rgyud): mi snang ba dang bcud kyis len//rigs 'dzin 'khor lo sgyur nyid dang//bu mo 'gugs pa mchog dang ni//bsgrub pos nga la brnyas byas pas//dngos grub rdo rje rnaMs 'thob po//'phro ba'i phyir ni
49b5(rgyud): mang por ni;;smras 'di yis ci byar yod//nga spangs grub pa med do kye//byang chub med cing sgom pa med//gang zhig nga ni rtag dran zhing//rtag tu klog cing sems dpa' dang//'phre sngag+sa
49b6(rgyud): la ni bzlos byed cing//nga'i sku ni sgom byed cing//gal te sgyu ma 'i gzugs kyis kyang//gal te rmi lam na nga mthong//shin tu bsgoms pa'i rgyun las ni//mngon gsum du yang mthong bar 'gyur;;
49b7(rgyud): gzugs brnyan la ni rab tu sbyar ba'i phyir//de'i dngos grub sbyin par bya//bde ba chen po sbyar ba'i phyir//rdo rje pad ma rab sbyor bas//gang zhig bdag la 'dod ster na//lus can de la su zhig brjod;
49b8(rgyud): rdo rje brjod pa rab sbyar zhing//gang zhig nyin gcig sgom byed na;;shes rab pha rol phyin grub pa//de ltar nga ster the tsom med//bdag nyid he ru bkar bcas la//rang gi btsun mor nga sgoms te//chag+sa
50a1(rgyud): @#//chen gyis ni 'dod spyod gang //de rnams la ni nga brnyes 'gyur//'dod pa'i yon tan lngar ldan pas//dga' ba'i rol par ldan pa dang //klu dang rol mo sna tshogs dang //'dod kun bde ba 
50a2(rgyud): thob pa dang //sna tshogs skyeg pas sbyor ba dang //sna tshogs ro'i btung ba dang //gos dkra sna tshogs kyis ldan zhing //sna tshogs dri yis dbyugs ldan pas//gang zhig rtag par da bsnyen par//de la dngos 
50a3(rgyud): grub rtag ster te//de'i 'dod ldan ma nga yin//phyag rgyas chen po'i go 'phang gnas////A+o~M a A i I A U ri rI li lI e A+e o A+o~M A~M a swahA//gang zhig 'di yis nga la ni//bskul 
50a4(rgyud): par byed pa'i rgyal sras te//de'i bla sdengs su 'dug ste//bud med sgyu ma snyoms 'jug bde//shar du rdo rje ma sbyin bya//lho ru g+ho ra'i gzugs can ma//nub tu chu'i mkha' 'gro ma//byang du rdo rje 
50a5(rgyud): mkha' 'gro ma//dbugs su zhugs pa nga nyid do//dkyil 'khor ra snying por gsol byed gang //de'i mdzes ma rtag par nga //phyag gnyis zhal ni gcig pa la//gri khug ma'i thod pas sbrel//
50a6(rgyud): stag gi pags pas rnam bkris shing //spyan gsuM gyen du skra rjes ma//phyag rgya lnga yis rnam brgyan ma'o//shar sgor ser mo chen mo ste//lhor ni chum rkun gzugs can ma//ro langs ma ni nub 
50a7(rgyud): kyi sgor//de bzhin byang sgor za nus ma'o//lha mo spos mo dbang ldan du'o//dag byed mtshams su ri drod ma//srin po'i mtshaMsu drag 'dzin ma'o//rtag 'gro ru ni sme ma'o//mtshams dang mtshaMs bral de rnaMs su//
50a8(rgyud): bkod de gang zhig nga sgom pa//de'i lag tu dpal son te//sus kyang 'phrog par nus ma yin//de kun mdog nag 'od dang ldan//yi dgas steng du gar dang bcas//dpa'o khro tshul chen mo 'chang //
50b1(rgyud): g.yon na kha traM kha 'dzin ma//de nas bcom ldan rdo rje can//mkha' 'gro ma ni rdo rje gur//ting 'dzin mchog dga' la bzhugs te//rig pa'i dbang ni yang dag gsungs//rdo rje thugs ni rab bsgoms
50b2(rgyud): pas;;rang gyi rig dang skyes bu'i chu//bum pa brgyad po thabs kyis dgang//rdo rje can bsaMs mnyes par bya'o//'di ni dbu brgyan sbyin bya ba//rdo rje nyi mar rab sgom bya'o//rdo rje tshig gshad rab sbyar nas;
50b3(rgyud): thams cad sngon bzhin bsam byas la//rdo rje chen po rtse dgu pa//rang gi lag pas snying gar bya'o//slob ma 'jig rten dbang byas la//rdo rje sems dpa' zhes dbang skur//sangs rgyas kun dbang
50b4(rgyud): rdo rje yis//de ring khyod ni dbang skur 'gyur//'di de sangs rgyas kun nyid du //rab grub phyir na rdo rje bzung//lha mo sgrol mas mkhyud ldan ma'o//slob ma rta mchog tu bsams te//lag par rdo rje
50b5(rgyud): rdo rje dril 'khrol bzhugs//rdo rje bdag pos dbang bskur bya//A+oM badzra a te pa te taM a b+hi shin tsa mi ti Sh+Ta badzra se ma yA stam zhes paso//slob ma rnam snang mdzad byas la//ming gis
50b6(rgyud): kyang ni dbang bskur bya'o//A+oM badzra sa ta taM a b+hi shin tsa ya mi//badzra na ma a b+hi shi ka ta zhes te//kyai rdo rje che ge mo rdo rje zhes so//gang gang snang ba de kun rang //de rang tso bo e ru ka;;chos
50b7(rgyud): rnaMs rang bzhin gyis dag pa//sangs rgyas ye shes gang lta ba'o//'di de sangs rgyas thams cad nyid//rdo rje sems dpa'i phyag na gnas//rdo rje snying po rtul shugs brtan//khyod kyis kyang ni
50b8(rgyud): rtag tu bzung////A+oM badzra sarba ta thA g+ha ta sid badzra sa ma ya ti shA;e sho d+ha na d+ha ra ya mi ti/hUM hi hi hi hUM//thams cad rim pas cho ga gcig//thaMs cad bla med mchod nas ni//de nas rdo rje lag
51a1(rgyud): //gnas lag|slob ma rdo rje brtul zhugs sbyin//thog mtha' med pa'i sems dpa' ni//rdo rje sems dpa' bde chen po//kun tu bzang po kun bdag nyid|rdo rje snying po bdag po'i
51a2(rgyud): bdag//de la yang ni dril bu sbyin//shes rab pha rol phyin skye bar//sems can kun gi don gi phyir//kun du rdo rje dril bu sbyin//'di de sangs rgyas thaMs cad kyi//shes rab dbyangs kyi rjes
51a3(rgyud): 'gror gshad//rgyal bas byang chub mchog brjod pa//khyod kyis kyang ni rtag par bzung //kun tu dbyugs dbyung rab byin pas//rang nyid shakya thub 'gyur te//slob ma chen po lung bstan na//sangs
51a4(rgyud): rgyas nyid 'di the tsom med//khyod 'chi 'jigs mya ngan med//de bzhin nad dang rgas pa med//bde chen bde ba dI phyir na//phyin chad 'chi ba yod ma yin//nga'i sa bu khyod lung stan 'gyur//A+oM|ngas
51a5(rgyud): 'di khyod la lung stan pas//srid pa'i nga rna 'gro las grol te//rdo rje seMs dpa' de bzhin gshegs//srid pa shin tu grub pyi ra ro//ke deng phan cad khyod sa 'og dang sa stangs dang sa bla
51a6(rgyud): na|rdo rje che ge mo zhes bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa grub po//bcom ldan 'das rig pa chen mo'i dbang bskur ba'i dam tshig gis de skad ces bka' stsal to//de nas bcom ldan rdo
51a7(rgyud): rje 'dzin che//ting 'dzin rdo rje ston pa pos//rdo rje dam tshig zhes bya ba//byung ste 'di skad bka' stsal to//bla ma rdo rje can mchod cing //bdun po rnam par dag par bya//byang chub tu ni sems bskyed
51a8(rgyud): chos kyi 'byung gnas rnam par sgom//de las gnas gsams te//rnam pa lnga po bsgom par bya//dang po skyes bu bsam de nas//mkha' 'gro ma'i 'khor lo spro//dri za rab tu zhugs pa ni//lhung ste
51b1(rgyud): zhu ba rje dran pa//'tshams kyi lha mo rnaMs kyis bskul//bskul nas 'khor lo 'dren pa ni//mi stsogs la ni gti mug bsogs//rdo rje gsum pa gnas gsuM dgod//de nas rdo rje rjes chags te//ye shes
51b2(rgyud): dngos po rab bcug la//rig ma brgyad rnams kyis dbang bskur//bdud rtsi yang ni myang bya zhing//lha mo brgyad rnaMs kyis mchod pa nyid//'khor lo'i 'dren pa la sdod bya'o//de nas yan lag drug
51b3(rgyud): bsgoms te//rang yang rang byin rlabs pa yis//byin gyis rlabs pa bya ba yin//cho ga'i go rims rim pa yis//de nas mtshon ma mtshon par bya//dang por sprin gi rnam pa ste//gnyis pa du ba
51b4(rgyud): lta bu'o//gsum po sprin bu me khyer dngos//bzhi pa mar me gsal bar bya'o//lnga pa rtag tu snang ba ni//sprin med nam mkha' lta bu'o//thams cad mkhyen pa'i rgyude sde//dngos grub des 'jug nye ba
51b5(rgyud): yin//phyi nas sgyu ma'i rnam pa lta//rmi lam rnam pa skad cig 'gyur//khams gsum rgyu dang mi rgyu ba//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//'jig rten khams ni thams cad du//ji srid
51b6(rgyud): rdo rje'i lus can rnams//ye shes phung po'i blo ldan pa//de'i rjes su thams cad 'dreng//khams gsum las ni nyams dga' bar//ji srid skye gnas las byung rnams//phyag rgya chen po rab
51b7(rgyud): sbyor bas//de rnams kun la nye bar spyad//byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//rdo rje'i tshig du thos nas ni//dga' zhing spro bar rab gyur nas//sbyin mdzad rdo rje rnamasu 'gyur//rig pa
51b8(rgyud): chen mo bdag med dang//mnga' bdag rdo rje snang mdzad dang//rgyal chen rdo rje sems dpa' dang//rta mchog thub pa ma chen po dang//rdo rje nyi ma gzi brjid rdo rje che//mnga' bdag chen po rta mchog ni//
52a1(rgyud): //lus kyi gong bu 'gul 'gyur zhing //lus dang ngag sems nyid kyang ngo//rgyal po lan cig gis bton nas//'phral du mngon gsum byed pa'o//de nas rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//sku
52a2(rgyud): gsung rdo rje mchog mchang ma//ting 'dzin rdo rje mnyam bzhugs te//gsung ni 'di skad bka' stsal to//dkyil 'khor mkha' 'gro ma dbus bzhugs//zhal brgyad pa la stobs po che//bcu drug
52a3(rgyud): phyag rnams kyis ldan zhing //a ni dra ni la dang mnyam 'od//'bar ba chen mo zhabs bzhi pa//sgyeg pas grags pa'i gzugs can te//dkyil dkrung phyed pa'i gar gis bzhugs//rus pa'i rgyan
52a4(rgyud): gyis rnam par rgyan//dang po'i zhal ni nag po la//dkar po dmar po rnam par brgyan//bdud bzhi pa la rab gnan cing //spyi bor rdo rje 'dzin mchog ste//rdo rje de la mdog sgom pa//'dod chen
52a5(rgyud): kha dog nag por ro//dbang skur ba'i gnas nyid du//sangs rgyas lngas ni rnam par brgyan//glang po rta bong ba lang dang //rnga mo mi dang seng ge sgo//de bzhin du yang byi la rnaMs//
52a6(rgyud): g.yas pa'i thod pa brgyad pa ru//gnyis mthung la sogs rim shes bya//sa dang chu dang 'arlung dang ni//gzi brjid zla ba nyi ma dang //mthar byed dang ni nor sbyin nyid//g.yon pa'i thod pa brgyad po na'o//
52a7(rgyud): skyes bu bzhengs pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//mig la gti mug rdo rje dgod//zhe sdang rdo rje rna bar ro//ser sna chags dang de bzhin reg//bdag med ma yang bsgom par bya'o//kun kyang zhal
52a8(rgyud): cig phyag gnyis 'gyur//sna tshogs gzugs ldan btsun mo rnaMs//gri khug thod pa phyag rnams sprel//srogs stong du yang dag gnas//sa spyod ma dang mkha' spyod gnas//de bzhin sa 'og gnas pa
52b1(rgyud): mo//de nyid kyi ni cho ga yis//gnas gsuM du ni rnam par bsgom//yi ge oM ni rgyal chen po//sku ni rdo rje rnam par brgyan//rgyal po chen po rdo rje thugs//rdo rje sems dpa'i gnas na bzhugs//yi ge a ni
52b2(rgyud): de bzhin mchog//ngag gi lam gyi gsal byed brjod//sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje yis//chos can rigs gsum po rnams 'byung//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//mgo' ri'i gzugs can
52b3(rgyud): bsgom par bya//lcags kyu dang ni sdig 'dzub 'dzin//kha dog dmar po'i gzugs can no//mgo' ri rdo rje can zhes ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//dkyil 'khor dmar po'i dbus gnas par//gtso ri
52b4(rgyud): gzugs can bsgom byas te//gtso ma gser gi mdog 'dra ma'o//mtshon chen rdo rje zhags pa can//rdo rje dkyil 'khor dbus gnas bar//dmar po'i 'od 'dra 'be ta li//rdo rje lcags sgrog nga 
52b5(rgyud): rgyal can//bdun//byang chub rnam par 'chang mdzad ma'o//'od kyi dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//'ag ma ri ni de nas bsgom//rdo rje dril bu phyag 'brel ma'o//ma ra ka ta'i
52b6(rgyud): mdog 'dra ma//rdo rje sgo skyong ma'i nges par bsgom pa//rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//gra ru lha mo bzhi po rnams//byang chub sbyang phyir bsgom par bya//byang chub sems de bum
52b7(rgyud): pa ni//me thab dang ni rgyal mtshan che//gnyis med ma kir rab sbyar nas//mgon po de ltar bsgom par bya//'dod chags chen po rjes chags kyis//bde ba rba rlabs med pa thob//'byung
52b8(rgyud): ba bzhi'i mchog rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//de nas rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//theg pa mchog gsum bston pa po//rnam kun 'dzin la gzhugs te//gsung ni 'di skad bka' stsal to//
53a1(rgyud): /hU~M las byung ba'i 'khro bo rnams//rtag tu zhe sdang mchog ldan de//sems kyis nang du khros byas la//rab tu 'bad pas bsgrub par bya//na ma sa man ta bud te b+h+yo//na mo
53a2(rgyud): badzra mud ka ru;ha sta ya tsan da;man Ta la kwe pa ra ya hU~M phaT//de nas khro bo che gsungs pas//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' kun//'jogs shing 'dar bar gyur nas ni//mnga' bdag
53a3(rgyud): rdo rje 'dzin dran 'gyur//mi mo'i pad ma gas chag med//sbubs gcig pa 'am sbubs gnyis pa//rig ma bzhi'i rab sbyor bas//sangs rgyas kun kyis mnyam par gang//rkang rjes rdul
53a4(rgyud): gyi cho ga yis//rkang pas gnan te de zlas bya//sku gsung skyed nasa bsgom pa yis//rtag tu bu mo dgug par bya//gshin rje gshed po rdo rje zhes//bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to/
53a5(rgyud): na ma sa man ta b+hu te b+h+yo//ma ha d+ha na d+ha pra b+ha bad+ha su ra a su ra//mar d+ha da na bu nan ne/rim ma tha hU~M phaT//de nas gzhan gis mi thub stobs//khro bo chen po de gsungs pas/
53a6(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//gzhan yang 'dar te de dran 'gyur//'byung po yi dgas sha zan rnams//grol bu dang mkha' 'gro ma//rgyas btab pa la la snyems byas
53a7(rgyud): hU~M//brjod de rdo rje gzir bas bde//gzhan gyis mi thub pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//na ma sa man ta b+hud te b+h+yo/hu lu hu lu/ma ha pad ma a /mi ti ku ru bi shan a//dug
53a8(rgyud): chen po ni dgug byas shing//de bzhin rgyal ba slang bar bya'o//pad ma snyems pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nga 'dzin to//na ma sa ma na ta bud de b+h+yo/na ma badzra kro ta ya ma ha dang sh+Tod od ka ta;
53b1(rgyud): b+he ra b+ha ya/badzra yan d+ho ta;d+ha ra pa ya;A+o~M a mri ta kun d+ha li hU~M phaT////de nas khro bo che gshad pa//bgegs 'joms rdo rje 'chang ba ste//sangs rgyas kun dang sems dpa' che//yid
53b2(rgyud): ni dga' ba med par 'gyur//rlung khyer ras ni rab blangs te//bsgrub bya'i ming yang rab bzang nas//de'i rkang rjes rdul byas pa'i//tshog bdag rkang pa'i 'og tu bcug//rdo rje bdud rtsi'i ting 'dzin
53b3(rgyud): hU~M phaT yig brjod hU~M byas te//khro bo'i sngags kyis rab sbyor gyis//sangs rgyas nyid kyang rab sbyor gyis//sangs rgya nyid kyang bskrod par 'gyur//bdud rtsi rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//
53b4(rgyud): shin tu sbas pa rnamasu ni//bskul ba 'di ni rab tu bya'o//ye shes rdo rjes byas nas ni//nyi ma bdun gyis 'grub par 'gyur//na ma sa man ta bud de b+h+yo;A+o~M ya ma d+ha na d+ha ya ti nI la tan ta ya;
53b5(rgyud): tu ru tu ru;mu ru mu ru;sarba du sta na ma ra ya hU~M phaT//de nas khro chen dbyiga sngon po//ar tshar che ldan gsung tsam gyis//sems dapa' sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//srog kyang
53b6(rgyud): 'don par byed yin te//bdug pa rnams ni lhung ba yin//rab tu sbyor ba'i cho gas bskul//bsgom zhing rgya phrag brgyad du ni//bzlas na shin tu 'grub pa yin pa yin//dbyig pas
53b7(rgyud): mnan pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge'adzin to//na ma sa man ta b+hud d+he b+h+yo//na mo ma h+ya b+ha la ya;u tsas ma kro ta ra tsa ya rban gA nAM hri ta ya ni tra hU~M phaT//de nas bdum po gsungs pa yis//dug dong 'jig+sa
53b8(rgyud): shing stobs bral te//'jigs shing 'dar bar gyur nas ni//stobs po che ni dran par 'gyur//rdo rje stobs kyis snang bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//na ma sa mnata ;b+hu te b+h+yo;;
54a1(rgyud): //na mo a tsa la ka na tse ta ta/mo Ta mo Ta/tu Ta tu Ta/g+ha g+ha g+ha da ya/g+ha ta ya g+ha ta ya hU~M phaT//de nas mi g.yo ar tsam che//rdo rje khro bo gsungs pa yis//lha dang klu dang gnod sbyin
54a2(rgyud): che//rmongs te sa la 'gyel bar 'gyur//khro bo'i rgyal po 'di yis ni//rdo rje'i 'khaur rab sbyar nas//seng ge zhon pa'i cho ga yis//mi dang lha rnams 'gugs par 'gyur//A+o~M suM b+ha ni sum
54a3(rgyud): b+ha ni hU~M/g+hi h+na g+hi h+na hU~M/g+hi ha na pa ya g+hi ha na pa ya hU~M/a na ya ho/b+ha g+ha wan bi da ra tsA ya hU~M phaT//rdo rje sa 'og tu gnas pa'i//khro bo chen po gsungs pa yis//lha'i bu mo klu mor bcas/
54a4(rgyud): sgyego ma nu ma 'dar ldan ma//bsku gsung thugs ni bsgom pa yis//rdo rje zhags pas bcings bcas te//lcags kyu yis ni rnaM grangs nas//bu mo thams cad dgug pa'o//'jig
54a5(rgyud): rten gsum 'gugs pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//rdo rje chos ni srin chen po//rdo rje sems dpa' mchog sred dang //rdo rje nyi ma tshogs chen dang //rta mchog gi
54a6(rgyud): ni tshogs chen po//rtag pa yis kyang srin kyis ni//zhags pa lcags kyu'i bsgom pa yis//sangs rgyas rnams kyang 'gugs gsungs na//sa stengs skye bo smos ci dgos//
54a7(rgyud): rang gi rigs ma'i skyes bu bsam//sku gsung rdo rje'i rnam par mdzes//lcags kyu la sogs sbyor ba yis//bu mo bkug la spyad par bya//bcom ldan 'das dgug pa'i dam tshig
54a8(rgyud): chen pos de skad bka' stsal to//na ma sa man ta b+hu te b+h+yo/A+o~M ta ki hU~M//rdo rje rgyus sam ni tsag la yA//rdo rje lcags kyu rab sbyar bas//zla ba bza' yis zin dusu//kha ru bcug
54b1(rgyud): nas bsgrub bya ste//'di ni bris pa tsam dang ni//mdzes pa brgya ni bzlas pa yis//rlung dang khyab 'jug dbang dang grag//chung mar bcas pa dgug par bya//rdo rje daM tshig lcags kyu
54b2(rgyud): zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//bsam gtan sngags kyi ting 'dzin gnas//rang rig dang ni skyes pa'i bdag//dgug pa'i gnas la gzung bya ste//thub pa gzhan du mi gsung ngo//na ma sa man
54b3(rgyud): ta saM man ta bu d+ha naM࿒ na ma sa man ta dar ma naM࿒ na ma sa man ta saM g+ha naM/A+o~M si ta ta pa tre࿒ oM b+hi ma la࿒ A+o~M shaM ka ra࿒ A+o~M pra taM gyi ra badzra u Sh+ya sha tsag kra bar ti/sarba dza na tra mu la/kar ma
54b4(rgyud): b+han d+ha na dang /lnga na kyi la naM bA࿒ ma ma kri te ye na ke na tsid krid taM tad/sarba tshin d+ha tshin d+ha࿒ b+hin d+ha b+hin d+ha࿒ tsi ri tsi ri࿒ g+hi ri g+ha ri࿒ ma ra ma ra࿒ hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M hU~M hrU~M hU~M hU~M hA hU~M phaT 
54b5(rgyud): phaT phaT//bdag nyid srung ba'i rim pa yis//'khrul 'akhor sngags bsogs gshig pa la//bung bar rig par ganas byas nas//'jig rten gsuM gyis 'da' mi
54b6(rgyud): 'gyur//khro bo chen po rdo rje 'khor lo zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//dben pa nags tshal dag du rung//g.yeng ba thaMs cad rnam spangs par//mchog gi rnal ('byor)'di bsgrubs na//thub pa
54b7(rgyud): gzhan du gsungs pa min//khro bo chen po'i 'khor lo'i sngags kyi 'jug pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i stan pa'i le'u ste//bzhi pa'o////de nas bcom ldan rdo rje can//sems can
54b8(rgyud): kun kyi snying la zhugs//ye shes kun mkhyen du 'byung ba//nges par rab tu gsal bar bya//sangs rgyas skur ni rab sgoms te//rdo rje sems dpa' rab sgom bya'o//sangs rgyas skur
55a1(rgyud): @#//ni rab sgoms te//rdo rje rnam snang dran par bya'o//sangs rgyas skur ni rab sgoms te//rdo rje nyi ma rab sgom bya'o//sangs rgyas skur nirab sgoms te//'dod chags rdo rje rnaM
55a2(rgyud): sgom bya'o//sems dpa'i skur ni rab sgom byas//don yod rta por rab sgom bya'o//sangs rgya sku las rab byung ba'i//rtag tu dkyil 'khor rnam sgoms bya'o//sngag nyid kyi ni thaMs cad kyi ( )snying
55a3(rgyud): 'khar ye shes gnasu dgod//ye shes sems dpa' rab sbyor bas//sangs rgyas byang chub thob par 'gyur//rang sngags yige rdzogs pa na//phyag rgya'i mtshan nyid brtag par bya'o//sro zhing
55a4(rgyud): sdu ba byas pa ni//dkyil 'khor rnaMs kyi 'byung gnas yin//sgra la rnam gsuM shes byas te//lha rnaMs la ni dbul bar bya//de nyid bcom ldan rdo rje can//rdo rje seMs dpa' grags chen po//gzugs la
55a5(rgyud): rnam gsuM shes byas te/lha rnaMs la ni dbul bar bar bya//de nyid ( )bcom ldan rgyal ba ste//mnga' bdag rdo rje rnam snang mdzad//dril ( )rnam gsuM shes byas ste//lha rnaMs la ni dbul par bya//de nyid
55a6(rgyud): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das//rin chen phung por rab tu grag( )//ro la rnam gsum shes byas te//lha rnams la ni dbul bar bya//de nyid bcom ldan 'dod chags che//de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje
55a7(rgyud): chos reg la rnam gsum shes byas te //lha rnams la ni dbul par bya//de nyid bcom lda na sna tshogs pa//'bras bu don yod gsol mdzad pa'o//bsrung ba'i dam tshig dbus gnas par //
55a8(rgyud): dkyil 'khor lnga pa bsgom par bya'o//lnga lnga'i bdag nyid rdzogs sangs rgyas//ye shes lnga ni rnam par sgom//'dod pa'i yon tan lngas bkod cing//sna tshogs 'od zer kun tu 'khrigs//
55b1(rgyud): sna tshogs la ni byed par gshad//thams cad sdus pa'i dkyil 'khor ni//nyi shu rtsa lngar phye ba'i//rdul tshon gyis ni bdab par bya//sangs rgyas lnga rnams rang bzhin gyis//phung po lnga rnaMs
55b2(rgyud): rab tu grags//sangs rgyas 'gon po'i dkyil 'khor ni//shar kyi char ni rab grags te//rdo rje nyi ma'i lho ru ni//'dod chags chen po'i nub tu ste//rta'i rgyal po'i byang du'o//dbus su
55b3(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' nyid//shar du dkar po chen po ste//lho ru ser po yang dag ldan//nub gi char ni dmar mo ste//byang du ljang gser yang dag ldan//sgra'i cha rnams thams cad snang//sgo rnaMs
55b4(rgyud): kyi ni bsgo khyud du//rdo rje rin chen rnam btsugs pas//rnam pa sna tshogs mdzes par ldan//sangs rgyas 'gon po gang zhig gis;;dkyil 'khor dbus mar 'gyur ba yin//de nyid kyi ni dkyil
55b5(rgyud): 'khor gshad//snying po len cing dkyil 'khor te//dkyil 'khor re re bsgom par bya//yang na bsdus pa'i dkyil 'khor ro//brgyad brgyad kyi ni dbugs ma 'am//
55b6(rgyud): yang na nyi shu lnga rnams bsgor bkod pa//de tshe bsdus pa'i dkyil 'khor ro //lcags kyu zhags pa lcags bsgrog da//de bzhin rdo rje 'bebs ma nyid//bcom ldan 'das
55b7(rgyud): bsdus pa chen po'i dam tshig gis de bskad ces bka' bstsal to//dred dang de bzhin bya rgod gse'//ba min dang ni stag nyid dang//wa sbre chu srin na kri ni//rdo rje chos kyi
55b8(rgyud): g.yas rnams su//khra dang 'ug pa chu bya dkar//seng ge gzhad dang de bzhin lug//bkri snyan sa le phag dang ldan//g.yon na brtag tu gnas pa'o//rma bya ma he
56a1(rgyud): @#//ma ka ra|nya dang nya rgyal rus sbal dang//klu lhag de bzhin dang mo rnams//rtag pa'i g.yas pa rnams na'o//da byin srog chags 'dzi ta dang//srog chags
56a2(rgyud): ha la ha la dang//a 'dzi na dang byi la dang//'bri dang 'bri ldan sa rang ka//g.yon pa'i thod pa rnams la na'o//ka ran ta ka'i bya dang rung ba dang//lug dang byi ni tsa ka tso ra nyid
56a3(rgyud): bya'u khri dang ni hor khra dang//'ol ba dag ni nya khra rnams//nyi ma'i g.yon pa min pa na'o//stobs kyi rgyu dang dung skyong dang//rigs ldan pad ma pad bzangs dang//'jog po dang
56a4(rgyud): ni nor rgyas mtha' yas rnams//rin chen rgyal po'i g.yon rnams na'o//bza' lhag phur bu bzhug rings dang//sgra gcan mig ( )mar ba ba sangs//spen pa dang ni khyab 'jug rnams//rta
56a5(rgyud): rgyal g.yon pa ( )sa min par grags//dbang po tshangs pa de ba zhin drag//'dod lha de bzhin gshol 'dzin dang//gcer bu dang ni thag bzangs ris//gtor ma zhes bya g.yon rnams
56a6(rgyud): na'o//blo dang dbugs dang de bzhin smyo//khrag skyug mdze dang g.yan pa dang//khog skabs lgang pa'i rnam bzhin rnams//glang po bsogs par rab tu grags//gzhang nad dang ni
56a7(rgyud): 'bog nad dang//chu rdo'i nad khong gzer dang//rgyu nad lcos nad 'bras nad dang//mig ser nad rnams dred mo bsogs//mig tshag dang ni so nad dang//khrag 'khris dang ni bad rlung dang//

56b1(rgyud): rkang grums rnams//ka ran ta'i bya bsogs rnams//rlung 'khris de bzhin ma zhu dang//ling tog rab rib za khu can//bzhogs byed na dang lha gor nad//rlung snyon bsogs rnams bza'
56b2(rgyud): lhag bsogs//'gro ba rnams na rab mchog ni//mi rnams kyi 'gro ba ste//de ni nad kyis g.yengs mthong ste//sems can gzir bar mi 'gyur phyir//sangs rgyas lnga rnams rdzu 'phrul che//nyid kyi
56b3(rgyud): sku las zlog mdzad pa'o//nad bzhi bya phyir sangs rgyas ni//glang po la sogs nad rnams snams//de ltar bsgom par byed pa la//nad dang khod nad khong khro med//nad med
56b4(rgyud): 'chi ba med do kye//nga rgyal med cing gti mug med//bkres pa med cing bskom pa med//de bzhin ngan song sdug bsngal med//lha dang lha min mi rnams ni//sus kyang
56b5(rgyud): 'ching bar mi 'gyur ro//nad rnams thams cad dbugs dbyung zhing//rab tu zlog pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyi le'u ste lnga pa'o////de nas rgyal po rdo rje

56b7(rgyud): rin chen sems ni rab bsams te//chos rjes dran pa bsgom par bya'o//las rjes dran ldan rnal 'byor pas//'khor lo chen po rab sgom bya'o//rnal 'byor rab 'byor 'di lta bu//
56b8(rgyud): ma byung byung bar mi 'gyur ro//phyag rgya bzhi yis rgyas btab te//rdo rje sems dpa' sgom par bya'o//phyag rgya chen po rgyas btab dngos//rdo rje snang mdzad bsam par bya'o//rdo rje phyag
57a1(rgyud): @#//rgya'i rab sbyor bas//rin chen phung po rab sgom mo//chos kyi phyag rgya'i rab 'byor gyis//rdo rje las la phyag rgyas gdab//sems kyi phyag rgyar rab sbyor na//las kyi rdo rjes
57a2(rgyud): rgyas btab bya//zhe sdang rgyal po rgyal chen po//skyon rnams kun ni zad mdzad pa'o//spro ba'i cho ga sbyor bas sgom zhing//rab tu bsdus par stan pa'o//sangs rgyas 'gon por rab sgos
57a3(rgyud): te;;sangs rgyas srin gis kun 'khrigs par//spro zhing sdu ba'i sbyor ba yis//sangs rgyas byang chub rab sgrub pa'o//rin chen chen po rin chen sems//nga rgyal skyon ni bzad mdzad pa'o;;
57a4(rgyud): sems kyi sprin gi rab sbyor bas//slar yang sdug sdu bar yang dag stan//chos kyi rgyal po gti mug cher//spro sdud pa'i gzugs can du//thud mtshams bzhir ni dam bcas la//sgom na myur
57a5(rgyud): du dngos grub thob//sems ni myur 'gro ltar bsams nas//don yod 'bras bu rtsol bar 'gyur//rta mchog gi ni srin chen gis//sdu ba rnam par sgom par bya//bcom
57a6(rgyud): ldan 'das spro ba dang sdu ba'i dam tshig chen pos de skad ces bka' bstsal to//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus nas par//sems kyi dngos po rnam par sgom//gsal ba'i dkyil
57a7(rgyud): 'khor dbus gnas par//sku'i yi ger rnam par sgom//dkyil 'khor dmar po'i dbus gnas par//rin chen sems kyi yig 'bru dgod//chos kyi dkyil 'khor dbus gnas
57a8(rgyud): par;;gsung gi yi ge rnam par sgom//las kyi dkyil 'khor dbus gsanas par//don yod gzugs can yi ge'o//sku gsung mi phyed sgom pa yis//ye shes rdo rje sgom par bya;;
57b1(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' mi phyed pa'o//chos kyi dkyil 'khor gnas nang du//rin chen sems kyi rin chen ste//'dod chags pad mar pad mar zad//shes rab ral gri don yod pa'i//shes
57b2(rgyud): rab rnon po'i rang phyir//rnam rtog ri bo gcod pa ste//sngags dang phyag rgyar btab pa yin//dpag tshad bye ba'i khyon tsam du//rdo rje dkyil 'khor sgom bya ste//rtag pa'i 'dakyil 'khor dag dang ni/
57b3(rgyud): rin chen phung po 'bar chen dang//chos phung 'gon po chen po dang//las kyi rdo rje de bzhin gshegs//'bar byed dkyil 'khor 'od zer las//sangs rgyas srin gis rnam mdzes par//nam
57b4(rgyud): mkha' dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//ye shes dkyil 'khor sgom par bya//bkur gnas chen por rab byas la//'dod pa'i yon tan lngar ldan par//kun tu sna tshogs gzugs rnaMsu/
57b5(rgyud): mchod pa kun gyis mchod par bya'o//mtshams kyi lha mo'i rab sbyor bas//ye shes rgya mtshor rab tu sgrub//ye shes 'khor lo 'bar ba che//dam tshig 'khor los
57b6(rgyud): phab byas te//sangs rgyas kun mnyam rnal 'byor pas//skad cig las ni gnyis med 'gyur//gse ru dung dang ru rag sha//bo do tse dang pad ma'i 'bru//mu tig bsogs dang byu ru
57b7(rgyud): bsogs//bsam gtan ldan pa'i sngags su zla'o//sngags ni spyan 'dren par gsungs te//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang//mkha' 'gro ma dang btsun mo bzang//
57b8(rgyud): mngon par phyogs par bya phyir dang//dper na skyes chen gang zhig la//che ba myur ba'i sgra bos gnas//thos pa tsam gis 'ong ba bzhin//sangs rgyad skad cig gis byon 'gyur/
58a1(rgyud): //nam mkha'i khams ni ji snyed par//'jig rten khams ni stong khrag na//bde chen phun tshogs phyag rgya che//sangs rgyas nam mkha' 'dra bar bzhugs//yang ni de
58a2(rgyud): rnams sngags rig la//rdo rje can kyis mi gzigs zhe'o//gang gis sngags kyi theg rnyed pa//des ni byang chub mchog thob bo//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' ni pa//sngags kyi spyod pa
58a3(rgyud): mchod spyod rnams//rdo rje sngags ni rab sgom pas//mtshungs med ye shes rab tu thob//de ltar mkhyen nas sangs rgyas rnams//sngags spyod pa'i tshul la bzhugs //'bras bu nyid la
58a4(rgyud): mi gnas te//de yang sangs rgyas mnyam brjod pas//mi gzigs pa nyid spyad cing bya////chos kyi dbyings ni 'od gsal bar//sangs rgyas rgyal la la dmigs bya//de'i phyir
58a5(rgyud): na sgoms pas ni//sems can sangs rgyas dang mnyam 'gro //ji ltar ma nor chos nyid du//ji ltar sangs rgyas 'jig rten de//nam mkha' dri ma med pa
58a6(rgyud): ma med pa bzhin//de bzhin sems can khams gshad do//rnam rtog bden pa ma yin pas//rin chen sems ni dri mar bcas//ji ltar nam mkha' 'jigs med
58a7(rgyud): bzhin//dag pa'i sems ni dag par 'gyur//rin chen sems ni rab skul bas//ji ltar nam mkha' de bzhin sems//skye 'dir sangs rgyas mchog nyid du//sems
58a8(rgyud): sgoms pas ni 'tshang rgya'o//rin chen sems las phyir 'gyur pa//sangs rgyas med cing sems can med//gnas pa'i don dang rnam shes dag//phyi rol gyur pa 'ga' yang med//
58b1(rgyud): khams gsum na ni dbang phyug che//sangs rgyas kun nyid yid kyis byed//khams gsum na ni rgyal chen po//sems nyid gcig bus thob par 'gyur//gzugs dang sgra dang
58b2(rgyud): de bzhin dri//ro rag bya skyes pa'i dngos//rin chen sems 'dir brjod nyid de//chos nyid mchog du zhi ba'o//gzugs dang tshor ba 'du shes dang//'du shes dang ni rnam
58b3(rgyud): shes nyid//sems can sems 'di dngos po las//thub pas gzhan du ma gsungs so//yin bzhin nor bu chen pos so//don 'dod 'bras bu rab stsol cig//bden bzhin dag
58b4(rgyud): pa'i sems nyid dang//sangs rgyas byang chub 'bras rab ster//de nyid kyi na sangs rgyas kyis//rdo rje thugs ni skul ba na//zab mo'i rnam pa'i rtsa ba can//phyed par dka' ba'i dngos grub
58b5(rgyud): rtsol////rdo rje snying po'i rab tu gsang bar byed pa'i le'u ste drug pa'o////de nas rdo rje 'dzin pa che//mkha' 'gro ma ni rdo rje'i gur//rnal 'byor ting 'dzin
58b6(rgyud): skyed bzhugs te//sbyor ba'i bdag nyid rab tu gsungs//sangs rgyas gnas cing rdzogs byas la//kun tu bzang po bde ba'i dngos//rnam pa lnga pos sgom byas te//rang
58b7(rgyud): lhag lha'i sgrub thabs bya//de bzhin dkyil 'khor rnams bkod la//mchod bstod bdud rtsi la sogs par//rim pa yis ni sgom byas nas//yan lag drug ni sbyor bar
58b8(rgyud): 'dod////oM sarba ta tha g+ha ta a bi she ka ta badzra swa b+ha ba ad mo ko ha~M////bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa mi skyod pa rdo rjes de skad ces bka' stsal to//oM sarba tA thA a mri ta
60a1(rgyud): //swa ta na badzra swa b+ha ba ad mo ko ha~M//bcom ldan 'das tshe dpag tu med pa rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pas de skad ces bka' stsal to//o~M sarba ta tha gA tA stu ti badzra swa b+ha ba ad
60a2(rgyud): mo ko ha~M//bcom ldan 'dasarin po che tog rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa de bskad ces bka' stsal to/sarba ta ga ta pu tsa badzra swa b+ha ad mo ko ha~M//bcom ldana'adas rta mchog rdo rje de bzhin
60a3(rgyud): gshegs pas de skad ces paka' stsal to//o~M sarba da tha ga ta a nu ra gA na badzra swa b+ha ad mo ko haM//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje nyi ma de bzhin gshegs pas de sa skad ces bka' stsal to/spros
60a4(rgyud): pa dang ni sdus pa'i sku//rgyal chen rnam par snang mdzad do//chos dbyings yi ge las skyes pa//des ni sangs rgyas gnas rgod bya//o~M bsarba ta tha ga ta a ni la ya
60a5(rgyud): badzra swa b+ha ba ad mo ka ha~M//rnam par snang mdzad rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//nam mkha' dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//'byung ba bzhi bdag dkyil 'khor ste/
60a6(rgyud): de steng sangs rgyas bsam byas nas//sro ba'i gnas ni rtsegs ma dgod//rdo rje lta bur mi phyed phyir//mi skyod pa ni de sgom bya//'jig min skye ba ma yin pa'i ma yin pa'i//
60a7(rgyud): rtag par rnam par snang mdzad do/sems ni rin chen zhes bya ste//rin chen gi mchog bla na med//chags pa chen po rdo rje chos//'dod chags mtshan nyid 'dzin pa'i phyir
60a8(rgyud): sems ni 'dod chags zhes bya ste//rdo rje sems dpa'i gnas la bzhugs//gang zhig dngos grub 'bras rtsol ba//des na don yod rab grags pa//rig par byed pa rdo rje zhes/bya ba'i
60b1(rgyud): ting nge 'dzin to//dang po bum pa'i dbang skur te//gnyis pa la ni gsang ba'i dbang//gsum pa shes rab ye shes te//ji ltar de bzhin gshegs pa min//dkyil 'khor dam pa bris nas ni;;
60b2(rgyud): dam tshig seMs kyi cho ga yis//nang gi sbyin sreg byas nas ni//de tshe dbyugs ni dbyung bar bya'o//dam tshig lnga rnaMs kyis bkang ste//mkha' 'gro lnga dang mnyam ldan zhing//mkha' 'gro ma'i tshogs
60b3(rgyud): ldan pas//slob ma rdo rje chos dbang skur//rdo rje sa bon rab sbyor ba//rang gi lhag pa'i lha sbyar nas//slob ma rtag par bsams la kye//rgyun gyis rigs kyi ming du bya'o//spyan dang ma ma kyi dang sgrol
60b4(rgyud): gos kar la sogs gzugs rnams kyis//rdo rje btsun mo sbyor ba yis//rdo rje bla na med pa ston//de nyid bla na med pa 'di//gnyis med 'gyur ba ma yin no//bde ba dam pa ro myong ba//chos 
60b5(rgyud): dbyings 'od gsal rnam par ro//sgrib pa med pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//g+ha yig ha yig zhes bya dang//swa yi ga dang po tha ma ni//'bar ba'i dam pa 'di yin te//
60b6(rgyud): dngos grub thams cad rab ster ba'o//de rnams cho gang zos tsam gyis//rdo rje bdud rtsi phun sum mtshogs//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor zhing//rdo rje dang yang mnyaM
60b7(rgyud): por 'gyur//nad rnams thams cad rab 'jig cing//'di ni lus zang 'dog sdug byed//'jig rten kun gyi yid 'phrog cing//phas kyi smra ba kun 'pham byed//'jig rten kun kyi
60b8(rgyud): brgyan byas zhing//lha dang lha min rnams kyis mchod//mkha' 'gro mnga' bdag bde 'gyur te//chos kyi kha zas rnams la spyod//dam tshig chen po bsam gtan la//gnas pa rdo rje
61a1(rgyud): //zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//mi skyod sten pa'i rnal 'byor pa//nad rnams rnam par spong bar 'gyur//rtag pa yis ni mdangs chen 'gyur//rdo rje nyi mas rgas pa med//
61a2(rgyud): rdo rje chos ni bden pa yis//bud med kun la long spyod de//srog chags lus 'byung rdo rje dang//don yod rgyal po chen po ste//dpal ldan don kun grub pa dang//rnal 'byor ma des zhi bar btod
61a3(rgyud): mi skyod btung ba'i sbyor ba yis//stong phrag lngar ni mtsho bar 'gyur//rtag pa yisi ni khri ru brjod//rdo rje nyi ma bye ba ste//tshe dpag med kyis grangs med tshe//namakha' ji bzhin rta bdun las//dpag med
61a4(rgyud): rdo rje nyi ma gnyis//'khor ba sgrub pa'i rtsa ba ste//'di dag rnal 'byor pas sten na//bskal bzangs rnaMs dang mnyaM par 'gyur//ye shes sh+se rab pha rol phyin//rdo rje nyi mar 'di bshado//chos rnaMs
61a5(rgyud): kyi ni thabs rnaMs kun//de bzhin gshegs pa tshe dpag med//sangs rgyas rnaMs dang rdo rje nyid//thabs dang shes rab 'phel mdzad pa//sa 'og sa stengs bar snang mkha'//'jig rten khams
61a6(rgyud): rnaMs kun na ni//khrag dang khu ba las byung lus//bcoM ldan 'das rdo rje dbang phyug gis de skad ces bka' stsal to//sen mo spu dang de bzhin brdung//khu ba snang ba mtha' yas so//khrag dang pags pa brjid
61a7(rgyud): chen ste//rkang dang tshil bu rdo rje 'dzin//de bzhin kun ni rnaM snang mdzad//mgo dang nu ma rta bdun pA+o//de rnams rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas lngar//rdo rje mkha' 'gros gsungs pa yin//sa khaMs rnaM par dag pa
61a8(rgyud): yin//spyan ma 'khor los mdzes pa'o//de bzhin gsal ba rdo rje dbyings//me ni pad mo'i nu ma'o//sgrol ma rlung khams dag pa'o//rdo rje tsun mo'i rnaMs su//rdo rje gur zhes bya ba yi//mkha' 'gro rdo rje
61b1(rgyud): 'dzes pas gsungs//sngags thaMs cad rnam par dag pa rnaMs dang /bdud rtsi lnga'i phan yon dang bcas pa stan pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i le'u bdun pa'o////khyod ni rdo rje sdang rnaMs yongasu skyod//mdzad
61b2(rgyud): dag pa'i thugs//shin tu rnaM dag chos kyis rab tu rgyan byas bzang po sku//lhos mo bdag la de ring gzigs te stod pa dor mdzod la//'dren pa 'jig rten pa'i rgyu las skye ba gzhengs mdzad cig//khyod
61b3(rgyud): ni rdo rti mug yongasu skyong mdzad dag pa'i thugs//gzugs rnyan lta bu'i chos kyis rab tu rgyan pa bzang po'i sku//ri khrod ma bdag la gzigs 'gon po stong pa dor mdzod la//'gon po 'dren pa 'jig rten
61b4(rgyud): ba'i rgyu las skye ba bzhengs mdzod cig//khyod ni rdo rje chags la yongasu skyob mdzad dag pa'i gsung//nges par so sor rtogs pas rab tu rgyan pa bzang pa'i sku//rdum mo bdag la de ring gzig te stong
61b5(rgyud): pa dor mdzod la//'gon('dren pa)po 'jig rten pa'i rgyu las skyid pa bzhengs mdzod cig//khyod ni sangs rgyas thaMs cad skyob mdzad da thaMs cad gzugs//thams cad nan tan mnyam pa nyid kyis
61b6(rgyud): rab tu rgyan mdzad pa//sme mo bdag la de rir gzigs te stong pa dor mdzod la//'dren pa 'jig rten pa'i rgyud la skyes pa bzhengs mdzod cig//thams cad slong bar byed pa rdo rje zhes
61b7(rgyud): bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//slong bar byed pa'i 'byung gnas ni//'khor lo rnams ni thams cad kyi//ye shes rdo rjes bya ni//dkyil 'khor bdag pos de skad gsungs////oM sarba
61b8(rgyud): ta tha ga ta khe tsa ri badzra swa b+ha ba a mo ko haM/oM sarba ta thA ga ta b+hu tsa ri badzra swa b+ha ba ad mo ko haM//oM sarba ta tha g+ha tA/pA tA lA wa/sid ni badzra sa b+ha ba/ad mo ka haM//
62a1(rgyud): //sku dang gsung thugs chos rnams ni//gnas rnams rtag par byin rlabs par//ye shes rdo rjes byas nas ni//byang chub tshul 'di brtan pa yin//bcom ldan 'das sku gsung thugs kyi bdag pos de skad ces
62a2(rgyud): bka' stsal to//phyag rgya chen por rab sbyor bas//rin chen skur ni rnam par sbyor//bsam gtan rdo rje rab bsams nas//rin chen sems su rab tu bsgrubs//rdo rje zlas pa'i khyad par gyis//dri med rdo rje'i
62a3(rgyud): gsung du 'gyur//gti mug rdo rje sbyar bas ni//mig gi rnam shes sbyong ba yin//sgra'i sgro ni bsam bya ba//zhe sdang rdo rje rab tu bsam//sna ni ser sna rdo rje ste//kha ni 'dod chags rdo rje'o//phra dog rdo rje
62a4(rgyud): lus po che//yid ni kun tu bzang pos so//de nyid bdag med mkha' 'gro ma//'di rnaMs kyis thams cad sbyong//gang gi tshe na dngos po yod min pa//de'i tshe'ang rgyal ba rnams kyis sbyong ba yod
62a5(rgyud): 'khor lo kun rdzob bden par rab tu gnas pa ste//chos nyid kyis ni rang bzhin sgoms pa tsam yin no//chos nyid dngos po ma yin ma nor yang dag mtha'//gang zag kun gyis rnam
62a6(rgyud): phral 'jig rten pa//sems de'i nus mthu 'jig rten 'go na med de//chos nyid gzugs kyis thaMs cad rnam par sbyong//sreg la sreg dang dug gis lus po bangs pa la ni dug gzhan sman du
62a7(rgyud): 'gyur//dper na rna ba ru ni chu song slar yang chu nyid thabasu 'gyur ba dang//rlung gis nyos pa'i sems ldan rnaMs la skye bo mkhas pa sran dro'u ji ltar ster ba dang//'dod chags kyis ni skye'o//myos pa
62a8(rgyud): rnams la 'ang 'dod chags me yis//de bzhin rtogs par bya//'dod chags kyis skyed 'jig rten//'dod chags spangs na rlag par 'gyur//rdo rje chags pa yod shes na//yid ni rdo rje seMs
62b1(rgyud): dpar 'gyur//chags pa'i zhags pas rnam cings pa//'jig rten chags pas myos par gshad//chags dang bral ba'i byang chub ni//chags pa mchog ldan rnaMs kyis smad//de nas yang dag
62b2(rgyud): gshad bya ba//rdo rje sgrol ma rab sgrub po//sbyin sreg las kyi cho ga yis//'dod pa'i don kun sgrub par bya'o//de la sngags kyi tshig du gyur pa 'di lta ste//oM na mo|ar+ya pa lo ki te shwa ra ya//b+ho te swa ta ya|
62b3(rgyud): ma ha sa ta ya|ma ha ka ru ni ka ya|tad+ya thA//oM ta re tud ta re tu re sarba du stan ma ma tri ta dzam pa ya//mo ha ya mo ha b+han d+ha ya//b+han d+ha ya|hU~M hU~M phaT phaT phaT/sarba stam b+ha ni ta re swahA//ma mo'i
62b4(rgyud): dkyil 'khor dbugs bzhugs par//lha mo sgrol ma sgom par bya//phyag brgyad ma la zhal bzhi ma//rgyan rnaMs kun gis rnam par rgyan|gzhon nu ma'i mtshan gyis 'bar//kha dog gser dang 'tshungs
62b5(rgyud): par sgom //rdo rje nyi mas dbang skur ma//gcod pan chen pos sangs rgyas bzhi//mdangs sdug na so gzhon byad can//gser ra rna cha g.yo bag can//sna tshogs pad ma'i bdan la bzhug+sa;
62b6(rgyud): 'od zer dmar pos rnam par brgyan//rdo rje de bzhin zhags pa dang//dung dang mda' ni g.yas na snams//gzhu dang sdigs 'dzub g.yon na snams//rdo rje dkyil dkrung sbyor ba
62b7(rgyud): yis|sa gsum po ni 'grub par byed//shar tu me tog sgrol ma ste//kha dog kar mo yid 'ong ma//yi ge oM rnam pas skyed//phyag ni me tog 'phreng 'dzin rtson//zhal cig phyag
62b8(rgyud): gnyis ma nyid de//rgyan rnams kun gis rnam par rgyan//lhor ni spos kyi sgrol ma ste//mdog nag po la gzugs mdzes ma//bdug pa'i yu bas phyag sgrel ma//rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par
62'a1(rgyud): //rgyan||nub du mar me'i sgrol ma ste//mar me'i kong bu 'dzin rtson phyag//rgyan cha che ldan kha dog ser//gser gyi rna cha g.yo bag can//byang du dri'i sgrol ma ste//dri'i dung por 'dzin
62'a2(rgyud): brtson phyag//lha mo kha dog 'od dmar mtshungs//dkyil 'khor snying po sgom bya'o//de nas sgo skyong ma rnaMs gsam//lcags kyu rtsogs pa rab phye ba'i//steng du gtsug gtor rnam rgyal ste//'og tu
62'a3(rgyud): gnod mdzes rab sgom bya'o//'khor lo klu zhags bsams nas ni//sngags ni spro zhing zla bar bya//oM ta re dud ta re tu re swahA//pha rol phyin pa bcu'i gnas//yi ge bcus ni lha mo bcur|'bad
62'a4(rgyud): pa yis ni sgoms 'gyur na//las kun rab tu grub par byed||rab dang mchog dang sngags 'di ni//sangs rgyas kun gyis phyag byas pa'o||glags par grub byed grag po ste//rdo rje gur gis gsungs
62'a5(rgyud): pa'o||sngags 'di yis gos sam skra la lan bdun du||mngon du sngags kyis btab par bya ste//'dud par bya cings nas//'grog dgon par song yang gang gis kyang bag tsha bar mi 'gyur
62'a6(rgyud): te||stag dang chom rkun dang chu srin ki ma bi ra dang/seng ge dang sbrul dang glang po dang/ma he dang dred dang/ba men la sogs pa rnams pha rol tu phyin pa bcu la gnas pa rnaMs su sgoms te//gong gi
62'a7(rgyud): rab tu sbyor ba yis//dgos pa thams cad la de dran pa tsam gis 'jig cing 'du bar 'gyur ro//sngags 'di yis ud pa la'i sbyin sregs ci srid du lan rgya rtsa brgyad du bya'o//oM ta re tu re che ge mo la
62'a8(rgyud): bdag gi dbang du gyur cig swahA//yang sngags 'di nyid kyis khwa'i gshog pa la lan sum bcu rtsa gnyis yongs su sngags te/dgra bo'i khyim du sbas na nyi ma bdun gyis skrod par 'gyur ro//oM ta re
62'b1(rgyud): dud da re du re'i tsa la phra tsa la sha g+ha gA|ma ni lha sbyin u ts+tsha ta ya hU~M phaT//de nas gzhan yang rab tu sgrub par byas te/o~M ta re dud da re tu re//gzhon nu ma ming che ge mo zhes bya ba'i de'i pha mas 
62'b2(rgyud): bdag la lugs bzhin du ster bar gyur cig/swahA//ma da na shing rtum po'i sa bon dang snye bar byed pa nyid dung ni//me tog lo ma a sho ki//stong phrag lngar ni sreg pa 'am/bu ram mar dang sbrang rtsir
62'b3(rgyud): ldan|bu mo 'grub pa'i sbyin sreg shis//rang gi bu mo khyer yongs te//rnal 'byor pa nyid bdun gyis 'thob//de nas gzhan yang rabatu sbyor ba yin te/a ta re tud de tu re/ming che ge mo zhes bya
62'b4(rgyud): ba rang gi gnas nas bdag gi drung du khug shig dza//sngags 'dis rgya shug gi tshar ma rang byung gi me tog latu sbas te/sngags 'di stong phrag gcig tu sbyin sreg byas/na mi'i bdag po'i bu mo
62'b5(rgyud): 'gugs par 'gyuro//de nas gzhan yang rabadu sbyor ra bar 'gyur te/bud med kyi rang byung gi sbags pa'i ras la/bcom ldan 'das ma zhal cig ma phyag gnyis ma/ud pa
62'b6(rgyud): la dang zhags pa snams pa'i rnam par bri'o//de'i 'dun du me tog bzang po mdzes pa 'di dag la sogs pas mchod pa byas la/de nas sgom pa dang ldan pas sngags kyi zlas pa bya'o//oM ta
62'b7(rgyud): re tud ta re tu re bdag 'tshal pa'i che ge mo rmi lam du gsungs shig hU~M phaT//stong phrag lngas 'ong bar 'gyuro//zhags pa yis ni 'gul bcings te//lcags kyu yis ni rnam grangs nas//sgrub bya rkang
62'b8(rgyud): 'og tu bsams na//bran gyi gzugs kyis spyod par byed//de nas gzhan yang rab tu sbyor bar 'gyur te//sgrol ma'i gzungs lan bdun du sngags pa'i sngags kyis mngon du gad ba cher byas la;bdag
63a1(rgyud): @#//nyid kyi mig gnyis po bdag la mig sman byug pa ltar byas na//rgyal po'i rigs rnams rab tu drung du 'jug go//de nas rgyal po de slob bzhin du snyen bkur byed par 'gyur zhing /ngan du
63a2(rgyud): mi smra bar dkor rnaMs ster bar 'gyuro //snyan par smra bar byed cing dran lta bur nye bar 'gro bar 'gyur zhing /khros pa yang ltas pa tsam gis rkyen gi dbang du 'gyuro//de lta bu ni yang dag par 'gyur ba yin no|
63a3(rgyud): de nas gzhan yang rab tu sbyor bar 'gyur te/sgrol ma'i gzungs rgya phrag gcig gis rnam par skor te/sbrul bdug pa kha gnyis pa phye mar byas la/mchod yon shing bar du dril de bar byas ste/ba kha dag mar po'i
63a4(rgyud): mar kyis rab tu sbyar nas mig sman du byas te blangs la/rdo rje chos dang rdo rje nyi mar snos la /rdo rje rta mchog gis gdug par gdugs nas/rdul de nyid dang bcas pa mig sman du byas nas/rdo rje mig gis
63a5(rgyud): gang la ltas na;de de'i tshe pad ma gar gi dbang phyug dbab par ma gyur na/bdag sgrol ma ma yin zhing /bdag gis sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams gsad par 'gyur ba yin no//de nas 
63a6(rgyud): gzhan yang rab tu sbyor ba 'gyur te/byis pa so ma bskyes pa'i bal+che blangs te/sgrol ma'i gzungs kyis lan bdun mngon par sngags kyis gdab par byas la/shing sbram ma'i rgyal po'i rtsi ba g+hi wang dang
63a7(rgyud): lhan cig du ldan par byas nas/pral bar thig le byas te/gang la ltas na de dbang du byed par 'gyuro//de nas gzhan yang rab tu sbyor bar 'gyur te/lto ba'i sprin bu la gzungs kyis lan bdun du mngon
63a8(rgyud): par sngags kyis btab te/rdo rje chos kyis ldan par byas la/go la dang ldan par bu mo gang yin pa la byin te/'di zos na mchog gi sbyor ba 'dis spong bar mi 'gyur zhing /rang gi bdag po btang nas gzhan
63b1(rgyud): la dga' ba med cing ༐ gzhan du 'gro bar mi 'gyuro//de nas gzhan yang rab tu sbyor bar 'gyur te/mar khu chen pos bdag nyid kyi yan lag bskus la/'od dpag med sgoms nas/sgoMs nas sgrol ma'i
63b2(rgyud): gzungs zla bar bya'o//bzlas pa'i mthar 'bras dkar po btags pas skus te/dril ba'i dri ma la snum khur byas te/gang la byin na de 'tshog chas lnga dang bral yang spong bar mi 'gyuro//de nas gzhan yang rab
63b3(rgyud): tu 'byor bar 'gyur te/zla ba bzas bzung ba'i dus su/sgrol ma'i gzungs bzlas pa byed cing snyen par byas te/de las byung ba'i 'jig rten dbang phyug blangs la/dung gi phye ma dang snos nas/rab tu myos

64a1(rgyud): @#//lag brgyan rnams kun gis mdzes//mchog tu dga' bar mdzad pa'i//a yig ye shes 'byung gnas ni//lte ba'i dkyil 'khor la sgom bya//dam tshig sems dpa' rnam rdzogs na//ye
64a2(rgyud): shes sems dpa' sgom par bya//rnal 'byor zla ba drug sgoms nas//shes rab pha rol phyin par 'gyur//zhag bdun du ni rab sbyor bas//bstan chos de ni thams cad rnam gsal 'gyur//'bum phrag

64a4(rgyud): b+hud d+hi b+har d+ha ni swahA//kha skad mi 'don pa'i sngags 'dis/slangs pa'i ma yin pa'i sings po la lan bdun du sngags kyis gdab par bya zhing/ji srid du zla ba drug gi bar du mthungs na/des shes

64a7(rgyud): kyi theg pa rim par dgus//gzugs ldan mthong na dga' ba dang//dkyil 'khor 'bri mkhas zlas par ldan//rtsa ba'i ltung ba rab 'byoms shing//sngags dang rgyud kyis rab sbyor gyis//'jig rten
64a8(rgyud): rnams la de ster ba//de 'dra bla ma'i dngos byas nas//gang gis kyang ni smad mi bya//bla ma la smod ma skyes bu gang//dmyal ba chen por 'gro bar 'gyur//dper na rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//sangs rgyas

65a1(rgyud): @#//ba࿒ mkha' 'gro ma'o//klu ma hU~M dang mnyam ldan pas//gar ni pus mo btsugs tshul nyid//bla med mchod pa de rnams kyis//sangs rgyas rnams kyis mnyes par bya'o//sangs rgyas

66a1(rgyud): /stong pa'i rgyal po 'dis gsol ba btab bo//zhe sdang rdo rje phyag 'tshal stod//gti mug rdo rje phyag 'tshal stod//ser sna rdo rje bdag la skyobs//'dod chags rdo rje bdag la rtsol;;
