74b1(rgyud): 'byin rtsol ba pos//dam tshig rdo rje bsrung don du//spa bya'i spyod yul gang gsungs pa'o//rdo rje khro bo'i ag tshar che//thams cad zhal gcig phyag gnyis te//snying khar khu
74b2(rgyud): tshur sdig 'dzub la//g.yas pa rnams na mtshon cha 'char//gshin rje gsod pa'i tho ba ste//rdo rje dbyig pa gzhan mi thub//ud pal dmar po rta 'grin gyi'o//bdud rtsi 'khyil pa mi phyed
74b3(rgyud): pa'o|lcags kyu 'dod pa'i rgyal po'o//dbyig sngon rang gi ming ldan la'o//stobs kyi rgyal po rtse gsum pa//g.yas pa rnaMs na mtshon cha 'char//mi g.yo ri rtse dkyil 'khor can;rgyal
74b4(rgyud): po 'dung skyob dkar po'i//chos kyi 'khor lor rnam par sgo|;gsam zhing rdo rje sa 'og gi'o//g.yas rkyang ba'i rnam pas bzhugs//in d+ha snyil dang//mnyam dngos su ;mtha'
74b5(rgyud): med rnam thar sgom par bya'o//zla ba'i nor bu chen po ltar//rdo rje gzhan gyis mi thub pa'o//khro bo pad ma dmar po che//sngon po'i rdo rje bdud rtsi dag//'tshams rnams khro bo sngon
74b6(rgyud): po che;;rdo rje 'dzin //spyi bor 'dzin//gshin rje snang mdzad nyid kyi ste//rdo rje sems dpa' dbyig pa la'o//khro bo pad ma 'jig rten rtsa//sems dpa' rdo rje mkhyil ba la'o//rdo rje dkyil 'khor
74b7(rgyud): dbus gnas par 'og tu pad ma'i bdan bsams te//rdo rje nyi ma rnam sgoms la//khro bo bcu rnaMs rab tu sgom//ye shes rdo rje rang nyid dbus//bcu phyed dkyil 'khor bdag nyid de;;dkyil
74b8(rgyud): 'khor lnga po sgom bya zhing//bdud rnams zhabs kyi 'og tu bsam//bsrung ba'i 'khor lo 'bar ba che//skal pa'i me chen dang 'tshungs pa//g.yo ba med par ltar 'khor zhing //dpag tshad lnga rgya
74'a1(rgyud): //'tshid tsam du //srung ba'i dam tshig rnam par rgyal ba'i stobs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to////de nas bcom ldan 'das dpa'o chen po rang byin gis rlabs pa'i theg pa la
74'a2(rgyud): zhugs te rang byin gyis rlab pa'i sbyor ba ru //de nyid phyir na gsungs pa ste//snying kha'i gzugasu zla ba'i gnas//zla ba'i gzugs la mi shigs pa//sna tshogs 'od kyi rnam 'khrugs ldan//des
74'a3(rgyud): ni rdo rje rnam par sgom//mkha' 'gro rdo rje bdag med ma//skul ba'i cho ga sgom par bya//mkha' 'gro ma'i sna rtser ni//nyung dkar gnas pa rnam par sgom//khams gsum rgyu dang mi rgyu pa'i
74'a4(rgyud): 'gro ba ma lus nyung kar du'o//de lta bur ni sgom 'gyur na//rang byin rlob par rab tu grub//gzugs dang sgra dang de bzhin dri//ro dang reg byar mngon ldan zhing //rnal 'byor nyid
74'a5(rgyud): kyis de bsams na//de nas bdag dang bden med par//rdo rje pad ma las 'byung bzhin//yang dag bde ba mngon mchog de//rba rlabs med cing snang ba med//rnam par rtog med 'gyur
74'a6(rgyud): ba med//nyung zad chang la rab zhugs te//bdag med ma la rang rig gzhag//bral ba'i dga' ba dag gi mtha'//rtog pa'i bri ba rnam bral zhing //mi g.yo rnam par 'phrul med pa//dges
74'a7(rgyud): rdor zhes bya'i dngos por bsam//he ru ka dngos bems po min//rnam pa lnga rnaMs spangs pa yi//bdag nyid 'gon po he ru ka//nyid 'ang rig pa med pa ste//skad cig de la thams cad 'gyur
74'a8(rgyud): 'das dang ma byon da ltar rnaMs//sangs rgyas sangs rgyas sras rnams kyang //thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du mthong //chu rgyus dre ba dang bcas shing //yan lag kun ldan bu ga can//lus
74'b1(rgyud): de med par snang bar 'gyur//de'i skad cig de nyid la//dges pa rdo rje'i sku 'dzin 'gyur//nam mkha' ji bzhin dmigs med cing//mtshan ma med cing skye ba med//sna tshogs rnam 
74'b2(rgyud): gral sgrib lnga spang+sa//sangs rgyas dngos rnams dang bral ba//me long gzugs rnyan ji bzhin du//de'i bdag nyid du snang bar//he ru ka'i dngos por gsal//'di nyid dbang po yul
74'b3(rgyud): rnams dang//phung por gnas pa mkha' dang mnyam//bden nyid 'dir rab skyed lus te//'dus byas don gyis gzhan min pas//mig gi gzugs ni 'dzin med cing//rna ba yis ni
74'b4(rgyud): sgra min no//lce yis dkyang ni ro mi rig//sna'is kyang ni dri la min//lus kyis gang du reg med de//bde chen mo'i gzugs phyir na'o//bud med mchog mthong las
74'b5(rgyud): bde zhing//klu thos las kyang yang dag bde//dri zhim snams las bde ba che//skye gnas reg la kyang bde ba che//ro mchog tung ba'i rab sbyar ba'i//bde ba sus kyang zlog
74'b6(rgyud): mi nus//bde ba chen por 'gro ba khyab//bde ba spangs na rig pa med//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang//rnal 'byor ma dang btsum mo mchog//de bzhin sangs rgyas du ma lnga;
74'b7(rgyud): sna tshogs sangs rgyas bde bar song//de nas phyag na rdo rje byang chub sems dpa' chen pos bcom ldan 'das la kyis bde ba chen po so sor gsungs pa la//tshig 'di skad ces gsungaso//tha mal
74'b8(rgyud): pa gzhom don du//yang dag sgom par rab grags kyi//rang bzhin gnas skabs sgom pa ni//sgom pa ma yin sgom min pa//byang chub de lta bur de shes te//'on kyang de ltar bur ni dri mas
75a1(rgyud): //spags pa dag par dag par bya ba'i phyir//sangs rgyas kyi skur rnam par sgom par bgyi ba lags so//phyag na rdo rjes yang gsol pa bcom ldan 'das thabs ni ci zhig //
75a2(rgyud): lus kyi sgom pa la dgos pa ci zhig mchis/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/bsang ba'i bdag po dgos pa yod do//bcom ldan 'das mtshan ji skad ces bgyi mchis//rdo rje
75a3(rgyud): 'dzin kyis bka' stsal pa//sangs rgyas kyi de kho na nyid ces bya ba de ji lta bu//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid//de ci'i lus can zhig gam de bzhin du bden no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/rdo rje
75a4(rgyud): 'dzin gyis bka' stsal pa//mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis dang //dpe' byad bzang po rgya bcus brgyan pa'i sku de ni nga yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na /yul rnams kyi rgyus sgrub par bya ba
75a5(rgyud): yin pas na'o//phyag na rdo rje yang gsol pa/ci bcom ldan 'das bems po 'am/rdo rje 'dzin kyis bka' stsal pa //da ni me long lta bu'i ye shes so//me long lta bu'i ye shes
75a6(rgyud): de ji lta bu /rdo rje 'dzin kyis bka' bstsal pa/de bden pa yang ma yin zhing /rdzun pa yang ma yin la/blang bar bya ba yang ma yin/la dor bar bya ba yang ma yin no//de bzhin du mnyam pa nyid
75a7(rgyud): kyi ye shes kyang nga'o//so sor rtog pa ye shes kyang nga'o//bya ba nan tan kyi ye shes kyang nga'o//de bzhin du rnam par dag pa'i chos kyi dbyings kyi ye shes kyang nga'o//de pas na byang chub sems
75a8(rgyud): dpa' sems dpa' chen po byang chub 'di de skad du dngos po med par ma smra shig//dngos po med nyid dngos po 'ang nga//de bzhin gshegs par 'dod nas grol//mchog ni sku dang ldan pa ste//
75b1(rgyud): bzab cing rgya che ba'i sku mnga' ba//he ru ka nga'o sems dpa' che//bdag med ma dang thabs cig sbyar//bems po ma yin bde chen nga//'jig rten bzhug phyir bems po nga;;
75b2(rgyud): bud med skyes pa ma ning dang//'gro ba rnams la ngas khyab po//de nyid kyis na gsang 'dus dang//rgyal ba'i rgya mtshor ngas gshad do//ma ning gnas po 'ang 'grub pa ste;;byang chub
75b3(rgyud): sems dpa' rdo rje can//lha mo ma ma kyi de gang//bud med rang bzhin ma yin la//sangs rgyas he ru ka nga min//'di nyid phyir na ma ning ngo//'gro nyid dngos por mthong na nga;;thabs
75b4(rgyud): dang shes rab bdag nyid do//shin tu sbyor ba'i rnal 'byor gyis//ma ma kyi med mi skyod med//don dam pa'i rang bzhin go 'phang chen po rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//;//
75b5(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'dapa' chen po//rdo rje 'dod pa bde chen po//bde chen yan lag med rdo rjes//gsung ni 'di skad bka' stsal to//phyag drug po ni sgeg dang bcas
75b6(rgyud): zhal cig pa ste thor tshugs 'chang//rgya phrag gnyis lon 'do ga ser po//yan lag med pa'i rdo rje sgrub//dpa' bo g.yas na 'da' snams shing//gnyis pa'i phyag na ral gri ste;
75b7(rgyud): gsuM par me long shes par bya//g.yon pa'i phyag na gzhu snams shing//gnyis par ud pal dmar po nyid//gsum par lo ma me tog ldan//g.yas rkyang ba'i gnas pas bzhugs;;
75b8(rgyud): phyag drug pa na nyid du sgoM//bu mo'i snying khar bsams nas ni//bud med thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//mda' yis 'o ma 'dzin phug nas//mya ngan med kyi snying khar snan//ral gri mi
76a1(rgyud): //mo sdigs bya zhing//me long yang ni de bzhis stan//gzhu 'gyengs thabs kyis gnas bsams te//ud pal zhags pas bcings te dgug//gang 'dod rnam par myos
76a2(rgyud): gsams te//skra dgol 'dod pa rtson chen ma//snyan pa'i tshig ni rab smra zhing//gad mo rnam par rgod par byed//yan lag 'grin skad gis rgyan spro//gos ni las khar ni bkar nas ni;;
76a3(rgyud): 'dod pa'i stobs dang bral bar bsam//rdo rje mchod pa rab sbyor byas//rtan de sems ldan rjes chags bya'o//'dod byed sngags ni mi shigs pas//snying khar de ni skul bar bya'o////
76a4(rgyud): oM che ge mo a ka ri sha ya ma rdzu go sha d+h+ri dza//de ltar 'jam pa'i 'gon po ni//yid la sgom par byas nas ni//bud med rnams la dbang 'gyur te//sgrub pa pos 'dod pa
76a5(rgyud): spyod;;zla phyed tsam gyi sbyor ba yis//zla ba khri ru rab sbyar zhing//sgrub bya dga' ba'i sems ldan na//gser gi rna cha g.yo bag can//ha~M yig sa bon las skyed pa'o//
76a6(rgyud): rgyan rnams kun kyis rab 'dzes pa'o//myos byed btung ba'i rab sbyor bas//rdo rje yan lag med pa 'grub//bde dang sdug sngal rang bzhin ni//sa bon mchog ni mi shigs pas;;bde
76a7(rgyud): chen mchog tu zhi ba ste//sems can thams cad mgu bar mdzad//de nyid kyis ni srog chen po//mchog dga' ba ste rab mdzes pa'o//rtag tu bde dang sdug sngal ni//de'i spro ba grags pa
76a8(rgyud): yin;;mya ngan gzir dang de bzhin nad//bde dang bde bral de bzhin nyid//rdo rje sems dpa' rgyal po che//de nyid dbyangs yig drug pas mchog//zla ba phyed pas rab ldan zhing;;thig
76b1(rgyud): les spyi bor mchod pa'o//lha rnams kun kyi bzhi po ste//rgyud rnams kyi yang khyad par ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad/bcom ldan 'das byang chub kyi
76b2(rgyud): sems rdo rje 'dzin pa chen po'i sa bon gi yi ge gsan pa'i thugs rab tu dges par 'gyuro//gyur nas 'di skad ces gsungaso//bsam gtan med cing dngos po 'ang med//sngags med
76b3(rgyud): lha yang med pa ste//rdo rje sems las rab byung ba//byang chub tshul 'di brtan pa'o//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje dam tshig gis de skad ces bka' stsal to//bsod nams med cing sdig pa
76b4(rgyud): 'ang med||byang chub med cing sangs rgyas med//ye shes lnga'i rab sbyor bas//byang chub sems 'di brtan pa'o//bcom dan 'das sangs rgyas pa'i dam tshig gis/de skad ces bka'
76b5(rgyud): stsal to//sngags med zlas pa 'ang med pa ste//chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin gyis//'gro ba don gsum kun yod pa ste//sgom pa med cing sgom pa 'ang med||
76b6(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das rin po che'i dam tshig gis 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/kun rdzob tu ni 'jig rten yod//spyod pas gang na 'jig rten cing //rtag tu 'jig rten ldan par
76b7(rgyud): 'byung||byang chub tshul 'di rtan pa'o//bcom ldan 'da'as pad ma'i dam tshigs gis 'di skad ces bka'-i stsal to//skye ba med cing dmigs pa med//mi byed nam mkha' 'byung gnas
76b8(rgyud): te||zab cing rgya che'i rang bzhin can//byang chub tshul 'di rten pa'o//bcom ldan 'das pad ma'i dam tshig gis 'di skad zhes bka' stsal to//sgyu ma 'am me long bzhin||de bzhin
77a1(rgyud): @#//bde dang bsdug bsngal te//dngos rnams ji bzhin yongasu shes//byang chub tshul 'di brtan pa'o//bcom ldan 'das yum mkha' 'gro ma bdag med mas 'di skad ces bka'
77a2(rgyud): stsal to//stong zhing bdag med gnas kyi mchog//rmi lam sgyu ma'i rang bzhin can//sems kyi rang bzhin sgom pa ste//ji srid du yang med pa'o//zhes bya ba ma ma kyi'o//sred med mya
77a3(rgyud): ngan 'das med tshe//de 'di rdo rje bde chen po//sgom pa med cing sgom pa 'ang med//dgos pa mchog tu sgrub pa'o//ma ma ki spyan can ma'i'o//dbyangs dang yi ge rnam bral te//de ni sems dang
77a4(rgyud): thig le min//'di ni bde chen dam pa ste//bra shing snan pa yod ma yin//zhes pa gos kar ma'i'o//dper na gzugs rnyan rang bzhin ni//de bzhin sgom bya'i dngos gang yang
77a5(rgyud): stong zhing snang med gnas mchog med//de tshe sdig dang bsod naMs med//dper na chu nang zla ba ni//grogs dang bden min rdzun pa 'ang min//de bzhin dkyil 'khor 'khor lo 'od/
77a6(rgyud): dangs zhing gsal ba'i rang bzhin no//zhes pa ni sgrol ma'o//de nas gzhu las mda' rab tu 'bebs pa 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las 'byung ngo//gong 'dzub sing rna'i bar 'dzub
77a7(rgyud): gyis;;khu tshur dag gis mda' bzung ste//khu tshur g.yon par gzhu gzhag la//pha rol dpung la gzir cing sdigs//dpung mgos gos ko rab bzung ste//mtshan char khu tshur gis grangs te;
77a8(rgyud): bdud kyi dkyil 'khor rab sdigs par//yi ge hUM gis 'phang bar bya//pus gnyis bcung zad skul byas nas//gzhu 'di dbyugs rdub gis dgang zhing //khu tshur rna ltag tu bzung bas;;brang ni
77b1(rgyud): rgyas par dgang byas te//dmig dang sems ni mtshon byar btang//kha ni hU~M gis dgang byas te//g.yon rkyang ba'i basbyor ba yis //srin po gshin rje gsad par bya//mang du smras pas
77b2(rgyud): 'dis ci bya//sdus pa tsam 'di 'jug bya ste//gzhu'i ri'i rig byed 'ol pa ni//de bzhin rdo rje 'dzin pas spyad//de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje rol mo gsum zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la
77b3(rgyud): snyoMs par bzhugs te//rnaM par sgyeg pa'i rol mo 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las byung ngo////thi hi thi hi;tha ka tha ka;ar thu tha ar thu tha;thi ho thi ho;'bi hi 'bi hi;u
77b4(rgyud): lde a ldeM;kr-i ka kr-i ka ma ka;tha ka tha ka;ti l+Ti ma ti lkim;////cha lang gsil bu khar lnga dang//pi wang rdza rnga kha cig dang//kha ni pin tsa ra zhes byab//pi ldor dang ni bling bu nyid//mu kun ta
77b5(rgyud): ka'i rda dang ni//de bzhin kar ta ka sa//rnam gsuM rab dbye rdur bya ste//dal dang mig 'dzums thal bdebs pa'o//'di ni dkyil 'khor snyen bkur ba la bla med pa'i mchod pa'o
77b6(rgyud): rang gi tshig gi gshad pa yis//mang por smras pa 'dis ci bya;ji srid klu dkar bstan chos bzhin//rdo rje can gyi rol mo de'o;;de nas bcom ldan 'das dri zhim pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i
77b7(rgyud): ting nge 'dzin la snyoMs par bzhugs te/bdug pa'i mchod pa 'di nyid kyis sku dang thugs rdo rje las byung ngo//tsan dan dar ni gur gum nyid//drag gzhun de bzhin sder ldan te//spos ni spos
77b8(rgyud): chen de rnaMs kyis//he ru ka la rab dbul bya//ka tso ra dang tso ka nyid//de bzhin sgra sgra zhes pa nyid//spos su grub pa de rnaMs kyis//rtag pa la ni nges par sbyar//lce pog dang ni bo ba nyid;
79a1(rgyud): //de bzhin sder mo ri dgas te//spos de rin chen rgyal po la'o//a ru dwang bur ram nyid//thams cad la ni ldan par bya'o//sha dri dri zhim nyi sha dri nyid//drag zhum de bzhin
79a2(rgyud): shi la ka//'jig rten 'gon po bdug par ni//rdo rje 'dzin gis lhag par gsungs//sna tshogs dri'i khyad par rnaMs//sna tshogs rdo rje mang po ste//rta bdun rgyal po'i mchod pa ste;
79a3(rgyud): tshar bcad sgrub par dus gsum du//ji srid dri'i stan chos las//bdug pa'i ri lu phye ma mchog//de kun phyag na rdo rje'i//rdo rje mchod par rab tu grags//de nas bcom ldan 'das
79a4(rgyud): rdo rje gom pa'i rnam par dgod pa //rnam par rol pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs te//gar 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo;;
79a5(rgyud): mtshon cha dang bcas bsgyegs bcas mchog//rol bcas mchog gi rjes sgyur zhing//rdo rje gom ba rgyad rnams kyis/rdo rje go par gar bya'o//me tog snying par rab sbyar
79a6(rgyud): dang //bdug snod 'dzin tshul rab sbyor zhing//lag par mar me'i snod 'degs tshul//byug pas kyang ni byug tshulo//de bzhin lag pas mig tshul dang//skur rten rkang pa
79a7(rgyud): phyogasu skor//dkrol pa dang bstun sbyor bas skor//de bzhin slar yang zlog cing skor//de nas rgyun pa'i gar bya ste//skyed pa'i gar ni yang bya'o//de bzhin dkrol myur ba'i dgar;;
79a8(rgyud): byas te 'khor lo rab tu skor//gru bzhi ru ni gdengs byas te//phyis ni pad ma'i lag pas skor//sa la pus mo gnyis btsugs te//lag pa mchod rten byed lta bu//ji srid gar gi stan
79b1(rgyud): chos su;;mngon par bshad pas byas pa ni//ji srid rdo rje sems dpa'i //rnaM par 'ol pas byang chub sgrub//de nas bcom ldan 'das dam tshig brnyes pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoMs
79b2(rgyud): par zhugs ste//tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//rang gi lhag pa'i lhar dmigs te//bzang po'i rgyan gis rnam rgyal la//phun suM mtshogs pa kun
79b3(rgyud): dmigs na sa//lto ba'i gnasu sreg lugs bya//shar dang nub dang byang lho dang ni//phyogasu gnas pa rnaMs phyir mchod//lha mo phyogs bral gnas rnaMs la//lhag pa'i tshe ni mchog gi mchod;;yid
79b4(rgyud): kyi mar me sbyar ba dang//so ba'i 'bras dang mal cha 'bras;;'gru rnaMs dang ni zho zan dang//yam shing de bzhin til la bsam//dur pa'i chun po nyid dang ni//bsam gtan rdo rje dag gis
79b5(rgyud): sreg;;de la sngags kyi tshig rnaMsu 'gyur ba ni 'di rnamaso;;oM ag ne ye hUM mar gyi'o//oM sarba pa pA da ha badzra ya badzra swo twa sa;sarba pa pa dwaM swAhA//til nag po'i'o//oM rba dzra puSh+Ta
79b6(rgyud): ye swAhA;;'bras kyi rgyal po ma chag pa'i'o//oM swa swa hA;zho zan gyi'o//oM badzra b+ha dzaM ya swahA//'bras so ba'i'o//oM ma ha b+he ga ye;;swe hA nas kyi'o//oM ma ha pa la ya swahA//mon dre'u 'i'o;;
79b7(rgyud): oM badzra g+ha sma ri swahA//gro'i'o//oM badzra a yu she swahA//dur pa'i chun po'i 'o//oM badzra a pra ti ha ta badzra ya swahA//ku sha'i 'o//oM badzra dz+ya nya ya swahA//u dum b+ha ra'i'o//oM b+ho d+hi b+hi kri ya
79b8(rgyud): swahA;;byang chub shing gi'o//oM badzra la kra ya swahA;;shing klag sha'i 'o//oM badzra ku pe ra ya swahA//shing n+ya tro ta'i 'o//oM swa hA ka ra ya swahA//shing oM mra'i'o;;tsam pa mya ngan med dang;;pun
80a1(rgyud): @#//na ka/ka ta pa dang/a rdzu na'i me tog nyid//ji lta bur ni snyen pa zlas byas te//me tog 'di dag gis ni sbyin sreg bya'o//rgu 'brum a na pa na sa nyid dang//kha rdzur dang na ri ke
80a2(rgyud): la ste//'khor lo lnga por cho ga'i rim pa yis//'bras bu de rnaMs kyis ni sbyin sreg bya'o//shar lnga bdud rtsi lnga nyid dang//dkyil 'khor lnga'i sgom pa yis//'di yis rdo rje can gnyes shing
80a3(rgyud): ye shes me yang rab mnyes so//rgyal ba rtag par mnyes pa dang//gser gi 'od kyang mnyes gyur te//pad ma gar dbang mnyes 'gyur zhing//rta mchog bdag po mnyes par 'gyur//tshangs pa
80a4(rgyud): rgyal chen dbang phyug che//khyab 'jug rlung dang chu chen dang//'dod lha dang ni gshol d+ha 'dzin//dang nyi ma zla ba bza' gzhan dang//sa dang zhags pa 'dzin pa dang//de bzhin nor sbyin
80a5(rgyud): khrag mthung dang//chos rgyal dang ni klu nyid dang//dri za lha dang lha min dang//sum bcu gsuM gnas lha rnaMs dang//'og min na ni gnas rnams dang//rgyal chen rigs na
80a6(rgyud): gnas rnams dang//gzhan 'phrul dbang byed pa rnaMs dang//'phrul dga'i ni lha rnams dang//gtsang ma'i ni gnas na gnas rnaMs dang//'od zer can gi 'jig rten gi//khams na gnas pa rnaMs nyid

86b5(rgyud): cad kyis//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la zhus pa/bcom Ildan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang//dpal rdo rje 'dzin pas dbang skur ba'i slob dpon la ji ltar blta bar bgyi//de
86b6(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa rnaMs kyis gsungs pa/'gro ba'i ston pa slob dpon ste//sangs rgyas kun kyis stod byas pa//bdag gi (kyang )ni pha dang mar//'di ltar de ni yid lags sam//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de
86b7(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis gsungs pa/rdo rje sems dpa' sangs rgyas dang//rdo rje chos dang gzi brjid dang//rta bdun rgyal po'i dngos grub ni//gang phyir des ni dka' ba min//mig sman dang 
86b8(rgyud): ni rkang 'gyogs dang//ral gri dang ni sa 'og grub//ri lu dang ni mkha' spyod nyid//mi snang ba dang bcud kyis len//gang phyir des ni rdo rjes can//mnyes par byas pas myur du thob;;
87a1(rgyud): @#//de phyir slob dpon mdun byon nas//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la phyag 'tshalo//gang nyid rdo rje dpa' zhes//de nyid slob dpon gzugasu 'dzin//sems can phan gdags 
87a2(rgyud): dmigs nas ni//tha mal pa'i gzugasu gnas//slob dpon 'da' byed dus na ni//'jig rten pa ni shi zhes rol//rmongs ldan mi rnams gang zer ba//shi zhes bde 'di rmongs pa'o//shi ba zhes
87a3(rgyud): bya rnam rtog ste//dag pa'i gnasu 'khrid pa'o//yang na bde ba can du 'am skye//gang 'dod rigasu skyes 'gyur ba//sna tshogs me tog lag 'brel cing//sna tshogs rgyal mtshan byug pa dang//sna tshogs
87a4(rgyud): klu'i tshogs rnams dang//rdo rje 'dzin bcas rgyal srid kyis//sna tshogs rin chen cher 'khor zhing//sna tshogs rgyan dang tshigs gcad kyis//dbang skur gzugs nyid kyis ni//lus

87a6(rgyud): rdo rje gti mug bswogs//rdo rje gsum ni gnas gsum dgod//rdo rje thugs khar rab sbyor kyis//'dus byed rab tu bya bar bstan//nyi ma gsum ni lon pa na//rus pa'-i 'phreng bsdus byas

87b1(rgyud): hA mu tra ti la ka na ma ma ha yo gI ni;dan ta ra tsa b+hi ra dza~M//bod skad du/dpal phyag na rgya chen po'i thig le zhes bya ba'i rnal 'byor ma chen mo'i rgyud kyi rgyal po'i mnga' bdag //dpal phyag rgya chen po'i
87b2(rgyud): thig le la phyag 'tshalo//bcom ldan 'das mkha' 'gro'i ye shes nyid //gsang bde snying po'i kha 'byed ma//gsang pad lte ba'i nang bzhugs pa//bdag gi zhu khyod gson cig;;ye shes rgya mtsho
87b3(rgyud): kye rdo rje //'bum lnga yi ni bdag nyid rgyud//gang zhig khyod kyis de nyid gsungs//sbyor ba drug rnal 'byor ma'i bzhung//thams cad mkhyen pa dbang phyug mchog//de nas dbang po bdag gis
87b4(rgyud): shes;;don dam par ji lta bu //de ltar bdag gis the tsom skyes//char yang thig le'i tshul dang ni//tshul tsam mchog gi mchog gzhan min//sbyor ba drug shes rnam par shes//de'i gnas ni gang na
87b5(rgyud): yod;;rdo rje 'dzin pa gang du rlag dor seMs mtshan mo gang nyin ci;;'tshams gang shar ba gang zhig lags//gang na nub pa'i dus dang rtsa//mchog gi dbang phyug du tsam mchis//dus
87b6(rgyud): kyi mtshan nyid gang lags gsungs//de'i dus kyi don ci byed //a li ka li bya ba gang//mnga' bdag gang na byung ba lags//ro'i bye brag du zhig tu bstan te//dga' ba yang
87b7(rgyud): ni de bzhin no//gang gi dus kyi dga' ba rnams//rdo rje dbang phyug 'byung ba gsungs//chos kyi dbyings ni ji ltar bshad//rdo rje yang ni ji ltar bstan//bdag gi sgra ni ji ltar gsungs;;
87b8(rgyud): gsang ba zhes gyi ji ltar lags//mchog ces bgyi ba ji ltar lags//dges pa'i don ni gsal bar mdzod //rigs kyi bye brag du tsam stan//phung po lasogs skye mched cing//tshangs dang
88a1(rgyud): @#//khyab 'jug drag po dang//dbang phyug rtag tu zhi ba dang//gang nas skye zhing gang du gnas//chen po'i chen po de ji lta bu//phyag rgya'i bye brag du zhig bshad//phyag rgya chen po
88a2(rgyud): ji ltar lags//mgon po mchod pa'i bye brag cing//de ru me tog mchog sbyin ni//mgon po chen po mchog sbyin ni//ji lta bu zhig 'tshal ba lags//mchog gi dngos po'i dkyil 'khor
88a3(rgyud): ni;;bcom ldan 'das de ji lta bu//de ru lha mo ci 'dra ba//lha mo bgyid pa gang lags gsungs//bcom ldan du khrod du zhig mchis//de ru shing ni ji ltar bshad//dur khrod dang ni
88a4(rgyud): shing rnaMs ni//rgyal ba'i dbang phyug de nas gsungs//mgon po'i nang gi sbyin sreg dang//bdud rtsi lnga'i cho ga dang;;rnal 'byor ma'i 'dus pa dang/de ru skad ni ji lta bu//
88a5(rgyud): bdag nyid kyi ni rang ngo bo//mgon po chen po khyod kyis gsungs//dpal phyag rgya chen po'i thig le las////rgyud thams cad kyi rgyud zhes bya ba'i le'u ste dang po';
88a6(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//dris pa yang dag gting dpag dka'//snying po yi yi ni snying po mchog//sangs rgyas kun gyi gsang chen po//mchog las mchog gi yi dag
88a7(rgyud): mchog;;lha chen po ni lha bsogs pa//bzang mo de ru byol song yin//don dam de ni khyod cig bu+o//zhal bzang po ni snod yin no//gsang ba'i gsang ba yang dag rab//nyon cig
88a8(rgyud): yang dag bdag gis bshad//dang po re zhig gar che slob//lan cig dbang rnams kyi ni skur//de tshe gsang chen bshad pa yin//nges par snod du 'gyur ba'o//dbang med na ni dngos
88b1(rgyud): grub med;;bye ma tsir yang mar med ltar//gang rgyud lung nga rgyal gis//dbang skur med na gsal byed pa //slob dpon slob ma shi ma thag//dngos grub thob kyang dmyal bar 'gro//de

88b4(rgyud): ma de lta bu la bya//de lta bu'i bla ma yis//'bad pas slob ma rtag par bya//de ltar sreg la sogs pa'i gser//ji ltar mkhas pa'i rin po che//ji ltar bde ldan bu mo ni//de ltar slob
88b5(rgyud): ma rtag par bya//de bas thams cad rab 'bad de//phan tshun so so rtag par bya//de ltar min na skyon du 'gyur//nam yang dngos grub thob mi 'gyur//ji ltar dug la
88b6(rgyud): rab sngags pa'i//dag par byas na bdud rtsir 'gyur;;de bzhin slob ma dbang bsogs kyis//dus ni dri ma med par 'gyur//gang zhig dbang skur sbyin byin pa//de ni dor
88b7(rgyud): sems lta bur lta//bla ma sprul pa rgyal lta bur lta/dpal ni bla ma me 'drar lta//bla ma ston pas lhag par lta/yid bzhin rin chen mnyam par lta/ji ltar rab sbyor dang sngags kyis//sprul gi
88b8(rgyud): rgyal po'i spyi bo las//tshad med pa'i rin chen rnyed//dbul po thams cad med par byed//rab sbyor log dang sngags log gis//dga' mo nges par tshig par byed//de lta bu'i cho ga yis
89a1(rgyud): /khrims la bsogs pa'i sbyor ba yis//dpal zhal de nyid rin chen dbang //tshe 'di nyid kyis rnyed par 'gyur//de ltar min na de ma gus na//gsung gi mes des tshig byed

89a6(rgyud): 'bras bu dpa' gsum dang bral//yang na bdag nyid gsum po yin//dga' ba'i bye brag shes par bya//'khor lo rnams kyi ris pas so//nga ro yis ni gsuM pa'i tshe//slob ma rabs la dbang

90a1(rgyud): //sgyu 'phrul chen po 'gro ba'i ma//char yang grangs med rten pa yin//de ni mchog dang rab mchog phra//skye med gnyis med bsam du med//rlung gi 'khor lo skyed byed me//sangs rgyas kyis

91b7(rgyud): rtsa kun 'dzag 'gyur te//snod 'dzeg pa'i chu thigs bzhin//de nas sprul pa'i 'khor lo ni//'dus nas gcig tu gyur ba'o//rtsa gnyis kha'i rtse mo las//zla ba dang ni nyi ma 'dzag//dngal kyi dbus

92a1(rgyud): @#//phyogs nyi ma ni|de tshe bde ba dmar por 'gyur//g.yon nas zla ba 'byung ba'i phyir//de ni bde ba dkar por 'gyur//de gnyis nang na mi gnas pa//sgong skyes gnyid log (ji)bzhin no/

92a3(rgyud): 'dod chags chen po'i yid//reg bya bdun pa mchog tu 'gyur//b+ho la la ni ka ko zhugs//ji ltar chu la chu bzhag bzhin//de tshe sa bon 'byung ba yin//sa bon las ni gong bu 'byung //

92b1(rgyud): ye shes rang bzhin skye|rgya rtsa brgyad kyis rim pas so//rdo rje 'phreng ba'i rgyud chen las //sngar ni bdag gi bstan pa'o//mtshan mo gang yin nyin gang yin//mtshan mo bdag gis gshad par

93a6(rgyud): ni tshod ni bces bstan pa ste//dus la bye brag gnyis su dbyed//dus ni bsam du med pa dang//shin du'i dus ni dus yin no//shin du dus dang ngan dus dang//sna'i 'phar ma bug ga gnyis//'od pa

93b6(rgyud): rang bzhin de bzhin no//dga' ba zhes bya snang ba yin//mchog ni snang ba 'ched par stan//chad pa snang ba thob ces bya//rang bzhin de ni yang dag 'od//de'i dus na skad cig skyes
93b7(rgyud): sna tshogs la sogs mtshan nyid do//snang ba zla ba zhesu bstan//snang ba 'ched pa nyi ma'o//mtshams ni snang ba zhesu thob par grags//gsum dang bral ba yang dag yod//gang tshe mdud pa'i nang

94a1(rgyud): //'gyur ba'o//gang zhig gsum bstan dang gsum spangs pa//de ni de kho rang bzhin shar//de ni gsang ba chen po'i mchog//gsang ba'i gsang ba yang dag mchog//'dus pa

94a3(rgyud): gsal bar byas//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang //gzhan ma shi med kun nyon mongs//de lta'i mchog gi de kho na//bdag min na ni de mi shes//gzhan yang chos kyi dbyings grags

94a6(rgyud): dbyings ni b+ha gar grags//kha ga rin chen snod yin no//dbang phyug la sogs yon tan ldan//de ni ba ga kun tu grags//chos ni b+ha ga zhes byar bstan//dbyings ni sems su shes par dran//

94b1(rgyud): kyi mtshan nyid dang ////chos kyi dbyings bstan pa zhes bya ba'i le'u ste drug pa'o////gzhan yang nyon cig bdag gis gshad//ji bzhin rdo rje'i mtshan nyid do//mi phyed rdo rje zhesu bstan;;

94b3(rgyud): de ni rdo rjer kun tu grags//bdag gi sgra'i nges pa'i don//nyon cig bdag gis de nyid gshad//a ni yig 'bru gzugs kyi mchog//thams cad kun ni lus nang yod//rnam pa thams cad
94b4(rgyud): mchog gi rdzogs//kun yod thams cad ma skyes mkhyen//rtag tu stong pa thams cad khyab//thaMs cad kun cig byed pa'i rgyu//srid pa srid mchog nyin mo ni//sprul pa'i sku'i bde ba'o;;

94b6(rgyud): par grol//thams cad byad gzugs rnam par gral//dngos dang dngos min rnam par grol//skye dang 'jig pa'i byed pa po//dngos po thaMs cad nub pa po//chos kyi sku'i rang bzhin

94b8(rgyud): bdag ni thams cad thaMs cad nang //thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu bzhugs//bdag nyid thams cad dngos po la//khyad par de ni yod pa ste//bdag ni min na 'gro ba kun//ji ltar rtsa bcad shing bzhino;;
95a1(rgyud): @#//ha~M gi yi ge mtshams mtha' mar//a'i yig 'bru mgo bo'i rtser//a'i yig 'bru zla ba dag//dam gi yig 'bru nyi ma'i 'od//a'i yig 'bru rten yin te//ha~M gi yig 'bru rten pa'o;;
95a2(rgyud): a'i yig 'bru srog ces bya//ha~M gi yig 'bru rtsol ba dran//gang zhig gnasu de gnyis sbyor//de ni bdag nyid gsal bar 'gyur//rgyud ni rnams thams cad kun las rnyed dka' ba//ne ni yang dag bdag sgrar

95a5(rgyud): gsang ba yin//gsang ba khu bar bstan pa'o//khu ba srog ces bstan pa ste//srog ni rten du dran pa'o//rten ni sar ni shes par bya//khams gsuM kun gi 'byung ba'o//
95a6(rgyud): mchog ni chu zhes bstan pa ste//chu ni srog gi tshe yin no//chad pa de ni 'od ces bstan//'od ces ni rnam par dag ces dran//rnam par dag pa bde chen dngos//de ni rang bzhin bstan pa'o;;
95a7(rgyud): rnam par dag pa 'byung min gnas//bcas pa rnam dag brjod ma yin//bcas pa dngos min zhes byar bstan//dngos min rnam dag brjod ma yin/rnam dag phra mo'i phra mo ste//phra mo'i rnam dag
95a8(rgyud): gzhan na gnas//phra mo'i rang bzhin zhes byar bstan//gzung dang 'dzin pa rnam par spangs//rang dngos bde ba chen po ni//ye shes rlung du rab tu grags//de ni nam mkha' zhes su gshad//nam mkha'
95b1(rgyud): stong par bstan pa'o//stong pa la ni thams cad 'byung//stong pa'i nang du thaMs cad brlag//stong pa yang dag dri med gnas//rnam pa thams cad dang bral ba//mchog gi dbang po skye med dag/

97b1(rgyud): ni ka ma ru pa'o//nye ba'i gnas ni ma lar stan//sin d+hu ra la gar sing nga//zhing ni mu mu ni ru dang grags//de bzhin 'di bi ko ta dang//nye ba'i zhing ni ku lu ta;;ar pu tar ni de bzhin dran//ha re

97b5(rgyud): rtse ste;;sin du mig gnyis yin par grags//na gar rna ba gnyis su dran//sing khar kha'i pad ma'i gnas//mu mu ni ni kos ko'o;;de ba ko ta 'grin kling du//ku lu ta ni rgyab kling du;

97b7(rgyud): ling gzhan ka'i gnasu ste//kong ka na ni rla gnyis dran//b+hin d+ha pus mo gnyis yin no//yi dgas 'dus pa byin pa ste//rkang pa gnyisu rgya mtsho'i ngogs//ko'u mar bur ni 'the bora'o;;tsa ri
97b8(rgyud): traM ni sor mor ro//kha che'i yul ni lus rnaMs kun//ke sa ra ni rkang 'thil lo//ne ba la ni ltag pa ste//ka nu dzi ni rkyed pa'o//de ni don dam pa'i gnas//ri bong can 'od me tog dang;;me
98a1(rgyud): @#//tog dmar po nyi mnyam gyis//mchod pa'i dus su mchod par bya//srog gcod pa'i mchod sbyin ni//'bad pa'i de ru dbul bar bya//pad ma chen po'i dbus gnasu//srog ni bcad p+Ra

150a3(rgyud): 'das dang a ba d+ho ti'o//de lta bu yis nang gi 'phar ma bde ba chen po'i 'khor lo na yod pa'i rtsa sum bcu rtsa gnyis rnam par dag pas//phyi ma phyi ma'i rgyud kyi go rims kyis rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi nang gi nang
150a4(rgyud): gi rnam par dag pa'o//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po lha'i dbang phyug phyi rol du mtshan dang /dpe byad dang ldan pa rnam pa dang bcas pa brgyad gnyis kyi lo//byad gzugs rgyas pa chen po'i
150a5(rgyud): rgyas pa gregs pa/sgyeg pa'i nga rgyal can /phyag bzhi pa spyan gsum pa/sbra zhing mtho ba'i nu ma dang /rus pa sna tshogs kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa/phyag rgya lnga 'dzin pa/g.yon rkyang ba ro'i steng na
150a6(rgyud): ka pa la dang/kha taM kha dang/mda' gzhu 'khyengs par 'gyengs pa/bdud grangs med pa 'joms pa kho na/sku mdog sna tshogs mdzes pa'i gzugs 'dzin pa//thugs rje chen pos seMs can 'gugs pa/
150a7(rgyud): sems can gyi don mdzad par brtson pa/brel zhing long med pa'i sems dang ldan pa'o//phyi'i 'phar ma shar dang byang dang nub dang/lho 'tshams re rer nang gi 'tshams kyi lha mo bzhin du lha mo gnyis gnyis te//
150a8(rgyud): sgra dmar ma dang rin chen ma dang//phug ron ma dang ser mo dang//nag mo dang ri bong ma dang//ri dgas mo dang ra ba mo'o//shar dang nub dang/lho sgo re rer sgo skyong ma gnyis gnyis te/dregs chen 'jigs

150b2(rgyud): kha taM kha 'dzin pa/spyan gsum pa phyag rgya lngas brgyan pa'o//gcer bu skra bgrol ba mi'i rus pa sna tshogs kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa/g.yon rkyang ba mgo bo'i chun 'phyang ba rgyu ma la rus pa 'chang ba'o//byang
150b3(rgyud): sgor rdo rje rtse mo dang /de bzhin du rdo rje zlum pa dang /sku mdog ser po seng ge dang /bya khyung bsnams pa'o//nub sgor rdo rje ska rags ma dang /rdo rje ser mo ma/gling bu dang rdza rnga chen po'i rol mo sgra kho na'o;;
150b4(rgyud): lho sgor rdo rje rgod ma dang /rdo rje nyi ma/sku mdog ljang khu rdza rnga dang pi wang gi rol mo'i sgra kho na ste/ji ltar shar sgor bzhug+sa pa'i rnal 'byor ma/de ltar thams cad rus pa laswogs pa'i rgyan dang 
150b5(rgyud): bzhugs thabs dang phyag mtshan dang ldan pa'o//longs spyod kyi 'khor lo'i nang na yod pa'i rtsa brgyad gnyis kyi grangs de dag kun gyi rnam par dag pa'o//sku brgyad bdag nyid kyis ni mgon;;
150b6(rgyud): mchod sbyin sgra yis kun tu btags//mchod pa de ni mchod sbyin marjod//rtsa kun gyi ni gnas rab mchog//gnas ni bdan laswogs pas dges//gnas kun gyi ni bla na med//gsang chen dag pa pad
150b7(rgyud): ma yis;;'dab ma brgyad pa lte bar bcas//bde ba yi ni yangs pa'i sar//rtsa ba'i dkyil 'khor gyis brgyan no//gnas ni bdan la bya bar grags//gnas ni rten du brjod pa'o//bde ba 'byung
150b8(rgyud): ba'i ngal sos ni//de yi zhing ni don dam par//bde ba'i ye shes 'byung ba ste//tshan d+ho har ni 'di ru bshad//thig le gsum gyi ye shes bde//gcig tu byed pa'i brtan tshigs te//de ni 'dus
151a1(rgyud): @#//par bstan pa yin//rnal 'byor rgyud du brnyed dka' ba//gang du tshor ba yod pa ste//bde ba 'byung ba'i brlab bde bzhin//de nyid 'di ru dur khrod brjod//yang na bde ba'i rnam sheso/
151a2(rgyud): gang du bde ba yang dag brnyed//de ni byi la pa ru grags//skye dang rlag pa'i ye shes te//gang du bde ba'i zla ba sgye//de ni lam rab tu ni bstan//'gro ba'i bdag nyid lam min no//bde ba'i rgyal po
151a3(rgyud): gang zhig 'gog//de ni sbyor ba'i ye shes rab//de ni gnas brgyad bgyes pa yin//sku brgyad ro yi ngo bo can//ye shes brgyad kyi bdag nyid dag//sangs rgyas kun gyis kyang brnyed dka'//rtsa kun gyi
151a4(rgyud): ni dag pa bzhi//de la yang ni rtsa kun gnas//nye ba'i gnas ni thams cad kun//lha'i dbang phyug rnam par smin//bskyed dang rdzogs pa'i rim pa yis//thig le drug ni rab tu grags//thig le
151a5(rgyud): dbus na ye shes yod//gsung gi lam gyis brnyed par dka'//bskyed pa dang ni rdzogs pa med//drug min de bzhin gsum pa min//gsum dang bral ba gsum par grags//ye shes rang
151a6(rgyud): bzhin de skyes med//nyi ma'i thig le ye shes shar//'bad pa yis ni mchod sbyin bya//gang tshe mchog 'byung ma shar bar//de tshe nyi ma 'byung mi 'gyur//zla ba de ni chad pa'i dga'//chags
151a7(rgyud): pas 'di ru brlag par byed//nyi ma'i dbus na zla ba 'byung//zla ba'i dbus na nyi ma shar//dri ma med phyir yangs par 'gro//shes rab bdag nyid gzugs kyi phyir//thams cad la ni tshim par
151a8(rgyud): mdzad;;thams cad kyi ni bskyed pa'i rgyu//'bras bu byed pa'i bdag nyid zla//rgyu byed bdag nyid nyi ma'o//rgyu byed bdag nyid zla ba ste//'bras byed bdag nyid nyi ma'o//rgyu dang 'bras bu gnyis med pa'o
151b1(rgyud): rgyu dang 'bras bu gnyis med pa'o//bde ba chags chen bdag nyid do//chags pa chen po dngos med na//ji ltar bde ba 'byung bar 'gyur//bde ba ye shes dri med grags//tha mal bdag nyid bde ba min/
151b2(rgyud): byol song bag chags rab rmongs pa//dbang po gnyis kyis bde bar mdzod//bde ba mchog gi rang rig pa//chags pa dang ni chags bral med//bde ba'i thig le'i nang du ni//char yang nang du
151b3(rgyud): bde ba tshe//de gnyis ye shes tshul gyi nang//rnam par shes pa tshul tsam nang//rnam par shes dang ye shes bral//gzhan dang gzhan min nang yod pa'o//sbyor drug dges pa rnam par shes//
151b4(rgyud): char yang rtsa ba kun btags bral//rtog pa spangs pa de nyid ni//bsam gtan 'dzin pa med pa'o//tshor bya tshor byed gnyis las grol//srid dang mya ngan 'das dag pa'o//de ni gsang
151b5(rgyud): chen khro bo nyid//gang du yang ni gsal ma byas//'bad pa/yis ni sba bar bya//smra bar mi bya gang la lar//rnal 'byor rgyun du mor gyur pa//lha'i dbang phyug gsal mi bya//dpal
151b6(rgyud): ye shes thig le las//sngags kyi zlas pa'i rim pa dang//mchod sbyin gyi ni cho ga dang//dkyil 'khor bskyed pa'i rim pa dang//gnas kyi don dang/rtsa ba'i 'khor lo 'byung ba dang//de kho na nyid don bstan pa
151b7(rgyud): zhes bya ba'i le'u ste/bcu gcig pa'o////de nas mchod pa'i cho ga 'di//nges par bstan pa bdag gis bshad//brlag dang longs spyod dbang gis ni//yid 'phrog yang dag mchod pa'o//
151b8(rgyud): zla nyi me'i me tog gi//sku'i rgyal po nyid la mchod//'dod pa 'di ni bral bar du//mar ngo'i tshes kyis dang po dbang//yid la de ru 'dod par 'gyur//nges par a li thigs gcig pas//brlag pa 'di ru
152a1(rgyud): @#//tshes gnyis pa//bdud rtsi 'di ni smin phrag tu//dga' bas de la 'dar bar 'gyur//a li'i thig le gnyis pas so//bde bas khur tshos gnas su ni//tshes gsum pa la longs spyod 'gyur;
152a2(rgyud): de la rnam par smin pas 'gro//a li'i thig le gsum pas so//bdud rtsi kos ka'i gnas su ni//tshes bzhi pa la brlag par 'byung//de las mgu ba las dga' ba//bzhi pa'i ye shes thig les so;;bde
152a3(rgyud): ba yi ni mgrin gnas su//tshes lnga pa la longs spyod 'byung//de la dges pa rgyas par 'gyur//thig le lnga pa'i khyengs pas su//'dod pa'i grang gi gnas su ni//tshes drug pa la brlag par 'byung;;
152a4(rgyud): de la bsam gtan las drag go//thig le drug pas gang bas so//bde ba'i nu ma'i gnas su ni//tshes bdun pa la dbang ni 'byung//de la bdun pas 'byung ba yin//thig (7)pa bdun pas khyengs
152a5(rgyud): pas so//'dod pa'i rtsi ba lobs gnas su ni//tshes brgyad pa la brlag par 'byung //de la chags pas mchog gi dga'//thig le brgyad pa gang bas so//bde ba'i lte ba'i gnas su ni;
152a6(rgyud): tshes dgu pa la longs spyod 'byung //de las rnam smin las 'bar 'gyur//thig le dgu pas khyengs pas so//'dod pa'i 'doms kyi gnas su ni//tshes bcu pa la brlag par 'byung//de las gtan las thig
152a7(rgyud): ler 'gyur;;thig le bcu pa gang ba 'byung//bde ba'i spyi bor gnas su ni//bcu gcig pa la dbang ni 'byung //de la stobs la myos par 'gyur//bcu gcig thig le khyengs pas so//bde ba'i rdo rje brtsa
152a8(rgyud): gnas su;;bcu gnyis tshes la brlag pa 'byung //de la 'od las chad pa dka'//bcu gnyis thig les gang bas so//rgyas pa rdo rje dbus gnas su//bcu gsum tshes la longs spyod 'byung//de las
152b1(rgyud): snang bas u dar 'gyur//a li bcu gsum thig les so//bde ba'i 'dud pa'i gnas su ni//bcu bzhi pa la brlag pa shar//de la reg las tshul du 'gyur//bcu bzhi zla ba'i thig les so//bde ba'i
152b2(rgyud): nor bu'i dbus su ni//nya gang ba las dbang ni shar//de las phrad las gnyid du 'gyur//bcwo lnga pa yi thig les so//dpa' bo bcu drug pa yi char//gzhan ni gang du mi dmigs pa'o//de bzhin du ni so
152b3(rgyud): sor gzugs//rang bzhin de ni yod pa min//dga' ba'i bye brag rang bzhin dga'//bcu drug pa yi don du yod//char yang bcu drug 'di ru ni//yod pa ma yin med pa min//de bzhin du ni ye shes che//
152b4(rgyud): lha'i dbang phyug rang rang bzhin no//g.yas g.yon yan lag gnyis su ni//khu ba yi ni 'byung ba dran//mar ngo'i de bzhin yar ngo yis//'dod pa yis ni 'byung ba'i tshe//bcu dang lnga yis
152b5(rgyud): gnyis med sbyor//bde ba 'di ni 'byung ba yin//de nyid gsuM shes bcu dang ldan//shes rab dang bral bde ba che//bla ma'i zhal snga'i dka' grin gyis//gnyis su med pa'i bde ba shes//
152b6(rgyud): rtag tu goms pa yis ni shar//bsam gtan gyis ni shes pa'o//bcu drug ni yi ge gzugs//a li'i thig le'i rang ngo bo//a la swogs pa 'bru char yang 'byung//a li'i ye shes bde bdag med
152b7(rgyud): ka bswogs yig 'bru'i char yang 'byung//ka li'i ye shes bde ba che//a li ka li gnyis sbyor las//mchod sbyin gyi ni rab mchog bya//mchod pa yang ni de bzhin te//gsang ba'i pad ma'i snying khar ru//
152b8(rgyud): gsang ba de nyid gsum zhes bya//brlag dang longs spyod bswogs bye drag//brlag pa dang ni longs spyod dbang//dga' ba gsum du bstan pa'o//de ni thig le gsum zhes bya//sku gsum yig gsuM bdag
153a1(rgyud): @#//nyid do//tshangs pa'i bu ga de bzhin skyer//snying kha ru ni thig le gsum//de'i bskyed pa'i rim shes bya//mdun pa'i nang ni man chad du//zlog pa yi ni thig le gsum//gyen du rdzogs
153a2(rgyud): pa'i rim pa gsum//rgyu 'bras rnam dag bye brag tu//thig le drug ni rab tu grags//dri med ye shes char yang drug//de ni rnam shes sbyor drug bshad//de'i dbus su de nyid bzhugs//rang bzhin 
153a3(rgyud): dga' ba bde ba che//mchod pa'i las sbyor che ba yis//the tsom med par brnyed par 'gyur//mchod pa'i dam tshig dus su ni//gsang ba'i sgra ni 'bad pa yis//gang zhig 'dod pa grub pa che//dam tshig
153a4(rgyud): sems dpas brjod par bya//la la rdo rje mda' la pad//a li zla ba rab tu grags//ka la nyi ma ri bong zhu//nyi ma khrag tu rab tu grags//khu ba ga bur shes bya ste//khrag ni sihA zhes
153a5(rgyud): su dran;;sbrul ni dbang po gnyis ka yin//'dus pa phung por brjod pa'o//na ra ka pa la zhes brjod//chags pa chung ngu rab tu grags//sha ni sdang yid gti mug ste//'phra dog bskyu

153a7(rgyud): pa'o;;ri rab kyi ni rtse mo bri//gling bzhi pa ni reg bya'o//sa ni ma ru rab tu grags//chu ni srid mo yin par dran//me ni yum dang bu mo gnyis//rlung ni rab tu grags pa'o//namakha' sgyug mor shes par bya;
153a8(rgyud): bcings pa med pa namakha' min//skal ldan ma yin khrung khrung ngo//skal ldan ngang pa 'on pa 'tsho//'on pa med pa rgya mtshor brjod//dbang skur grong khyer ba ru dran//dbang bskur min pa grong pa yin;;gsang ba

154a2(rgyud): 'di thams cad//de ni thams cad sems yin no//sems las thams cad byung ba ste//sems kyis yongasu 'das pa'o//lus ni mgo bo'i gnas na dga'//dag skye ru ni mngon par dga'//sems ni snying

161a1(rgyud): //bcom ldan ('das) (dangs) (dangs) mo phyag rgya chen po pad ma mchog skyed pas/shin du dga' bas yang zhus so//kye bcom ldan 'das dpal ye shes thig le 'di'i tshad ci gnas gang/mchog gi dbang

161a4(rgyud): gshad//srog gi dbyug pa gang zhig ste//sor bcu gnyis ni tshigs pa med//'og gi cha ni bcu gnyis phyed//de bzhin sprul pa'i pad ma ru//drug gi phyed kyis spyan gnyis pad//rtsa ba dang bcas
161a5(rgyud): lde nang du//rtsa ba rtsa ba'i nang 'ongs nas//'dab ma'i rtse ru bcar te gnas//gug pa'i rnam pa'i ngo bor grags//bu kha phra mos nang na ldan//de ni srog pa rtsa ni nyid//khams gsum gyi ni rten yin/
161a6(rgyud): rgyu dang 'bras bu gnyis med kyis//rtsa ba 'dab bu rgya yis bdab//drug phyed rtsa ba dang ldan ni//rtsa ba'i skud pa byang gzugs ldan//bcu drug phyed phyed bye drag du//rtsa ba'i skud pa gcig bu'o//rtsa ba
161a7(rgyud): gsum dbye skal ldan mo//'bras bu yang ni de bzhin no//rtsa ba 'bras bu gnyis kyi gnas//bcu gnyis phyed kyi gcig bu'o//de ni shin du phra mo 'jam//rin chen kha nang man cad do//de tsam ye shes
161a8(rgyud): thig skud do//rnam shes drug gi rten mchog go//chang dang yang dang ni thig le'i tshul//tshul tsam mchog dang gzhan mchog go//dpal ni de gzhan min 'di ru char//thig le nya zhes bya bar dran//
161b1(rgyud): tshul ni na'i yig 'bru yin//ta'i yi ge tshul tsam mo//mchog ni la'i yig 'bru ldan//gzhan mchog ka ru dran pa'o//gug pa'i rnam pa'i kha ru ni//yungs kar che ba'i sbom po tsam//gnyis su
161b2(rgyud): med pa'i ye shes dpal//char yang du grags mchog dbang phyag//de'i snying kha'i gnas su ni//ye shes thig le grub pa bzhugs//tshul mdud pa'i skye ru gnas//na'i yi ge mtshan zhes mchog/g.yon
161b3(rgyud): blo'i ni 'dab ma'i nang//thig le 'di ni tshul tsam gnas//g.yas su la'i yig 'bru grags//lha mo mchog ni bzhugs pa'o//steng du gzhan min gzhan gyi mchog//kha'i gnas su rtse mo 'cha'/

161b5(rgyud): sgyur//nam mkha' 'gro ba yi ni phyir//mkha' 'gro ma ni rtsa kun dran//'jug pa'i gnas pa long spyod yin//brlab pa 'gro ba'i gnas su yod//'byung ba dbang gi gnas su ste//rang bzhin de ni
161b6(rgyud): mchog gzhan mchog//g.yon gi blo ru i ti rtsa//ping ka la'i gnas su gnas//so sho ma na'i steng du gnas//phyag rgya chen po'i rang bzhin no//steng dang 'og gnas//yod pa'i rtsa//srid dang mya ngan
161b7(rgyud): 'da' dbang byed//g.yon blo ru ni yod pas gang//de bzhin 'di ni dran pa'o//de rnams lte ba'i dbus su ni//skra rtse bye ba brgya'i cha//tshad dang de bzhin du ni gnas//zhal bzang ye shes thig leso/
161b8(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma'i ye shes dbang//gsang ba mchog ni bdag gis gshad//thos pa yi ni sdig pa zad//de nas de la dga' ba skye//dran pa yis ni bsod nams brnyed//de nas de la mchog
162a1(rgyud): @#//dga' skye|blta ba yi ni grub par 'gyur//chad pa'i dga' ba rab tu skye//reg pa yis ni rang bzhin dga'//de nas yang dag rab 'od gsal//'od rab gnas las thams cad 'di/
162a2(rgyud): gnas la sogs pa'i dga' dgun no//lha mos gsol pa//gnas la grangs ni ci tsam dbang//mchog gi dbang phyug khyod dbang mdzad//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//rtag tu sprul
162a3(rgyud): pa yi ni sku//sa ni bcu gcig pa ru dran//longs spyod rdzogs skus bcu gnyis pa//chos kyi skus ni bcu gsum pa//bde ba chen pos bcu bzhi pa//gang zhig bco lnga pa yis//de ni ye shes sa

162b3(rgyud): gzugs ni rnam par snang mdzad grags//gti mug rdo rje rang ngo bos//tshor ba dang ni rin chen rgyal//nga rgyal rdo rje rang ngo bo//'du shes de ni snang mtha' yas//chags pa rdo rje rab tu grags//'du
162b4(rgyud): byed don yod grub pa ste//'phra dog rdo rjer bstan pa'o//rnam par shes 'dir mi skyod pa//zhe sdang rdo rje rab tu bsgom//namakha' rdo rje sems dpar grags//rnam pa dang bral grub nga' bdag//gti mug
162b5(rgyud): me long lta ye shes//nga rgyal mnyam pa nyid du dran//so sor rtog pa 'dod chags yin//bya ba grub pa 'phra dog ste//zhe sdang chos dbyings rnam par dag//bde chen gcig pu las ni 'dir;

162b8(rgyud): de ni rin chen 'byung ldan yin//nam mkha' dang bral grub mgon po//rdor sems rgyal ba kun mnga' bdag//spyan rings bzang mo de phyir sku//lha'i rnam pa bde ba'i mchog//'bad pa yis ni de

177b6(rgyud): zhig gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor ni//de ni khu ba 'pho med yin//yang na bde ba chen po'i ngang //bde ba'i rnam pa rtog dang bral//lha mo tshig rgyas gang zhig ni//bya gdong ma de khong du chung //yang na tshigs ni zhu byed

200b6(rgyud): las bsogs pa'i mtsho ba spangs ste//rang gi spyod yul pa'i rigs dang ldan pas//skye bo gzhan la rnyed kyang rung ste/cung zad tsam gis 'gu zhing kha na ma tho ba med pa dang/'phags
200b7(rgyud): pas rjesu snang ba'i 'tsho ba yang dag pa'i 'tsho ba'o//rtsol ba gang gis 'phags pa'i rjesu snang ba 'di ni/chags pa dang /zhe sdang dang/gti mug la sogs pa sten par mi byed cing/'ayang dag
200b8(rgyud): pa'i 'phags pa'i la bden par bzhugs te//lam gi mthar thug par mya ngan las 'das pa'i grod pa rten cing rjesu 'gro ba 'di ni yang dag par rtsol ba'o//gang du nye bar gnas nas sku mi g.yo
200b9(rgyud): zhing 'khyogs po ma yin par 'gyur ba ste//'khor ba'i skyon rnams dang /mya ngan las 'das pa dang/lam gi yon tan rjesu dran shing/lam gi mthar thug pa yang dag par 'byer ba 'di ni/
201a1(rgyud): //yang dag pa'i dran pa'o//de nyid kyi ting nge 'dzin gang la gnas nas/sems can thams cad rnam par thar par mdzad cing/mya ngan las 'das pa'i rtan por mnon pa 'di ni//yang dag
201a2(rgyud): pa'i ting nge 'dzin to//de ltar yang dag gshad de dag//rab tu dga' la sogs pa yis//dbang po la sogs rang bzhin gis//byang chub sems dpa' rnams dang ni//phung po la sogs khyad par kyis/
201a3(rgyud): sangs rgyas rnams ni kun kyi gnas//sangs rgyas byang chub sems rnams kyis//sangs rgyas nyid du 'jug pa'i mchog//byang chub sems ni sum bcu gnyis//gang tshe 'dir ni yongs gang ba//
201a4(rgyud): de tshe dbang po 'byung ba dang//phung po sangs rgyas nyid du 'gyur//rang gi lus la sangs rgyas bzhugs//gzhan du gang na sangs rgyas med//mi shes mun pas sgribs pa rnams//lus la gzhan
201a5(rgyud): du sangs rgyas 'dod//rtog pa thams cad rnam spangs pa//ye shes chen po lus la gnas//dngos po kun la khyad pa ste//lus la gnas kyang lus mi skye//rdo rje snying pos gsol pa//lus
201a6(rgyud): la rtsa ni du zhig skyes//'khor bzhi yis ni dbye ba yis//bcu drug gnyis bcas brgya phrag gcig//byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin gyis//rtsa ni sum bcu rtsa gnyis gzhed//'di lta ste mi phyed ma
